===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\App.jsx =====
// src/App.js
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { BrowserRouter as Router, Routes, Route, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from './context/AuthContext';
import ProtectedRoute from './components/ProtectedRoute';
import MainLayout from './layouts/MainLayout';
import Dashboard from './pages/Dashboard/Dashboard';
import Leads from './pages/Leads/Leads';
import Clients from './pages/Clients/Clients';
import Reports from './pages/Reports/Reports';
import Followups from './pages/Followups/Followups';
import Quotations from './pages/Quotations/Quotations';
import Agreements from './pages/Agreements/Agreements';
import Settings from './pages/Settings/Settings';
import CallPanel from './pages/CallPanel/CallPanel';
import Calendar from './pages/Calendar/Calendar';
import Telecallers from './pages/Telecallers/Telecallers';
import Help from './pages/Help/Help';
import Demo from './pages/Demo/Demo';
import Payment from './pages/Payment/Payment';
import Categories from './pages/Categories/Categories';
import Login from './pages/Login/Login';
import Communication from './pages/Communication/Communication';
import './layouts/MainLayout.css';
import LeavePermissionAdmin from './pages/LeavePermissionAdmin/LeavePermissionAdmin';
import AttendanceAdmin from './pages/AttendanceAdmin/AttendanceAdmin';

// Main App Content with Auth
const AppContent = () => {
  const { isAuthenticated, logout } = useAuth();
  const [activePage, setActivePage] = useState("dashboard");
  const [navigationState, setNavigationState] = useState(null);

  const handleNavigate = (page, options = {}) => {
    if (page === "logout") {
      logout();
      setActivePage("dashboard");
      return;
    }
    if (options.state) {
      setNavigationState(options.state);
    } else {
      setNavigationState(null);
    }
    setActivePage(page);
  };

  const renderPage = () => {
    const pageMap = {
      dashboard:  <Dashboard />,
      leads:      <Leads onNavigate={handleNavigate} />,
      callpanel:  <CallPanel onNavigate={handleNavigate} navigationState={navigationState} />,
      clients:    <Clients />,
      followups:  <Followups />,
      quotations: <Quotations />,
      agreements: <Agreements />,
      reports:    <Reports />,
      calendar:   <Calendar />,
      attendance: <AttendanceAdmin />,    
      leavePermissions: <LeavePermissionAdmin />,
      settings:   <Settings onNavigate={handleNavigate} />,
      'settings-profile': <Settings onNavigate={handleNavigate} activeTab="profile" />,
      'settings-users': <Settings onNavigate={handleNavigate} activeTab="users" />,
      'settings-security': <Settings onNavigate={handleNavigate} activeTab="security" />,
      'settings-notifications': <Settings onNavigate={handleNavigate} activeTab="notifications" />,
      'settings-calls': <Settings onNavigate={handleNavigate} activeTab="calls" />,
      'settings-quotations': <Settings onNavigate={handleNavigate} activeTab="quotations" />,
      'settings-about': <Settings onNavigate={handleNavigate} activeTab="about" />,
      telecallers:      <Telecallers />,
      help:       <Help />,
      demo:       <Demo />,
      payment:    <Payment />,
      categories: <Categories />,
      communication: <Communication />,
    };
    return pageMap[activePage] || <Dashboard />;
  };

  // If not authenticated, show login
  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return <Login onLogin={() => {}} />;
  }

  return (
    <MainLayout activePage={activePage} onNavigate={handleNavigate}>
      {renderPage()}
    </MainLayout>
  );
};

// Root App
const App = () => {
  return (
    <Router>
      <AuthProvider>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="/login" element={<Login onLogin={() => {}} />} />
          <Route path="/*" element={
            <ProtectedRoute>
              <AppContent />
            </ProtectedRoute>
          } />
        </Routes>
      </AuthProvider>
    </Router>
  );
};

export default App;

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\components\ProtectedRoute.jsx =====
// src/components/ProtectedRoute.js
import React from 'react';
import { Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';

const ProtectedRoute = ({ children }) => {
  const { isAuthenticated, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div style={{ 
        display: 'flex', 
        justifyContent: 'center', 
        alignItems: 'center', 
        height: '100vh',
        fontFamily: "'Public Sans', sans-serif",
        color: '#666'
      }}>
        Loading...
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  }

  return children;
};

export default ProtectedRoute;

import React from 'react';
import {
  PieChart as RPieChart,
  Pie,
  Cell,
  Tooltip,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  Legend,
} from 'recharts';

const data = [
  { name: 'New Lead', value: 10, color: '#2563eb' },
  { name: 'In Progress', value: 8, color: '#7c3aed' },
  { name: 'Interested', value: 2, color: '#16a34a' },
  { name: 'Not Interested', value: 6, color: '#dc2626' },
  { name: 'Call Back', value: 0, color: '#d97706' },
  { name: 'Converted', value: 0, color: '#7b1113' },
];

const RADIAN = Math.PI / 180;

const PieChart = () => {
  return (
    <div style={{ width: '100%', height: 200, marginBottom: '24px' }}>   {/* 👈 GAP ADDED */}
      <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
        <RPieChart>
          <Pie
            data={data}
            cx="50%"
            cy="50%"
            innerRadius={50}
            outerRadius={80}
            paddingAngle={2}
            dataKey="value"
          >
            {data.map((entry, index) => (
              <Cell key={`cell-${index}`} fill={entry.color} />
            ))}
          </Pie>
          <Tooltip
            contentStyle={{
              fontFamily: 'Public Sans',
              fontSize: 12,
              borderRadius: 8,
              border: '1px solid #f0f0f0',
            }}
          />
          <Legend
            layout="vertical"
            align="right"
            verticalAlign="middle"
            iconType="circle"
            iconSize={8}
              wrapperStyle={{
                right: -15,

  }}
            formatter={(value, entry) => (
              <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#4a4a6a', fontFamily: 'Public Sans' }}>
                {value} <strong style={{ color: '#1a1a2e' }}>{entry.payload.value}</strong>
              </span>
            )}
          />
        </RPieChart>
      </ResponsiveContainer>
    </div>
  );
};

export default PieChart;

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\components\Invoice\InvoiceEditorModal.jsx =====
import React, { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { FiDownload, FiPlus, FiSave, FiTrash2, FiX } from 'react-icons/fi';

import InvoiceTemplate from './InvoiceTemplate';
import { INVOICE_DEFAULTS, TEXT_KEYS } from './InvoiceDefaults';
import paymentAdminService from '../../services/paymentAdminService';
import invoiceAssetService from '../../services/invoiceAssetService';

import './InvoiceEditorModal.css';


const NUMBER_FORMAT = /^INV-IC\/\d{5,}$/;
const GSTIN_FORMAT = /^\d{2}[A-Z]{5}\d{4}[A-Z][A-Z\d]Z[A-Z\d]$/;
const PHONE_FORMAT = /^[0-9+\-\s]{7,15}$/;
const STATUS_OPTIONS = ['Paid', 'Partial Paid', 'Pending', 'Fully Paid', 'Verified'];

const num = (v) => {
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
};

const toInputDate = (value) => {
  if (!value) return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(value)) return String(value).slice(0, 10);
  const d = new Date(value);
  return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) : d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
};

/** Backend invoice -> editor form (every field editable). */
const formFromInvoice = (inv) => {
  const form = {
    invoiceNumber: inv.invoiceNumber || '',
    invoiceDate: toInputDate(inv.invoiceDate || inv.paymentDate),
    paymentStatus: inv.paymentStatus || 'Paid',
    paymentType: inv.paymentType || '',
    organizationName: inv.organizationName || '',
    address: inv.address || '',
    phone: inv.phone || '',
    customerGstin: inv.customerGstin || '',
    email: inv.email || '',
    discountAmount: inv.discountAmount ?? 0,
    taxRate: inv.taxRate ?? 18,
    amountPaid: inv.amountPaid ?? 0,
  };

  // company / bank / declaration / signature: saved value, else the template default
  TEXT_KEYS.forEach((key) => {
    form[key] = inv[key] !== undefined && inv[key] !== null ? inv[key] : INVOICE_DEFAULTS[key];
  });

  form.items =
    Array.isArray(inv.items) && inv.items.length
      ? inv.items.map((i) => ({
          description: i.description || '',
          quantity: i.quantity ?? 1,
          rate: i.rate ?? 0,
        }))
      : [
          {
            description: inv.description || `${inv.paymentType || 'Service'} charges`,
            quantity: 1,
            rate: inv.totalAmount ?? 0,
          },
        ];

  return form;
};

/** Editor form -> object understood by InvoiceTemplate / printInvoicePdf (numbers cleaned). */
const toInvoiceView = (form) => ({
  ...form,
  discountAmount: num(form.discountAmount),
  taxRate: num(form.taxRate),
  amountPaid: num(form.amountPaid),
  items: form.items.map((i) => ({
    description: i.description,
    quantity: num(i.quantity),
    rate: num(i.rate),
  })),
});

const money = (v) => num(v).toLocaleString('en-IN', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });

export default function InvoiceEditorModal({ payment, onClose, onPrint, onSaved }) {
  const [form, setForm] = useState(null);
  const [assets, setAssets] = useState({ qrDataUrl: '', logoDataUrl: '', signatureDataUrl: '', thankYouDataUrl: '' });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState('');
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [printing, setPrinting] = useState(false);
  const previewRef = useRef(null);
  const [previewScale, setPreviewScale] = useState(0.45);

  const hydrate = (inv) => {
    const f = formFromInvoice(inv);
    setForm(f);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    (async () => {
      try {
        const [invoice, qr, brand] = await Promise.all([
          paymentAdminService.getInvoice(payment.id),
          paymentAdminService.getSharedQr().catch(() => null),
          invoiceAssetService.getAssets().catch(() => ({})),
        ]);
        if (!active) return;
        hydrate(invoice);
        setAssets({
          qrDataUrl: qr?.dataUrl || '',
          logoDataUrl: brand?.logoDataUrl || '',
          signatureDataUrl: brand?.signatureDataUrl || '',
          thankYouDataUrl: brand?.thankYouDataUrl || '',
        });
      } catch (e) {
        if (active) setLoadError(e.message || 'Unable to load the invoice. Generate the invoice first.');
      } finally {
        if (active) setLoading(false);
      }
    })();
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [payment.id]);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const el = previewRef.current;
    if (!el) return undefined;
    const fit = () => {
      const w = Math.max(1, el.clientWidth - 48);
      const h = Math.max(1, el.clientHeight - 48);
      const s = Math.min(w / 1024, h / 1518, 1);
      setPreviewScale(Number.isFinite(s) ? Math.max(0.08, s) : 0.45);
    };
    fit();
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(fit);
    ro.observe(el);
    return () => ro.disconnect();
  }, [loading]);

  const set = (key) => (e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [key]: e.target.value }));

  const setItem = (index, key, value) =>
    setForm((f) => ({
      ...f,
      items: f.items.map((item, i) => (i === index ? { ...item, [key]: value } : item)),
    }));

  // Copy the Bill To name + address into the Shipping fields (both stay editable afterwards).
  const copyBillToShipping = () =>
    setForm((f) => ({ ...f, shippingName: f.organizationName, shippingAddress: f.address }));

  const addItem = () =>
    setForm((f) => ({ ...f, items: [...f.items, { description: '', quantity: 1, rate: 0 }] }));

  const removeItem = (index) =>
    setForm((f) => ({ ...f, items: f.items.length > 1 ? f.items.filter((_, i) => i !== index) : f.items }));

  // what the preview + PDF show
  const view = useMemo(() => {
    if (!form) return {};
    return toInvoiceView(form);
  }, [form]);

  const totals = useMemo(() => {
    if (!form) return { subtotal: 0, taxable: 0, tax: 0, grand: 0, balance: 0 };
    const subtotal = form.items.reduce((s, i) => s + num(i.quantity) * num(i.rate), 0);
    const taxable = Math.max(0, subtotal - num(form.discountAmount));
    const tax = (taxable * num(form.taxRate)) / 100;
    const grand = taxable + tax;
    return { subtotal, taxable, tax, grand, balance: Math.max(0, grand - num(form.amountPaid)) };
  }, [form]);

  const validate = () => {
    if (!NUMBER_FORMAT.test(form.invoiceNumber.trim())) return 'Invoice number format must be INV-IC/00760.';
    if (!form.organizationName.trim()) return 'Client / organization name is required.';
    if (form.phone.trim() && !PHONE_FORMAT.test(form.phone.trim())) return 'Contact number must be 7-15 digits (digits, +, - and spaces only).';
    if (form.customerGstin.trim() && !GSTIN_FORMAT.test(form.customerGstin.trim().toUpperCase())) return 'GSTIN must be a valid 15-character GST number (e.g. 33ABCDE1234F1Z5).';
    if (!form.items.length) return 'Add at least one item.';
    for (const [i, item] of form.items.entries()) {
      if (!String(item.description).trim()) return `Item ${i + 1}: description is required.`;
      if (num(item.quantity) <= 0) return `Item ${i + 1}: quantity must be greater than 0.`;
      if (num(item.rate) < 0) return `Item ${i + 1}: rate cannot be negative.`;
    }
    if (num(form.discountAmount) < 0 || num(form.discountAmount) > totals.subtotal) return 'Discount must be between 0 and the subtotal.';
    if (num(form.taxRate) < 0 || num(form.taxRate) > 100) return 'Tax rate must be between 0 and 100.';
    if (num(form.amountPaid) < 0) return 'Paid amount cannot be negative.';
    if (num(form.amountPaid) > totals.grand + 0.005) return 'Paid amount cannot be more than the invoice total.';
    return '';
  };

  const save = async () => {
    const problem = validate();
    if (problem) { setNotice({ type: 'err', text: problem }); return null; }
    setSaving(true);
    setNotice(null);
    try {
      const payload = { ...view, invoiceNumber: form.invoiceNumber.trim(), customerGstin: form.customerGstin.trim().toUpperCase() };
      const saved = await paymentAdminService.updateInvoice(payment.id, payload);
      hydrate(saved);
      setNotice({ type: 'ok', text: 'Invoice saved.' });
      onSaved?.(saved);
      return saved;
    } catch (e) {
      setNotice({ type: 'err', text: e.message || 'Unable to save the invoice.' });
      return null;
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const download = async () => {
    if (printing) return;
    const saved = await save(); // download exactly what is stored
    if (!saved) return;
    setPrinting(true);
    try {
      await onPrint(toInvoiceView(formFromInvoice(saved)));
    } finally {
      setPrinting(false);
    }
  };

  const text = (key, label, props = {}) => (
    <label>
      <span>{label}</span>
      <input value={form[key] ?? ''} onChange={set(key)} {...props} />
    </label>
  );

  const area = (key, label, rows = 3) => (
    <label>
      <span>{label}</span>
      <textarea rows={rows} value={form[key] ?? ''} onChange={set(key)} />
    </label>
  );

  return (
    <div className="invoice-editor-overlay" onMouseDown={(e) => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose(); }}>
      <div className="invoice-editor-modal">
        <div className="invoice-editor-header">
          <div>
            <h2>Edit Invoice</h2>
            <p>Everything on the invoice can be edited. Changes are saved and used for every PDF / Excel download.</p>
          </div>
          <div className="invoice-editor-actions">
            <button className="iee-save-btn" type="button" onClick={save} disabled={loading || saving || !form}>
              <FiSave size={16} /> {saving ? 'Saving…' : 'Save'}
            </button>
            <button className="invoice-editor-print-btn" type="button" onClick={download} disabled={loading || printing || saving || !form}>
              <FiDownload size={17} /> {printing ? 'Generating PDF…' : 'Save & Download PDF'}
            </button>
            <button className="invoice-editor-close-btn" type="button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">
              <FiX size={22} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {loading && <div className="iee-state">Loading invoice…</div>}
        {!loading && loadError && <div className="iee-state iee-err">{loadError}</div>}

        {!loading && form && (
          <div className="invoice-editor-body">
            <aside className="invoice-editor-form">
              {notice && (
                <div
                  className={`iee-notice ${notice.type}`}
                  style={{ position: 'sticky', top: 0, zIndex: 5 }}
                >
                  {notice.text}
                </div>
              )}
              <h3>Invoice</h3>
              {text('invoiceNumber', 'Invoice Number', { placeholder: 'INV-IC/00760' })}
              <small className="invoice-number-hint">Format: INV-IC/00000 (auto sequence, must be unique)</small>
              <div className="iee-row">
                <label>
                  <span>Invoice Date</span>
                  <input type="date" value={form.invoiceDate} onChange={set('invoiceDate')} />
                </label>
                <label>
                  <span>Status</span>
                  <select value={form.paymentStatus} onChange={set('paymentStatus')}>
                    {[...new Set([form.paymentStatus, ...STATUS_OPTIONS])].map((s) => <option key={s}>{s}</option>)}
                  </select>
                </label>
              </div>
              {text('paymentType', 'Received row label (shows as "<label> Received", e.g. Advance / Full)')}

              <h3>Company (invoice header)</h3>
              {text('companyName', 'Company Name')}
              {area('companyAddress', 'Company Address', 3)}
              {text('companyEmail', 'Company Email')}
              <div className="iee-row">
                {text('companyMobile', 'Mobile')}
                {text('companyWebsite', 'Website')}
              </div>

              <h3>Bill To</h3>
              {text('organizationName', 'Customer Name')}
              {area('address', 'Billing Address', 3)}
              <div className="iee-row">
                {text('customerGstin', 'GSTIN (optional)', {
                  placeholder: '33ABCDE1234F1Z5',
                  maxLength: 15,
                  style: { textTransform: 'uppercase' },
                })}
                {text('phone', 'Contact Number', { placeholder: 'Optional', inputMode: 'tel' })}
              </div>

              <h3>Shipping Address</h3>
              <button type="button" className="iee-add-btn" onClick={copyBillToShipping}>
                Copy billing address to shipping
              </button>
              {text('shippingName', 'Ship To Name')}
              {area('shippingAddress', 'Shipping Address', 3)}
              <small className="invoice-number-hint">Leave both blank to print the Bill To details as the shipping address.</small>

              <h3>Items</h3>
              {form.items.map((item, index) => (
                <div className="iee-item" key={index}>
                  <div className="iee-item-head">
                    <b>Item {index + 1}</b>
                    <button type="button" className="iee-icon-btn" onClick={() => removeItem(index)} disabled={form.items.length === 1} title="Remove item">
                      <FiTrash2 size={14} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <textarea rows={2} placeholder="Description" value={item.description} onChange={(e) => setItem(index, 'description', e.target.value)} />
                  <div className="iee-row3">
                    <label><span>Qty</span><input type="number" min="0" step="any" value={item.quantity} onChange={(e) => setItem(index, 'quantity', e.target.value)} /></label>
                    <label><span>Rate (₹)</span><input type="number" min="0" step="any" value={item.rate} onChange={(e) => setItem(index, 'rate', e.target.value)} /></label>
                    <label><span>Amount</span><input readOnly value={money(num(item.quantity) * num(item.rate))} /></label>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
              <button type="button" className="iee-add-btn" onClick={addItem}><FiPlus size={14} /> Add item</button>

              <h3>Amounts</h3>
              <div className="iee-row3">
                <label><span>Discount (₹)</span><input type="number" min="0" step="any" value={form.discountAmount} onChange={set('discountAmount')} /></label>
                <label><span>Tax rate (%)</span><input type="number" min="0" max="100" step="any" value={form.taxRate} onChange={set('taxRate')} /></label>
                <label><span>Paid (₹)</span><input type="number" min="0" step="any" value={form.amountPaid} onChange={set('amountPaid')} /></label>
              </div>
              <div className="iee-readonly">
                <div><span>Subtotal</span><b>₹{money(totals.subtotal)}</b></div>
                <div><span>Tax</span><b>₹{money(totals.tax)}</b></div>
                <div><span>Total</span><b>₹{money(totals.grand)}</b></div>
                <div><span>Balance due</span><b>₹{money(totals.balance)}</b></div>
                <small>These amounts are for this invoice only. Payment records are not changed.</small>
              </div>

              <h3>Payment Instructions</h3>
              <div className="iee-row">
                {text('bankName', 'Bank Name')}
                {text('bankBranch', 'Branch Name')}
              </div>
              <div className="iee-row">
                {text('bankIfsc', 'IFSC Code')}
                {text('bankAccountNo', 'Account No')}
              </div>

              <h3>Declaration &amp; Signature</h3>
              {area('declaration', 'Declaration text', 3)}
              {text('signatoryFor', '"For ..." line')}
              <div className="iee-row">
                {text('signatoryName', 'Signatory Name')}
                {text('signatoryTitle', 'Designation')}
              </div>

            </aside>

            <main className="invoice-editor-preview" ref={previewRef}>
              <div
                className="invoice-editor-preview-scale"
                style={{
                  '--preview-scale': previewScale,
                  '--preview-width': `${1024 * previewScale}px`,
                  '--preview-height': `${1518 * previewScale}px`,
                }}
              >
                <InvoiceTemplate
                  invoice={view}
                  qrDataUrl={assets.qrDataUrl}
                  logoDataUrl={assets.logoDataUrl}
                  signatureDataUrl={assets.signatureDataUrl}
                  thankYouDataUrl={assets.thankYouDataUrl}
                />
              </div>
            </main>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\components\Invoice\InvoiceTemplate.jsx =====
import React from 'react';
import './InvoiceTemplate.css';
import { INVOICE_DEFAULTS } from './InvoiceDefaults';

const money = (value) =>
  Number(value || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN', {
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  });

const split = (value) =>
  String(value || '')
    .split(/\n|,/)
    .map((item) => item.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);

const formatDate = (value) => {
  if (!value) return '—';

  const parsedDate = new Date(value);

  if (Number.isNaN(parsedDate.getTime())) {
    return String(value);
  }

  return parsedDate
    .toLocaleDateString('en-GB')
    .replace(/\//g, '-');
};

const amountInWords = (value) => {
  const number = Math.round(Number(value || 0));

  if (!number) {
    return 'Zero only';
  }

  const ones = [
    '',
    'One',
    'Two',
    'Three',
    'Four',
    'Five',
    'Six',
    'Seven',
    'Eight',
    'Nine',
    'Ten',
    'Eleven',
    'Twelve',
    'Thirteen',
    'Fourteen',
    'Fifteen',
    'Sixteen',
    'Seventeen',
    'Eighteen',
    'Nineteen',
  ];

  const tens = [
    '',
    '',
    'Twenty',
    'Thirty',
    'Forty',
    'Fifty',
    'Sixty',
    'Seventy',
    'Eighty',
    'Ninety',
  ];

  const convert = (amount) => {
    if (amount < 20) {
      return ones[amount];
    }

    if (amount < 100) {
      return (
        tens[Math.floor(amount / 10)] +
        (amount % 10 ? ` ${ones[amount % 10]}` : '')
      );
    }

    return (
      `${ones[Math.floor(amount / 100)]} Hundred` +
      (amount % 100 ? ` and ${convert(amount % 100)}` : '')
    );
  };

  let remaining = number;
  const parts = [];

  if (remaining >= 10000000) {
    parts.push(
      `${convert(Math.floor(remaining / 10000000))} Crore`
    );
    remaining %= 10000000;
  }

  if (remaining >= 100000) {
    parts.push(
      `${convert(Math.floor(remaining / 100000))} Lakh`
    );
    remaining %= 100000;
  }

  if (remaining >= 1000) {
    parts.push(
      `${convert(Math.floor(remaining / 1000))} Thousand`
    );
    remaining %= 1000;
  }

  if (remaining > 0) {
    parts.push(convert(remaining));
  }

  return `${parts.join(' ')} only`;
};

export default function InvoiceTemplate({
  invoice = {},
  qrDataUrl,
  logoDataUrl,
  signatureDataUrl,
  thankYouDataUrl,
}) {
  const payment = invoice || {};

  // Every text on the invoice is editable; missing values fall back to the defaults.
  const txt = (key) =>
    payment[key] !== undefined && payment[key] !== null
      ? String(payment[key])
      : INVOICE_DEFAULTS[key] || '';

  const lines = (value) =>
    String(value || '')
      .split(/\n/)
      .map((item) => item.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);

  // ----- items -----
  const rawItems =
    Array.isArray(payment.items) && payment.items.length
      ? payment.items
      : [
          {
            description:
              payment.description ||
              `${payment.paymentType || 'Service'} charges`,
            quantity: 1,
            rate: Number(payment.totalAmount || 0),
          },
        ];

  const items = rawItems.map((item) => {
    const quantity = Number(item.quantity ?? 1);
    const rate = Number(item.rate ?? 0);
    return {
      description: item.description || '',
      quantity,
      rate,
      amount: quantity * rate,
    };
  });

  // ----- totals -----
  const subtotal = items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.amount, 0);
  const discount = Number(payment.discountAmount || 0);
  const rate = Number(payment.taxRate ?? 18); // default GST 18% (editable per invoice)
  const taxable = Math.max(0, subtotal - discount);
  const tax = (taxable * rate) / 100;
  const grandTotal = taxable + tax;
  const paid = Number(payment.amountPaid || 0);
  const balance = Math.max(0, grandTotal - paid);

  const billName = payment.organizationName || '';
  const billAddress = split(payment.address);
  const shipName = txt('shippingName').trim() || billName;
  const shipAddress = txt('shippingAddress').trim()
    ? split(txt('shippingAddress'))
    : billAddress;

  const invoiceNumber = payment.invoiceNumber || '—';
  const invoiceDate = payment.invoiceDate || payment.paymentDate;
  const paymentStatus =
    payment.paymentStatus || (balance <= 0 ? 'Paid' : 'Partial Paid');

  const companyAddress = split(txt('companyAddress'));

  return (
    <div className="invoice-print-root">
      <article className="invoice-sheet">
        <header className="invoice-title-bar">INVOICE</header>

        {/* COMPANY HEADER */}
        <section className="invoice-company">
          <div className="invoice-company-copy">
            <h1>{txt('companyName')}</h1>

            {companyAddress.length > 0 && (
              <div className="company-row">
                <span>●</span>
                <div>
                  {companyAddress.map((line, index) => (
                    <React.Fragment key={index}>
                      {line}
                      {index < companyAddress.length - 1 && <br />}
                    </React.Fragment>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {txt('companyEmail') && (
              <div className="company-row">
                <span>✉</span>
                <div>Email Address: {txt('companyEmail')}</div>
              </div>
            )}

            {txt('companyMobile') && (
              <div className="company-row">
                <span>⌕</span>
                <div>Mobile: {txt('companyMobile')}</div>
              </div>
            )}

            {txt('companyWebsite') && (
              <div className="company-row">
                <span>◎</span>
                <div>Website: {txt('companyWebsite')}</div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="invoice-brand">
            {logoDataUrl ? (
              <img src={logoDataUrl} alt="Company logo" />
            ) : (
              <div className="logo-fallback">
                <b>▥</b>
                <span>IC</span>
                <b>♧</b>
                <strong>INDO</strong>
                <em>CAMPAS</em>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </section>

        <div className="invoice-rule" />

        {/* BILLING DETAILS */}
        <section className="invoice-address-grid">
          <div>
            <h3>BILL TO</h3>
            <b>{billName || '—'}</b>
            {billAddress.map((item, index) => (
              <p key={index}>{item}</p>
            ))}
            {payment.phone && <p>Contact: {payment.phone}</p>}
            {payment.customerGstin && <p>GSTIN: {payment.customerGstin}</p>}
          </div>

          <div>
            <h3>SHIPPING ADDRESS</h3>
            <b>{shipName || '—'}</b>
            {shipAddress.map((item, index) => (
              <p key={index}>{item}</p>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="invoice-meta">
            <p>
              <b>Invoice No:</b> {invoiceNumber}
            </p>
            <p>
              <b>Invoice Date:</b> {formatDate(invoiceDate)}
            </p>
            <p>
              <b>STATUS:</b> {paymentStatus}
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* ITEMS */}
        <table className="invoice-items">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Sl.</th>
              <th>Description</th>
              <th>Qty</th>
              <th>Rate</th>
              <th>Amount</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody>
            {items.map((item, index) => (
              <tr key={index}>
                <td>{index + 1}</td>
                <td>{item.description}</td>
                <td>{item.quantity}</td>
                <td>{money(item.rate)}</td>
                <td>{money(item.amount)}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}

            <tr>
              <td>{items.length + 1}</td>
              <td>{payment.paymentType || 'Advance'} Received</td>
              <td>1</td>
              <td>{money(paid)}</td>
              <td>{money(paid)}</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>

        {/* TOTALS */}
        <section className="invoice-lower">
          <div className="invoice-declaration">
            {lines(txt('declaration')).map((line, index, all) => (
              <React.Fragment key={index}>
                {line}
                {index < all.length - 1 && <br />}
              </React.Fragment>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="invoice-totals">
            <div>
              <span>Subtotal</span>
              <b>{money(subtotal)}</b>
            </div>

            <div>
              <span>Discount</span>
              <b>{money(discount)}</b>
            </div>

            <div className="dotted">
              <span>Subtotal Less Discount</span>
              <b>{money(taxable)}</b>
            </div>

            <div>
              <span>Tax Rate(%)</span>
              <b>{money(rate)}</b>
            </div>

            <div>
              <span>Total Tax</span>
              <b>{money(tax)}</b>
            </div>

            <div className="dotted">
              <span>Total</span>
              <b>{money(grandTotal)}</b>
            </div>

            <div className="paid-total">
              <span>PAID</span>
              <b>{money(paid)}</b>
            </div>

            <div
              className="balance-due"
              style={{ background: '#b8cce4', fontWeight: 700, padding: '4px 8px' }}
            >
              <span>Balance Due</span>
              <b>{money(balance)}</b>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* AMOUNT IN WORDS */}
        <div className="invoice-words">
          <b>IN WORDS:</b> {amountInWords(paid)}
        </div>

        <div className="invoice-bottom-rule" />

        {/* FOOTER */}
        <footer className="invoice-footer">
          <div className="payment-instructions">
            <h3>Payment Instructions</h3>

            {txt('bankName') && <p>Bank Name: {txt('bankName')}</p>}
            {txt('bankBranch') && <p>Branch Name: {txt('bankBranch')}</p>}
            {txt('bankIfsc') && <p>IFSC Code: {txt('bankIfsc')}</p>}
            {txt('bankAccountNo') && <p>Account No: {txt('bankAccountNo')}</p>}

            {qrDataUrl && (
              <img className="invoice-qr" src={qrDataUrl} alt="Scan to pay" />
            )}
          </div>

          {thankYouDataUrl ? (
            <div className="invoice-paid-image">
              <img
                className="thank-you-image"
                src={thankYouDataUrl}
                alt="Paid - Thank You"
              />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="paid-stamp">
              <div className="stamp-ring">THANK YOU</div>
              <div className="stamp-bar">PAID</div>
              <div className="stamp-bottom">PAID</div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="invoice-sign">
            <b>{txt('signatoryFor')}</b>

            {signatureDataUrl ? (
              <img
                className="signature-image"
                src={signatureDataUrl}
                alt="Authorized signature"
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="signature-fallback">{txt('signatoryName')}</div>
            )}

            <strong>{txt('signatoryName')}</strong>
            <b>{txt('signatoryTitle')}</b>

            <hr />

            <span>Authorized Signatory</span>
          </div>
        </footer>

        <div className="thank-you">
          <span />
          <em>Thank You!</em>
          <span />
        </div>
      </article>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\components\Invoice\printInvoice.js =====
import React from 'react';
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client';
import html2canvas from 'html2canvas';
import { jsPDF } from 'jspdf';
import InvoiceTemplate from './InvoiceTemplate';

const nextPaint = () =>
  new Promise((resolve) =>
    requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(resolve))
  );

const waitForImages = async (container) => {
  const images = Array.from(container.querySelectorAll('img'));
  await Promise.all(images.map((img) => {
    if (img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) return Promise.resolve();
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const done = () => resolve();
      img.addEventListener('load', done, { once: true });
      img.addEventListener('error', done, { once: true });
    });
  }));
};

/**
 * Renders the exact React invoice DOM and downloads it as a single A4 PDF.
 * The browser print dialog is not used, so clicking Save / Print PDF produces
 * a real .pdf file immediately.
 */
export async function printInvoicePdf(
  invoice,
  { qrDataUrl, logoDataUrl, signatureDataUrl, thankYouDataUrl } = {}
) {
  const mount = document.createElement('div');
  mount.className = 'invoice-pdf-mode';
  mount.style.position = 'fixed';
  mount.style.left = '-20000px';
  mount.style.top = '0';
  mount.style.width = '794px';
  mount.style.background = '#ffffff';
  mount.style.zIndex = '-1';
  document.body.appendChild(mount);

  const root = createRoot(mount);

  try {
    root.render(
      React.createElement(InvoiceTemplate, {
        invoice,
        qrDataUrl,
        logoDataUrl,
        signatureDataUrl,
        thankYouDataUrl,
      })
    );

    await nextPaint();
    await waitForImages(mount);
    await nextPaint();

    const sheet = mount.querySelector('.invoice-sheet');
    if (!sheet) throw new Error('Invoice template could not be rendered.');

    const canvas = await html2canvas(sheet, {
      scale: 2,
      useCORS: true,
      allowTaint: true,
      backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
      logging: false,
      windowWidth: 794,
    });

    const pdf = new jsPDF({
      orientation: 'portrait',
      unit: 'mm',
      format: 'a4',
      compress: true,
    });

    const pageWidth = 210;
    const pageHeight = 297;
    const imageData = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
    const imageRatio = canvas.width / canvas.height;

    let renderWidth = pageWidth;
    let renderHeight = renderWidth / imageRatio;
    if (renderHeight > pageHeight) {
      renderHeight = pageHeight;
      renderWidth = renderHeight * imageRatio;
    }

    const x = (pageWidth - renderWidth) / 2;
    const y = (pageHeight - renderHeight) / 2;
    pdf.addImage(imageData, 'PNG', x, y, renderWidth, renderHeight, undefined, 'FAST');

    const rawNumber =
      invoice?.editedInvoiceNumber ||
      invoice?.invoiceNumber ||
      invoice?.invoiceNo ||
      invoice?.invoiceRecord?.invoiceNumber ||
      'invoice';

    const safeNumber = String(rawNumber).replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/gi, '-');
    pdf.save(`${safeNumber}.pdf`);
  } finally {
    root.unmount();
    mount.remove();
  }
}

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\components\Navbar\Navbar.jsx =====
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  FiBell,
  FiCheck,
  FiCheckCircle,
  FiMenu,
  FiShield,
  FiX
} from 'react-icons/fi';
import './Navbar.css';
import notificationService from '../../services/notificationService';

const Navbar = ({ toggleSidebar }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState({
    fullName: '',
    username: '',
    role: ''
  });

  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState([]);
  const [showNotifications, setShowNotifications] = useState(false);

  const loadUser = useCallback(() => {
    try {
      const raw =
        localStorage.getItem('user') ||
        localStorage.getItem('currentUser') ||
        localStorage.getItem('authUser');

      if (raw) {
        const u = JSON.parse(raw);

        setUser({
          fullName: u.fullName || u.name || u.username || '',
          username: u.username || '',
          role: u.role || ''
        });
      } else {
        setUser({
          fullName:
            localStorage.getItem('fullName') ||
            localStorage.getItem('name') ||
            localStorage.getItem('username') ||
            '',
          username: localStorage.getItem('username') || '',
          role: localStorage.getItem('role') || ''
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Unable to load logged-in user', e);
    }
  }, []);

  const loadNotifications = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const data = await notificationService.getNotifications();
      setNotifications(data);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Failed to load notifications', e);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadUser();
    loadNotifications();

    const interval = setInterval(loadNotifications, 30000);

    const refresh = () => {
      loadUser();
      loadNotifications();
    };

    window.addEventListener('auth-changed', refresh);
    window.addEventListener('storage', refresh);

    return () => {
      clearInterval(interval);
      window.removeEventListener('auth-changed', refresh);
      window.removeEventListener('storage', refresh);
    };
  }, [loadUser, loadNotifications]);

  const initials = (user.fullName || user.username || 'User')
    .trim()
    .split(/\s+/)
    .slice(0, 2)
    .map(x => x[0])
    .join('')
    .toUpperCase();

  const roleLabel = (user.role || '')
    .replace('ROLE_', '')
    .replaceAll('_', ' ')
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\b\w/g, x => x.toUpperCase());

  const unread = notifications.filter(n => !n.read).length;

  const markRead = async (n) => {
    if (n.read) return;

    try {
      await notificationService.markAsRead(n.id);

      setNotifications(prev =>
        prev.map(x =>
          x.id === n.id
            ? { ...x, read: true }
            : x
        )
      );
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    }
  };

  const markAll = async () => {
    try {
      await notificationService.markAllAsRead();

      setNotifications(prev =>
        prev.map(x => ({
          ...x,
          read: true
        }))
      );
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    }
  };

  return (
    <header className="navbar">

      <button
        className="menu-btn"
        onClick={toggleSidebar}
      >
        <FiMenu />
      </button>

      <div className="navbar-right">

        {/* Notification */}
        <div className="notification-wrapper">

          <button
            className="nav-btn notification-btn"
            onClick={() =>
              setShowNotifications(prev => !prev)
            }
            aria-label="Notifications"
          >
            <FiBell />

            {unread > 0 && (
              <span className="notification-badge">
                {unread > 99 ? '99+' : unread}
              </span>
            )}
          </button>

          {showNotifications && (
            <div className="notification-dropdown">

              {/* Header */}
              <div className="notification-header">

                <div className="notification-title">
                  <h3>Notifications</h3>
                  <span>{unread} unread</span>
                </div>

                <div className="notification-actions">

                  {unread > 0 && (
                    <button
                      className="mark-all-btn"
                      onClick={markAll}
                    >
                      <FiCheck />
                      Mark all read
                    </button>
                  )}

                  {/* X / Close */}
                  <button
                    className="notification-close-btn"
                    onClick={() => setShowNotifications(false)}
                    aria-label="Close notifications"
                    title="Close"
                  >
                    <FiX />
                  </button>

                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Notification List */}
              <div className="notification-list">

                {notifications.length === 0 ? (

                  <div className="notification-empty">
                    <FiCheckCircle />
                    <span>No notifications</span>
                  </div>

                ) : (

                  notifications.map(n => (

                    <button
                      key={n.id}
                      className={`notification-item ${
                        n.read ? 'read' : 'unread'
                      }`}
                      onClick={() => markRead(n)}
                    >

                      <div className="notification-icon">
                        <FiBell />
                      </div>

                      <div className="notification-content">

                        <strong>{n.title}</strong>

                        <p>{n.message}</p>

                        <small>
                          {n.createdAt
                            ? new Date(
                                n.createdAt
                              ).toLocaleString()
                            : ''}
                        </small>

                      </div>

                      {!n.read && (
                        <span className="unread-dot" />
                      )}

                    </button>

                  ))

                )}

              </div>
            </div>
          )}

        </div>

      

        {/* User */}
        <div className="user-avatar">

          <div className="avatar-circle">
            {initials}
          </div>

          <div className="user-info">

            <span className="user-name">
              {user.fullName || user.username || 'User'}
            </span>

            <span className="user-role">
              {roleLabel}
            </span>

          </div>

        </div>

      </div>
    </header>
  );
};

export default Navbar;

// src/components/Sidebar/Sidebar.jsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import './Sidebar.css';
import logo from "../../assets/icons/logo.png";

  FiGrid,

      { icon: <FiInfo />, label: "About", id: "settings-about" },
    ]
  },

const Sidebar = ({ activePage, onNavigate, collapsed, userRole }) => {
  const [expandedMenus, setExpandedMenus] = useState({});

  // Determine which menu to show based on role
  const isSuperAdmin = userRole === 'SUPER_ADMIN' || userRole === 'ROLE_SUPER_ADMIN';
  const menuItems = isSuperAdmin ? superAdminMenuItems : adminMenuItems;

  // Find the settings menu item
  const settingsItem = menuItems.find(item => item.isParent);

  // Check if any settings sub-item is active
  const isSettingsSubActive = () => {
    if (!settingsItem) return false;
    return settingsItem.subItems?.some(sub => activePage === sub.id);
  };

  // Auto-expand settings if a sub-item is active
  useEffect(() => {
    if (settingsItem) {
      const isSubActive = settingsItem.subItems?.some(sub => activePage === sub.id);
      
      // If a sub-item is active, expand the settings menu
      if (isSubActive) {
        setExpandedMenus(prev => ({
          ...prev,
          [settingsItem.id]: true
        }));
      } 
      // If we're on the settings parent page, also expand
      else if (activePage === 'settings') {
        setExpandedMenus(prev => ({
          ...prev,
          [settingsItem.id]: true
        }));
      }
      // Otherwise, we don't auto-close - let user control via clicks
    }
  }, [activePage, settingsItem]);

  // Toggle sub-menu expansion
  const toggleSubMenu = (menuId) => {
    if (collapsed) return;
    setExpandedMenus(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [menuId]: !prev[menuId]
    }));
  };

  // Check if a sub-item is active
  const isSubItemActive = (subItemId) => {
    return activePage === subItemId;
  };

  // Check if any sub-item of a menu is active
  const isMenuActive = (menu) => {
    if (activePage === menu.id) return true;
    if (menu.subItems) {
      return menu.subItems.some(sub => activePage === sub.id);
    }
    return false;
  };

  // Check if menu has sub-items
  const hasSubItems = (item) => {
    return item.subItems && item.subItems.length > 0;
  };

  // Handle navigation
  const handleNavigate = (id, e) => {
    if (id === "logout") {
      onNavigate(id);
      return;
    }
    onNavigate(id);
  };

  // Handle parent menu click (toggle or navigate)
  const handleParentClick = (item) => {
    if (hasSubItems(item)) {
      // Toggle the sub-menu
      toggleSubMenu(item.id);
    } else {
      handleNavigate(item.id);
    }
  };

  return (
    <aside className={`sidebar ${collapsed ? "collapsed" : ""}`}>
      <div className="sidebar-logo">
        <img
          src={logo}
          alt="INDOCRM Logo"
          className="logo-image"
        />
        {!collapsed && (
          <span className="logo-text">INDOCRM</span>
        )}
      </div>
      
      <nav className="sidebar-nav">
        {menuItems.map((item) => {
          const isActive = isMenuActive(item);
          const isExpanded = expandedMenus[item.id];
          const hasChildren = hasSubItems(item);

          // When collapsed, only show icons
          if (collapsed) {
            return (
              <button
                key={item.id}
                className={`nav-item ${isActive ? "active" : ""}`}
                onClick={() => handleNavigate(item.id)}
                title={item.label}
              >
                <span className="nav-icon">{item.icon}</span>
              </button>
            );
          }

          return (
            <div key={item.id} className="nav-item-wrapper">
              {/* Parent Menu Item */}
              <button
                className={`nav-item ${isActive ? "active" : ""} ${hasChildren ? "has-submenu" : ""}`}
                onClick={() => handleParentClick(item)}
              >
                <span className="nav-icon">{item.icon}</span>
                <span className="nav-label">{item.label}</span>
                {hasChildren && (
                  <span className="nav-chevron">
                    {isExpanded ? <FiChevronDown /> : <FiChevronRight />}
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>

              {/* Sub Menu Items */}
              {hasChildren && isExpanded && (
                <div className="sub-menu-container">
                  {item.subItems.map((subItem) => {
                    const isSubActive = isSubItemActive(subItem.id);
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={subItem.id}
                        className={`nav-item sub-nav-item ${isSubActive ? "active" : ""}`}
                        onClick={() => handleNavigate(subItem.id)}
                      >
                        <span className="nav-icon sub-icon">{subItem.icon}</span>
                        <span className="nav-label">{subItem.label}</span>
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      <div className="sidebar-footer">
        <button
          className={`nav-item ${activePage === "logout" ? "active" : ""}`}
          onClick={() => handleNavigate("logout")}
        >
          <span className="nav-icon"><FiLogOut /></span>
          {!collapsed && (
            <span className="nav-label">Logout</span>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
};

export default Sidebar;

  'Interested': 'badge-interested',

  'No Answer': 'badge-no-answer',

const RecentCalls = () => {
  const [callData, setCallData] = useState(calls);

  // Handle Call button click
  const handleCall = (call) => {
    // Check if mobile number exists
    if (call.mobile) {
      // For web, you can use window.location.href for tel: protocol
      // Or you can implement a custom call dialog/modal
      const confirmCall = window.confirm(`Call ${call.name} at ${call.mobile}?`);
      if (confirmCall) {
        // For actual calling, use: window.location.href = `tel:${call.mobile}`;
        alert(`Initiating call to ${call.name} (${call.mobile})`);
        // You can also integrate with WebRTC or your call service API here
        console.log(`Calling ${call.name} at ${call.mobile}`);
      }
    } else {
      alert('No phone number available for this contact');
    }
  };

  // Handle View button click
  const handleView = (call) => {
    // Show detailed information in a modal or alert
    const details = `
      Name: ${call.name}
      Mobile: ${call.mobile}
      Status: ${call.status}
      Duration: ${call.duration}
      Agent: ${call.agent}
      Time: ${call.time}
    `;
    alert(details);
    // You can replace this with a modal component
    console.log('Viewing details for:', call);
  };

  // Handle Edit button click
  const handleEdit = (call) => {
    // Open edit modal or prompt for changes
    const newName = prompt('Edit Name:', call.name);
    if (newName && newName !== call.name) {
      const newMobile = prompt('Edit Mobile:', call.mobile);
      if (newMobile && newMobile !== call.mobile) {
        const newStatus = prompt('Edit Status (Interested/Not Interested/Callback/No Answer):', call.status);
        if (newStatus && newStatus !== call.status) {
          // Update the call data
          const updatedCalls = callData.map(item => {
            if (item.name === call.name && item.mobile === call.mobile) {
              return {
                ...item,
                name: newName,
                mobile: newMobile,
                status: newStatus
              };
            }
            return item;
          });
          setCallData(updatedCalls);
          alert('Call details updated successfully!');
        }
      }
    }
  };

  // Handle View All button click
  const handleViewAll = () => {
    // Navigate to calls page or show all calls
    alert('Navigating to all calls page...');
    // You can use react-router-dom: navigate('/calls')
    console.log('View all calls clicked');
  };

  return (
    <div className="recent-calls">
      <table className="calls-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Name</th>
            <th>Mobile</th>
            <th>Status</th>
            <th>Duration</th>
            <th>Agent</th>
            <th>Time</th>
            <th>Action</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {callData.map((call, index) => (
            <tr key={index}>
              <td className="call-name">{call.name}</td>
              <td className="call-mobile">{call.mobile}</td>
              <td><span className={`status-badge ${statusClass[call.status] || ''}`}>{call.status}</span></td>
              <td>{call.duration}</td>
              <td>{call.agent}</td>
              <td>{call.time}</td>
              <td>
                <div className="action-btns">
                  <button 
                    className="action-btn" 
                    title="Call"
                    onClick={() => handleCall(call)}
                  >
                    <PhoneCall size={18} />
                  </button>
                  <button 
                    className="action-btn" 
                    title="View"
                    onClick={() => handleView(call)}
                  >
                    <Eye size={18} />
                  </button>
                  <button 
                    className="action-btn" 
                    title="Edit"
                    onClick={() => handleEdit(call)}
                  >
                    <Pencil size={18} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <div className="view-all">
        <button className="view-all-btn" onClick={handleViewAll}>
          View All
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default RecentCalls;

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\context\AuthContext.jsx =====
// src/context/AuthContext.jsx
import React, { createContext, useState, useContext, useEffect } from 'react';
import { authService } from '../services/leadService';

const AuthContext = createContext(null);

export const AuthProvider = ({ children }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    const savedUser = localStorage.getItem('user');
    
    if (token && savedUser) {
      try {
        const userData = JSON.parse(savedUser);
        authService.validateToken()
          .then(() => {
            setUser(userData);
          })
          .catch(() => {
            localStorage.removeItem('token');
            localStorage.removeItem('user');
            setUser(null);
          })
          .finally(() => {
            setLoading(false);
          });
      } catch (e) {
        localStorage.removeItem('token');
        localStorage.removeItem('user');
        setUser(null);
        setLoading(false);
      }
    } else {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const ADMIN_ROLES = ['SUPER_ADMIN', 'ADMIN'];
  const normRole = (r) => String(r || '').trim().toUpperCase().replace(/[-\s]+/g, '_').replace(/^ROLE_/, '');

  const login = async (username, password) => {
    try {
      const response = await authService.login((username || '').trim(), password);

      // This portal is for Super Admin / Admin only. A telecaller (or plain USER)
      // account logs in fine on the server but every admin API then answers 403,
      // which used to throw the person straight back to the login page.
      if (!ADMIN_ROLES.includes(normRole(response.role))) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: 'This portal is for Super Admin / Admin accounts only. Telecallers must use the Telecaller portal.',
        };
      }

      localStorage.setItem('token', response.token);
      localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify({
        username: response.username,
        fullName: response.fullName,
        role: response.role
      }));
      setUser({
        username: response.username,
        fullName: response.fullName,
        role: response.role
      });
      return { success: true };
    } catch (error) {
      return { success: false, error: error.message };
    }
  };

  const logout = async () => {
    try {
      await authService.logout();
    } catch (e) {
      // Ignore errors during logout
    }
    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('user');
    setUser(null);
  };

  const register = async (userData) => {
    try {
      await authService.register(userData);
      return { success: true };
    } catch (error) {
      return { success: false, error: error.message };
    }
  };

  const value = {
    user,
    loading,
    login,
    logout,
    register,
    isAuthenticated: !!user,
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useAuth = () => {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\layouts\MainLayout.jsx =====
import React, { useState } from "react";
import Sidebar from "../components/Sidebar/Sidebar";
import Navbar from "../components/Navbar/Navbar";
import "./MainLayout.css";

const MainLayout = ({ children, activePage, onNavigate, userRole }) => {
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="layout">
      <Sidebar
        activePage={activePage}
        onNavigate={onNavigate}
        collapsed={collapsed}
        userRole={userRole}
      />

      <div className={`main-content ${collapsed ? "expanded" : ""}`}>
        <Navbar toggleSidebar={() => setCollapsed(!collapsed)} />
        <div className="page-content">
          {/* Pass onNavigate to child components */}
          {React.Children.map(children, child => {
            if (React.isValidElement(child)) {
              return React.cloneElement(child, { onNavigate });
            }
            return child;
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default MainLayout;

  ResponsiveContainer, LineChart, Line, BarChart, Bar, PieChart, Pie, Cell,
  XAxis, YAxis, CartesianGrid, Tooltip, Legend,
} from 'recharts';
import {
  FiUsers, FiUserCheck, FiUserX, FiTarget, FiPlusCircle, FiClock, FiPhoneCall,
  FiFileText, FiFile, FiCreditCard, FiTrendingUp, FiPercent, FiUpload, FiUserPlus,
  FiFilePlus, FiSend, FiArrowRight, FiRefreshCw,

import { dashboardService } from '../../services/dashboardService';

const STATUS_COLORS = {
  New: '#2563eb',
  'In Progress': '#0891b2',
  Interested: '#16a34a',
  'Not Interested': '#dc2626',
  'Call Back': '#d97706',
  Converted: '#15803d',
  'Demo Scheduled': '#7c3aed',
};
const FALLBACK_COLORS = ['#7b1113', '#2563eb', '#0891b2', '#16a34a', '#d97706', '#7c3aed', '#db2777', '#6b7280'];

const currency = (n) => {
  const v = Number(n || 0);
  return '₹' + (v >= 10000000 ? (v / 10000000).toFixed(2) + 'Cr' : v >= 100000 ? (v / 100000).toFixed(1) + 'L' : v.toLocaleString('en-IN'));
};

const MONTH_NAMES = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];

// monthlySales: [{year, month(1-12), sales}] -> chart rows for the selected period
function buildSales(monthlySales = [], period) {
  const map = new Map();
  monthlySales.forEach(({ year, month, sales }) => {
    let key; let label; let order;
    if (period === 'Yearly') { key = `${year}`; label = `${year}`; order = year * 100; }
    else if (period === 'Quarterly') { const q = Math.ceil(month / 3); key = `${year}-Q${q}`; label = `Q${q} ${String(year).slice(2)}`; order = year * 100 + q; }
    else { key = `${year}-${month}`; label = `${MONTH_NAMES[month - 1]} ${String(year).slice(2)}`; order = year * 100 + month; }
    const cur = map.get(key) || { month: label, sales: 0, order };
    cur.sales += Number(sales || 0);
    map.set(key, cur);
  });
  const rows = [...map.values()].sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order);
  return period === 'Monthly' ? rows.slice(-12) : rows;
}

const tooltipStyle = {
  fontFamily: 'Public Sans', fontSize: 12, borderRadius: 8,

};

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\pages\Agreements\AgreementCategories.jsx =====
// src/pages/Agreements/AgreementCategories.jsx
// Super Admin → Agreement Templates.
// Click a category (Admin Dashboard / Digital Marketing / ERP Pro / Pro Plus / Pro Max)
// → its agreement opens in an editor. Edit, Save, or Reset to the original text.
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { FiFileText, FiSave, FiRotateCcw, FiRefreshCw, FiEye, FiEdit2, FiAlertTriangle } from 'react-icons/fi';
import agreementCategoryService from '../../services/agreementCategoryService';
import './AgreementCategories.css';

const FIELD_LABELS = {
  agreement_date: 'Agreement date',
  signature_date: 'Signature date',
  client_name: 'Client name',
  client_address: 'Client address',
  pincode: 'Pincode',
  authorized_person_name: 'Signatory name',
  authorized_person_designation: 'Signatory designation',
  plan_name: 'Plan name',
  student_strength: 'Student strength',
  subscription_fee: 'Subscription fee',
  gst_percent: 'GST %',
  gst_amount: 'GST amount',
  total_payable: 'Total payable',
  yearly_fee: 'Professional fee',
  discount_amount: 'Discount',
  net_payable: 'Net payable',
};

// Fields every agreement should carry so the telecaller's data actually lands in the document.
const CORE_FIELDS = ['client_name', 'client_address', 'pincode', 'agreement_date'];

// Make contentEditable output well-formed XHTML (the PDF engine parses it as strict XML).
const toXhtml = (html) => {
  const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(`<body>${html}</body>`, 'text/html');
  const serializer = new XMLSerializer();
  return Array.from(doc.body.childNodes)
    .map((n) => serializer.serializeToString(n).replace(/\sxmlns="http:\/\/www\.w3\.org\/1999\/xhtml"/g, ''))
    .join('');
};

const fmtDate = (v) => {
  if (!v) return 'Default text';
  const d = new Date(v);
  return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? 'Customised' : `Edited ${d.toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })}`;
};

export default function AgreementCategories() {
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState([]);
  const [activeKey, setActiveKey] = useState(null);
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState(null);      // full category incl. contentHtml
  const [html, setHtml] = useState('');              // live editor content
  const [dirty, setDirty] = useState(false);
  const [loadingList, setLoadingList] = useState(true);
  const [loadingDoc, setLoadingDoc] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [readOnly, setReadOnly] = useState(false);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);

  const showToast = (msg, type = 'ok') => {
    setToast({ msg, type });
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 3200);
  };

  const loadList = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoadingList(true);
    try {
      setCategories(await agreementCategoryService.list());
      setError('');
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e.message || 'Unable to load agreement categories');
    } finally {
      setLoadingList(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => { loadList(); }, [loadList]);

  // Push HTML into the editor only when a document is loaded/reset — not on every keystroke,
  // otherwise the caret jumps to the start while typing.
  const setCanvas = (value) => {
    if (canvasRef.current) canvasRef.current.innerHTML = value || '';
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (current) setCanvas(current.contentHtml);
  }, [current]);

  const openCategory = async (key) => {
    if (key === activeKey) return;
    if (dirty && !window.confirm('You have unsaved changes. Discard them?')) return;
    setActiveKey(key);
    setLoadingDoc(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const doc = await agreementCategoryService.get(key);
      setCurrent(doc);
      setHtml(doc.contentHtml || '');
      setDirty(false);
      setReadOnly(false);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e.message || 'Unable to load agreement');
    } finally {
      setLoadingDoc(false);
    }
  };

  const onInput = () => {
    setHtml(canvasRef.current?.innerHTML || '');
    setDirty(true);
  };

  const format = (cmd, arg) => {
    canvasRef.current?.focus();
    document.execCommand(cmd, false, arg);
    onInput();
  };

  const insertField = (field) => {
    if (readOnly) return;
    canvasRef.current?.focus();
    document.execCommand('insertText', false, `{{${field}}}`);
    onInput();
  };

  const usedFields = useMemo(() => new Set((html.match(/\{\{\s*(\w+)\s*\}\}/g) || []).map((m) => m.replace(/[{}\s]/g, ''))), [html]);
  const missingCore = CORE_FIELDS.filter((f) => !usedFields.has(f));
  const unknownFields = useMemo(
    () => Array.from(usedFields).filter((f) => current && !current.fields.includes(f)),
    [usedFields, current]
  );

  const save = async () => {
    if (!current) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const clean = toXhtml(canvasRef.current?.innerHTML || html);
      const saved = await agreementCategoryService.save(current.key, clean);
      setCurrent(saved);
      setHtml(saved.contentHtml);
      setDirty(false);
      await loadList();
      showToast(`${saved.label} agreement saved`);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e.message || 'Unable to save agreement');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const reset = async () => {
    if (!current) return;
    if (!window.confirm(`Restore the original ${current.label} agreement text? Your edits will be lost.`)) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const restored = await agreementCategoryService.reset(current.key);
      setCurrent(restored);
      setHtml(restored.contentHtml);
      setDirty(false);
      await loadList();
      showToast('Restored to the original agreement');
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e.message || 'Unable to reset agreement');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  // Warn before closing the tab with unsaved edits.
  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = (e) => { if (dirty) { e.preventDefault(); e.returnValue = ''; } };
    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', handler);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('beforeunload', handler);
  }, [dirty]);

  const groups = [
    { title: 'Services', keys: ['ADMIN_DASHBOARD', 'DIGITAL_MARKETING'] },
    { title: 'School ERP', keys: ['ERP_PRO', 'ERP_PRO_PLUS', 'ERP_PRO_MAX'] },
  ];

  return (
    <main className="agt-page">
      {toast && <div className={`agt-toast ${toast.type}`}>{toast.msg}</div>}

      <header className="agt-header">
        <div>
          <h1 className="agt-title">Agreement Templates</h1>
          <p className="agt-sub">
            Pick a category to view and edit its agreement. Changes apply to every new agreement created for that category.
          </p>
        </div>
        <button type="button" className="agt-btn ghost" onClick={loadList} disabled={loadingList}>
          <FiRefreshCw className={loadingList ? 'agt-spin' : ''} /> Refresh
        </button>
      </header>

      {error && <div className="agt-error" role="alert"><FiAlertTriangle /> {error}</div>}

      <div className="agt-layout">
        <aside className="agt-side">
          {groups.map((g) => (
            <section key={g.title}>
              <h2 className="agt-group">{g.title}</h2>
              {g.keys.map((k) => {
                const c = categories.find((x) => x.key === k);
                if (!c) return null;
                return (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    key={k}
                    className={`agt-cat ${activeKey === k ? 'active' : ''}`}
                    onClick={() => openCategory(k)}
                  >
                    <span className="agt-cat-icon"><FiFileText /></span>
                    <span className="agt-cat-text">
                      <strong>{c.label}</strong>
                      <small>{c.description}</small>
                      <em className={c.customized ? 'edited' : ''}>{c.customized ? fmtDate(c.updatedAt) : 'Default text'}</em>
                    </span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </section>
          ))}
          {!loadingList && categories.length === 0 && !error && <p className="agt-empty">No categories found.</p>}
        </aside>

        <section className="agt-main">
          {!activeKey && (
            <div className="agt-placeholder">
              <FiFileText size={34} />
              <p>Select a category on the left to open its agreement.</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {activeKey && (
            <>
              <div className="agt-toolbar">
                <div className="agt-toolbar-title">
                  <strong>{current?.label || '…'}</strong>
                  {dirty && <span className="agt-dirty">Unsaved changes</span>}
                </div>
                <div className="agt-toolbar-actions">
                  <button type="button" className="agt-btn ghost" onClick={() => setReadOnly((v) => !v)} disabled={!current}>
                    {readOnly ? <><FiEdit2 /> Edit</> : <><FiEye /> Preview</>}
                  </button>
                  <button type="button" className="agt-btn ghost" onClick={reset} disabled={busy || !current}>
                    <FiRotateCcw /> Reset to original
                  </button>
                  <button type="button" className="agt-btn primary" onClick={save} disabled={busy || !dirty || !current}>
                    <FiSave /> {busy ? 'Saving…' : 'Save changes'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {!readOnly && (
                <div className="agt-format">
                  <button type="button" onMouseDown={(e) => e.preventDefault()} onClick={() => format('bold')}><b>B</b></button>
                  <button type="button" onMouseDown={(e) => e.preventDefault()} onClick={() => format('italic')}><i>I</i></button>
                  <button type="button" onMouseDown={(e) => e.preventDefault()} onClick={() => format('underline')}><u>U</u></button>
                  <button type="button" onMouseDown={(e) => e.preventDefault()} onClick={() => format('insertUnorderedList')}>• List</button>
                  <button type="button" onMouseDown={(e) => e.preventDefault()} onClick={() => format('formatBlock', 'H3')}>Heading</button>
                  <button type="button" onMouseDown={(e) => e.preventDefault()} onClick={() => format('formatBlock', 'P')}>Paragraph</button>
                  <button type="button" onMouseDown={(e) => e.preventDefault()} onClick={() => format('undo')}>Undo</button>
                </div>
              )}

              {current && (
                <div className="agt-fields">
                  <span className="agt-fields-label">Auto-filled fields — click to insert at the cursor:</span>
                  {current.fields.map((f) => (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      key={f}
                      className={`agt-chip ${usedFields.has(f) ? 'used' : ''}`}
                      onMouseDown={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
                      onClick={() => insertField(f)}
                      disabled={readOnly}
                      title={`{{${f}}}`}
                    >
                      {FIELD_LABELS[f] || f}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}

              {(missingCore.length > 0 || unknownFields.length > 0) && current && (
                <div className="agt-warn">
                  <FiAlertTriangle />
                  <div>
                    {missingCore.length > 0 && <div>Missing fields: {missingCore.map((f) => FIELD_LABELS[f]).join(', ')} — the telecaller’s details won’t appear in the agreement.</div>}
                    {unknownFields.length > 0 && <div>Unknown fields will print blank: {unknownFields.map((f) => `{{${f}}}`).join(', ')}</div>}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="agt-paper-wrap">
                {loadingDoc && <div className="agt-loading">Loading agreement…</div>}
                <div
                  ref={canvasRef}
                  className={`agt-paper ${readOnly ? 'readonly' : ''}`}
                  contentEditable={!readOnly && !loadingDoc}
                  suppressContentEditableWarning
                  spellCheck
                  onInput={onInput}
                />
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\pages\Agreements\AgreementDocumentEditor.jsx =====
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

export default function AgreementDocumentEditor({ value, onChange, readOnly = false }) {
  const ref = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (ref.current && ref.current.innerHTML !== (value || '')) {
      ref.current.innerHTML = value || '';
    }
  }, [value]);

  const command = (name, arg) => {
    if (readOnly) return;
    ref.current?.focus();
    document.execCommand(name, false, arg);
    onChange?.(ref.current?.innerHTML || '');
  };

  return (
    <div className="agreement-document-editor">
      {!readOnly && (
        <div className="agreement-editor-toolbar">
          <button type="button" onClick={() => command('bold')} aria-label="Bold"><b>B</b></button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => command('italic')} aria-label="Italic"><i>I</i></button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => command('underline')} aria-label="Underline"><u>U</u></button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => command('insertUnorderedList')} aria-label="Bullet list">• List</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => command('formatBlock', 'H2')} aria-label="Heading">H2</button>
        </div>
      )}
      <div
        ref={ref}
        className="agreement-editor-canvas"
        contentEditable={!readOnly}
        suppressContentEditableWarning
        onInput={(event) => onChange?.(event.currentTarget.innerHTML)}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\pages\Agreements\AgreementDocumentViewer.jsx =====
// src/pages/Agreements/AgreementDocumentViewer.jsx
// READ-ONLY version of the Telecaller's agreement editor. It renders the saved
// `mergeValues.doc` JSON with the same letterhead, headings, bullets, tables and
// signature boxes the Telecaller sees - so Super Admin verifies the exact same page.
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { FiPhone, FiGlobe, FiMapPin } from 'react-icons/fi';
import { PAGE_W, PAD_TOP, PAD_SIDE, pageBackground, usePageFlow, PageOverlays } from './pageFlow';
import quotationAdminService from '../../services/quotationAdminService';
import './AgreementDocumentViewer.css';

// mergeValues.doc is stored as a JSON string by the Telecaller form.
export const parseAgreementDoc = (mergeValues) => {
  try {
    const raw = mergeValues && mergeValues.doc;
    if (!raw) return null;
    const d = typeof raw === 'string' ? JSON.parse(raw) : raw;
    return d && Array.isArray(d.blocks) ? d : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
};

const fmtDate = (v) => {
  if (!v) return '';
  const m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/.exec(String(v));
  return m ? `${m[3]}-${m[2]}-${m[1]}` : String(v);
};

const Text = ({ children, className = '' }) => (
  <div className={`adv-text ${className}`}>{children}</div>
);

const SignBox = ({ title, sign = {} }) => (
  <div className="adv-sign-box">
    <div className="adv-b">{title}</div>
    <div>Signature: ______________</div>
    <div>Name: {sign.name || '______________'}</div>
    <div>Designation: {sign.designation || '______________'}</div>
    <div>Date: {sign.date || '______________'}</div>
  </div>
);

export default function AgreementDocumentViewer({ doc, fallbackHtml = '' }) {
  const [brand, setBrand] = useState(null);
  const bodyRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    quotationAdminService
      .getBranding()
      .then((b) => { if (alive) setBrand(b); })
      .catch(() => {});
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, []);

  const pad = brand?.letterpadDataUrl || '';
  const logo = brand?.hasLogo ? brand.logoDataUrl : '';
  const footerPhone = brand?.phone || '8925811993 / 8925811994';
  const footerWeb = brand?.website || 'www.indocampas.com';
  const footerAddress = (brand?.address || 'TIDEL NEO - Thiruchitrambalam\nKoot Road, Tamil Nadu 605111').split('\n');

  const paged = !!(pad || logo);
  const pages = usePageFlow(bodyRef, paged && !!doc);

  // Old agreements saved before the JSON doc existed: show the saved HTML snapshot.
  if (!doc) {
    return (
      <div className="adv-wrap">
        <div className="adv-page">
          <div className="adv-body" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: fallbackHtml || '<p>No document content.</p>' }} />
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const address = doc.clientAddress || '__________';
  const clientTail = String(doc.clientTail || '').replace(/\{\{address\}\}/g, address);

  const renderBlock = (b, i) => {
    if (b.t === 'h1') return <Text key={i} className="adv-h1">{b.text}</Text>;
    if (b.t === 'h2') return <Text key={i} className="adv-h2">{b.text}</Text>;
    if (b.t === 'p') return <Text key={i} className="adv-p">{b.text}</Text>;
    if (b.t === 'ul') {
      return (
        <React.Fragment key={i}>
