          {(b.items || []).map((it, k) => <ul key={k} className="adv-ul adv-ul-item"><li>{it}</li></ul>)}
        </React.Fragment>
      );
    }
    if (b.t === 'table') {
      const rows = b.rows || [];
      return (
        <table key={i} className="adv-table">
          <tbody>
            {rows.map((r, ri) => (
              <tr key={ri}>
                {r.map((c, ci) => (
                  <td key={ci} className={(b.head && ri === 0) || ri === rows.length - 1 ? 'adv-b' : ''}>{c}</td>
                ))}
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      );
    }
    return null;
  };

  const pageStyle = paged
    ? { width: PAGE_W, height: pages * 1123, padding: 0, minHeight: 0, ...pageBackground(pad) }
    : undefined;

  return (
    <div className="adv-wrap">
      <div className={`adv-page ${paged ? 'adv-paged' : ''}`} style={pageStyle}>
        {paged ? (
          <PageOverlays pages={pages} logo={logo} footerPhone={footerPhone} footerWeb={footerWeb} footerAddress={footerAddress} />
        ) : (
          <div className="adv-head">
            <div className="adv-indo">INDO</div>
            <div className="adv-brand">
              <div className="adv-brand1">CAMPAS TECH</div>
              <div className="adv-brand2">Private Limited</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="adv-body" ref={bodyRef} style={paged ? { padding: `${PAD_TOP}px ${PAD_SIDE}px 0` } : undefined}>
          {doc.intro && <Text className="adv-center">{doc.intro}</Text>}
          <div className="adv-p"><b>Agreement Date:</b> {fmtDate(doc.date)}</div>
          <Text className="adv-b">{doc.providerText}</Text>
          <div className="adv-center adv-b"><u>AND</u></div>
          <Text className="adv-b adv-org">{String(doc.clientName || '').toUpperCase()}</Text>
          <Text className="adv-p">{clientTail}</Text>
          <div className="adv-p"><b>Address:</b> {doc.clientAddress || '—'}</div>

          {doc.blocks.map(renderBlock)}

          <Text className="adv-h1">SIGNATURES</Text>
          <Text className="adv-p">IN WITNESS WHEREOF, the parties have executed this Agreement on the date first written above.</Text>
          <div className="adv-signs">
            <SignBox title="For INDOCAMPAS TECH PRIVATE LIMITED" sign={doc.providerSign} />
            <SignBox title={`For ${(doc.clientName || 'THE INSTITUTION').toUpperCase()}`} sign={doc.clientSign} />
          </div>
        </div>

        {!paged && (
          <div className="adv-foot">
            <div><span className="adv-ico"><FiPhone size={12} /></span> {footerPhone}</div>
            <div><span className="adv-ico adv-ico-dark"><FiGlobe size={12} /></span> {footerWeb}</div>
            <div>
              <span className="adv-ico"><FiMapPin size={12} /></span>{' '}
              {footerAddress.map((line, i) => (
                <React.Fragment key={i}>{i > 0 && <br />}{line}</React.Fragment>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\pages\Agreements\Agreements.jsx =====
// src/pages/Agreements/Agreements.jsx
// Super Admin Agreement verification.
// Mirrors the Quotation approval page workflow without changing the existing
// Telecaller agreement template or Telecaller data flow.

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import './Agreements.css';
import agreementAdminService from '../../services/agreementAdminService';
import AgreementDocumentViewer, { parseAgreementDoc } from './AgreementDocumentViewer';
import {
  FiAlertCircle,
  FiCheck,
  FiCheckCircle,
  FiChevronLeft,
  FiChevronRight,
  FiClock,
  FiDownload,
  FiEye,
  FiFileText,
  FiRefreshCw,
  FiSearch,
  FiShield,
  FiUser,
  FiX,
  FiXCircle,
} from 'react-icons/fi';

const ROWS_OPTIONS = [10, 20, 50];

const STATUS_CONFIG = {
  DRAFT: { cls: 'agr-status-draft', label: 'Draft' },
  PENDING_APPROVAL: { cls: 'agr-status-pending', label: 'Pending Approval' },
  RESUBMITTED: { cls: 'agr-status-pending', label: 'Pending Approval' },
  APPROVED: { cls: 'agr-status-approved', label: 'Approved' },
  REJECTED: { cls: 'agr-status-rejected', label: 'Rejected' },
  SENT: { cls: 'agr-status-sent', label: 'Sent' },
};

const statusConfig = (status) =>
  STATUS_CONFIG[status] || {
    cls: 'agr-status-draft',
    label: String(status || '—').replaceAll('_', ' '),
  };

const initials = (name) =>
  name?.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 2).map((w) => w[0]?.toUpperCase()).join('') || '?';

const fmtDateTime = (iso) => {
  if (!iso) return '—';
  const date = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return '—';
  return date.toLocaleString('en-IN', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
  });
};

const money = (value) => {
  const number = Number(value || 0);
  return `₹${number.toLocaleString('en-IN', {
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  })}`;
};

const Toast = ({ msg, type, onClose }) => (
  <div className={`agr-toast agr-toast-${type}`} onClick={onClose} role="status">
    {type === 'success' ? <FiCheck /> : <FiAlertCircle />}
    <span>{msg}</span>
  </div>
);

const StatusPill = ({ status }) => {
  const cfg = statusConfig(status);
  return (
    <span className={`agr-status-pill ${cfg.cls}`}>
      <span className="agr-status-dot" />
      {cfg.label}
    </span>
  );
};

const ApproveModal = ({ agreement, onClose, onConfirm }) => {
  const [remark, setRemark] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await onConfirm(agreement.id, remark.trim());
      onClose();
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="agr-modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="agr-modal-box agr-modal-box-sm" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="agr-modal-header">
          <div>
            <strong>Approve Agreement</strong>
            <span>{agreement.id}</span>
          </div>
          <button className="agr-modal-close" type="button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">
            <FiX />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="agr-modal-body">
          <div className="agr-form">
            <div className="agr-form-group">
              <label htmlFor="agreement-approval-remark">Remark (optional)</label>
              <textarea
                id="agreement-approval-remark"
                placeholder="Add a note for the Telecaller…"
                value={remark}
                onChange={(e) => setRemark(e.target.value)}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="agr-form-actions">
              <button className="agr-btn-outline" type="button" onClick={onClose} disabled={busy}>
                Cancel
              </button>
              <button className="agr-btn-primary" type="button" onClick={submit} disabled={busy}>
                <FiCheck size={14} /> {busy ? 'Approving…' : 'Approve Agreement'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

const RejectModal = ({ agreement, onClose, onConfirm }) => {
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async () => {
    if (!reason.trim()) {
      setError('A rejection reason is required.');
      return;
    }

    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await onConfirm(agreement.id, reason.trim());
      onClose();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e.message || 'Unable to reject agreement.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="agr-modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="agr-modal-box agr-modal-box-sm" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="agr-modal-header">
          <div>
            <strong>Reject Agreement</strong>
            <span>{agreement.id}</span>
          </div>
          <button className="agr-modal-close" type="button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">
            <FiX />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="agr-modal-body">
          <div className="agr-form">
            {error && (
              <div className="agr-inline-error">
                <FiAlertCircle size={14} />
                <span>{error}</span>
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="agr-form-group">
              <label htmlFor="agreement-rejection-reason">Rejection reason *</label>
              <textarea
                id="agreement-rejection-reason"
                placeholder="Explain why this agreement is being rejected…"
                value={reason}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setReason(e.target.value);
                  if (error) setError('');
                }}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="agr-form-actions">
              <button className="agr-btn-outline" type="button" onClick={onClose} disabled={busy}>
                Cancel
              </button>
              <button className="agr-btn-danger" type="button" onClick={submit} disabled={busy}>
                <FiXCircle size={14} /> {busy ? 'Rejecting…' : 'Reject Agreement'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

const ViewModal = ({ agreement, onClose, onDownload, onApprove, onReject }) => {
  const cfg = statusConfig(agreement.status);
  const agreementDoc = parseAgreementDoc(agreement.mergeValues);
  const history = Array.isArray(agreement.history) ? agreement.history : [];

  return (
    <div className="agr-modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="agr-modal-box agr-modal-box-wide" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="agr-modal-header">
          <div>
            <strong>{agreement.title || 'Agreement'}</strong>
            <span className={`agr-status-pill ${cfg.cls}`}>{cfg.label}</span>
          </div>
          <button className="agr-modal-close" type="button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">
            <FiX />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="agr-modal-body">
          <div className="agr-view-hero">
            <div className="agr-view-avatar">{initials(agreement.clientName)}</div>
            <div className="agr-view-hero-info">
              <div className="agr-view-client-name">{agreement.clientName || '—'}</div>
              <div className="agr-view-secondary">
                {agreement.organization || agreement.email || 'Client details unavailable'}
              </div>
            </div>
            <StatusPill status={agreement.status} />
          </div>

          <div className="agr-view-section">
            <div className="agr-view-section-title">
              <FiFileText /> Agreement Details
            </div>
            <div className="agr-view-grid">
              <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Prepared By</span><strong>{agreement.createdByName || agreement.createdBy || '—'}</strong></div>
              <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Agreement Type</span><strong>{agreement.agreementType || agreement.serviceType || '—'}</strong></div>
              <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Plan</span><strong>{agreement.planName || '—'}</strong></div>
              <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Agreement Date</span><strong>{agreement.agreementDate || '—'}</strong></div>
              <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Email</span><strong>{agreement.email || '—'}</strong></div>
              <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Phone</span><strong>{agreement.phone || '—'}</strong></div>
              <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Address</span><strong>{agreement.address || '—'}</strong></div>
              <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Pincode</span><strong>{agreement.pincode || '—'}</strong></div>
              <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Authorized Person</span><strong>{agreement.authorizedPersonName || '—'}</strong></div>
              <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Designation</span><strong>{agreement.authorizedPersonDesignation || '—'}</strong></div>
              {agreement.studentStrength != null && (
                <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Student Strength</span><strong>{agreement.studentStrength}</strong></div>
              )}
              <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Fee</span><strong>{money(agreement.feeAmount)}</strong></div>
              {Number(agreement.discountAmount || 0) > 0 && (
                <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Discount</span><strong>{money(agreement.discountAmount)}</strong></div>
              )}
              {Number(agreement.gstPercent || 0) > 0 && (
                <div className="agr-view-row"><span>GST</span><strong>{agreement.gstPercent}% · {money(agreement.gstAmount)}</strong></div>
              )}
              <div className="agr-view-row">
                <span>Payable</span>
                <strong>{money(agreement.totalPayable || agreement.netPayable)}</strong>
              </div>
              <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Submitted</span><strong>{fmtDateTime(agreement.submittedAt)}</strong></div>
              {agreement.approvedAt && (
                <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Approved</span><strong>{agreement.approvedBy || 'Super Admin'} · {fmtDateTime(agreement.approvedAt)}</strong></div>
              )}
              {agreement.rejectedAt && (
                <div className="agr-view-row"><span>Rejected</span><strong>{agreement.rejectedBy || 'Super Admin'} · {fmtDateTime(agreement.rejectedAt)}</strong></div>
              )}
              {agreement.approvalRemark && (
                <div className="agr-view-row agr-view-row-wide"><span>Approval Remark</span><strong>{agreement.approvalRemark}</strong></div>
              )}
              {agreement.rejectionReason && (
                <div className="agr-view-row agr-view-row-wide"><span>Rejection Reason</span><strong>{agreement.rejectionReason}</strong></div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="agr-view-section">
            <div className="agr-view-section-title">
              <FiShield /> Submitted Agreement
            </div>
            <div className="agr-readonly-notice">
              <FiShield />
              <span>Read-only view of the agreement exactly as the Telecaller prepared it.</span>
            </div>
            <AgreementDocumentViewer doc={agreementDoc} fallbackHtml={agreement.contentHtml || ''} />
          </div>

          {history.length > 0 && (
            <div className="agr-view-section">
              <div className="agr-view-section-title">History</div>
              <div className="agr-history-list">
                {history.map((entry, index) => (
                  <div className="agr-history-item" key={`${entry.at || 'history'}-${index}`}>
                    <div className="agr-history-dot" />
                    <div>
                      <strong>{entry.action || 'Updated'}</strong>
                      <span>{entry.actor || 'System'} · {fmtDateTime(entry.at)}</span>
                      {entry.remarks && <p>{entry.remarks}</p>}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="agr-form-actions agr-modal-footer-actions">
            {agreement.generatedPdfPath && (
              <button className="agr-btn-outline" type="button" onClick={() => onDownload(agreement)}>
                <FiDownload size={14} /> Download PDF
              </button>
            )}
            <span className="agr-footer-spacer" />
            {['PENDING_APPROVAL', 'RESUBMITTED'].includes(agreement.status) && (
              <>
                <button className="agr-btn-outline agr-reject-outline" type="button" onClick={() => onReject(agreement)}>
                  <FiX size={14} /> Reject
                </button>
                <button className="agr-btn-primary" type="button" onClick={() => onApprove(agreement)}>
                  <FiCheck size={14} /> Approve Agreement
                </button>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default function Agreements() {
  const [agreements, setAgreements] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState('Pending Approval');
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [rowsPerPage, setRowsPerPage] = useState(10);
  const [viewAgreement, setViewAgreement] = useState(null);
  const [approveAgreement, setApproveAgreement] = useState(null);
  const [rejectAgreement, setRejectAgreement] = useState(null);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState(null);

  const showToast = useCallback((msg, type = 'success') => {
    setToast({ msg, type });
    window.setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 3200);
  }, []);

  const loadAll = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const list = await agreementAdminService.getAll();
      setAgreements(Array.isArray(list) ? list : []);
    } catch (e) {
      showToast(e.message || 'Failed to load agreements.', 'error');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [showToast]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadAll();
  }, [loadAll]);

  const upsert = useCallback((saved) => {
    setAgreements((current) => current.map((item) => item.id === saved.id ? saved : item));
    setViewAgreement((current) => current?.id === saved.id ? saved : current);
  }, []);

  const stats = useMemo(() => ({
    total: agreements.length,
    pending: agreements.filter((a) => ['PENDING_APPROVAL', 'RESUBMITTED'].includes(a.status)).length,
    approved: agreements.filter((a) => a.status === 'APPROVED').length,
    sent: agreements.filter((a) => a.status === 'SENT').length,
  }), [agreements]);

  const tabs = [
    { id: 'All', label: 'All', count: agreements.length },
    { id: 'Pending Approval', label: 'Pending Approval', count: stats.pending },
    { id: 'Approved', label: 'Approved', count: stats.approved },
    { id: 'Rejected', label: 'Rejected', count: agreements.filter((a) => a.status === 'REJECTED').length },
    { id: 'Sent', label: 'Sent', count: stats.sent },
    { id: 'Draft', label: 'Draft', count: agreements.filter((a) => a.status === 'DRAFT').length },
  ];

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    let data = [...agreements];

    if (filterStatus !== 'All') {
      data = data.filter((agreement) => {
        if (filterStatus === 'Pending Approval') {
          return ['PENDING_APPROVAL', 'RESUBMITTED'].includes(agreement.status);
        }
        return statusConfig(agreement.status).label === filterStatus || agreement.status === filterStatus.toUpperCase();
      });
    }

    const needle = search.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (needle) {
      data = data.filter((agreement) =>
        [
          agreement.id,
          agreement.clientName,
          agreement.organization,
          agreement.title,
          agreement.agreementType,
          agreement.serviceType,
          agreement.planName,
          agreement.createdByName,
          agreement.createdBy,
          agreement.email,
          agreement.phone,
        ].some((value) => String(value || '').toLowerCase().includes(needle))
      );
    }

    return data;
  }, [agreements, filterStatus, search]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const maxPage = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / rowsPerPage));
    if (page > maxPage) setPage(maxPage);
  }, [filtered.length, page, rowsPerPage]);

  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / rowsPerPage));
  const paginated = filtered.slice((page - 1) * rowsPerPage, page * rowsPerPage);

  const pageNums = () => {
    const result = [];
    for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages; i += 1) {
      if (i === 1 || i === totalPages || Math.abs(i - page) <= 1) result.push(i);
      else if (result[result.length - 1] !== '...') result.push('...');
    }
    return result;
  };

  const handleApprove = async (id, remark) => {
    try {
      const saved = await agreementAdminService.approve(id, remark);
      upsert(saved);
      showToast(`${id} approved. The Telecaller can now send it to the client.`);
    } catch (e) {
      showToast(e.message || 'Unable to approve agreement.', 'error');
      throw e;
    }
  };

  const handleReject = async (id, reason) => {
    try {
      const saved = await agreementAdminService.reject(id, reason);
      upsert(saved);
      showToast(`${id} rejected.`, 'error');
    } catch (e) {
      showToast(e.message || 'Unable to reject agreement.', 'error');
      throw e;
    }
  };

  const handleDownload = async (agreement) => {
    try {
      const blob = await agreementAdminService.downloadPdfBlob(agreement.id);
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const anchor = document.createElement('a');
      anchor.href = url;
      anchor.download = agreement.generatedPdfFileName || `Agreement_${agreement.id}.pdf`;
      document.body.appendChild(anchor);
      anchor.click();
      anchor.remove();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    } catch (e) {
      showToast(e.message || 'Unable to download PDF.', 'error');
    }
  };

  const openApprove = (agreement) => {
    setViewAgreement(null);
    setApproveAgreement(agreement);
  };

  const openReject = (agreement) => {
    setViewAgreement(null);
    setRejectAgreement(agreement);
  };

  return (
    <main className="agreements-page">
      {toast && <Toast msg={toast.msg} type={toast.type} onClose={() => setToast(null)} />}

      <div className="agr-header">
        <div className="agr-title-row">
          <span className="agr-title-icon"><FiShield /></span>
          <div>
            <h1 className="agr-page-title">Agreements — Approvals</h1>
            <p className="agr-page-subtitle">
              Review agreements submitted by Telecallers. The original agreement content and selected details remain read-only here.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button className="agr-btn-outline" type="button" onClick={loadAll} disabled={loading}>
          <FiRefreshCw className={loading ? 'agr-spin' : ''} /> Refresh
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="agr-summary-grid">
        <div className="agr-summary-card">
          <span className="agr-summary-icon agr-summary-icon-total"><FiFileText /></span>
          <div><strong>{stats.total}</strong><span>Total Agreements</span></div>
        </div>
        <div className="agr-summary-card">
          <span className="agr-summary-icon agr-summary-icon-pending"><FiClock /></span>
          <div><strong>{stats.pending}</strong><span>Awaiting Confirmation</span></div>
        </div>
        <div className="agr-summary-card">
          <span className="agr-summary-icon agr-summary-icon-reviewed"><FiCheckCircle /></span>
          <div><strong>{stats.approved}</strong><span>Approved, Not Yet Sent</span></div>
        </div>
        <div className="agr-summary-card">
          <span className="agr-summary-icon agr-summary-icon-sent"><FiCheck /></span>
          <div><strong>{stats.sent}</strong><span>Sent to Clients</span></div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="agr-table-card">
        <div className="agr-tabs-row">
          {tabs.map((tab) => (
            <button
              key={tab.id}
              type="button"
              className={`agr-tab ${filterStatus === tab.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => {
                setFilterStatus(tab.id);
                setPage(1);
              }}
            >
              {tab.label} <span>{tab.count}</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="agr-filter-bar">
          <div className="agr-search-wrap">
            <FiSearch className="agr-search-icon" />
            <input
              className="agr-search-input"
              placeholder="Search by client, plan, Telecaller, type or ID…"
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => {
                setSearch(e.target.value);
                setPage(1);
              }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="agr-table-wrap">
          {loading ? (
            <div className="agr-loading-state">
              <FiRefreshCw className="agr-spin" />
              <span>Loading agreements…</span>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <table className="agr-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Agreement</th>
                  <th>Client</th>
                  <th>Telecaller</th>
                  <th>Type / Plan</th>
                  <th>Amount</th>
                  <th>Status</th>
                  <th>Submitted</th>
                  <th>Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {paginated.length === 0 && (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={8} className="agr-empty">
                      <FiFileText />
                      <strong>No agreements found.</strong>
                      <span>Submitted agreements will appear here automatically.</span>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )}

                {paginated.map((agreement) => (
                  <tr key={agreement.id}>
                    <td>
                      <div className="agr-id-cell">
                        <strong>{agreement.id || '—'}</strong>
                        <span>{agreement.title || 'Agreement'}</span>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td>
                      <div className="agr-client-cell">
                        <div className="agr-client-avatar">{initials(agreement.clientName)}</div>
                        <div>
                          <strong>{agreement.clientName || '—'}</strong>
                          <span>{agreement.organization || agreement.email || '—'}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td>
                      <div className="agr-telecaller-cell">
                        <FiUser size={13} />
                        <span>{agreement.createdByName || agreement.createdBy || '—'}</span>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td>
                      <div className="agr-plan-cell">
                        <strong>{agreement.agreementType || agreement.serviceType || '—'}</strong>
                        <span>{agreement.planName || '—'}</span>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="agr-amount">
                      {money(agreement.totalPayable || agreement.netPayable || agreement.feeAmount)}
                    </td>
                    <td><StatusPill status={agreement.status} /></td>
                    <td>{fmtDateTime(agreement.submittedAt || agreement.createdAt)}</td>
                    <td>
                      <div className="agr-action-group">
                        <button className="agr-act-btn view" type="button" title="View" onClick={() => setViewAgreement(agreement)}>
                          <FiEye size={14} />
                        </button>
                        {['PENDING_APPROVAL', 'RESUBMITTED'].includes(agreement.status) && (
                          <>
                            <button className="agr-act-btn approve" type="button" title="Approve" onClick={() => setApproveAgreement(agreement)}>
                              <FiCheck size={14} />
                            </button>
                            <button className="agr-act-btn reject" type="button" title="Reject" onClick={() => setRejectAgreement(agreement)}>
                              <FiX size={14} />
                            </button>
                          </>
                        )}
                        {agreement.generatedPdfPath && (
                          <button className="agr-act-btn download" type="button" title="Download PDF" onClick={() => handleDownload(agreement)}>
                            <FiDownload size={14} />
                          </button>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="agr-pagination-bar">
          <div className="agr-rows-select">
            Rows per page:
            <select value={rowsPerPage} onChange={(e) => { setRowsPerPage(Number(e.target.value)); setPage(1); }}>
              {ROWS_OPTIONS.map((n) => <option key={n} value={n}>{n}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="agr-page-nums">
            <button className="agr-pg-btn" type="button" disabled={page === 1} onClick={() => setPage((p) => p - 1)}>
              <FiChevronLeft />
            </button>
            {pageNums().map((number, index) => number === '...'
              ? <span key={`ellipsis-${index}`}>…</span>
              : (
                <button
                  key={number}
                  type="button"
                  className={`agr-pg-btn ${page === number ? 'active' : ''}`}
                  onClick={() => setPage(number)}
                >
                  {number}
                </button>
              ))}
            <button className="agr-pg-btn" type="button" disabled={page === totalPages} onClick={() => setPage((p) => p + 1)}>
              <FiChevronRight />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {viewAgreement && (
        <ViewModal
          agreement={viewAgreement}
          onClose={() => setViewAgreement(null)}
          onDownload={handleDownload}
          onApprove={openApprove}
          onReject={openReject}
        />
      )}

      {approveAgreement && (
        <ApproveModal
          agreement={approveAgreement}
          onClose={() => setApproveAgreement(null)}
          onConfirm={handleApprove}
        />
      )}

      {rejectAgreement && (
        <RejectModal
          agreement={rejectAgreement}
          onClose={() => setRejectAgreement(null)}
          onConfirm={handleReject}
        />
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\pages\Agreements\pageFlow.jsx =====
// src/pages/Agreements/pageFlow.jsx
// Shared by the Telecaller editor and the Super Admin viewer.
// Lays the agreement out on real A4 pages so it matches the generated PDF
// (AgreementPdfService: @page margin 42mm 20mm 52mm 20mm, letterpad stamped on every page):
//   * the letterpad is drawn once per page (never stretched / repeated through the text)
//   * a block that would run into the bottom ribbon/footer area is pushed to the next page
//   * logo + footer are drawn on every page at the same positions the PDF uses
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { FiPhone, FiGlobe, FiMapPin } from 'react-icons/fi';

export const PAGE_W = 794;                 // A4 @96dpi
export const PAGE_H = 1123;
export const PAD_TOP = Math.round(42 * 3.7795);      // 42mm  (logo ends ~39.5mm)
export const PAD_BOTTOM = Math.round(52 * 3.7795);   // 52mm  (footer starts ~249mm)
export const PAD_SIDE = Math.round(20 * 3.7795);     // 20mm

export const pageBackground = (pad) =>
  pad
    ? { backgroundImage: `url(${pad})`, backgroundSize: `100% ${PAGE_H}px`, backgroundRepeat: 'repeat-y' }
    : {};

// Moves whole blocks (direct children of the body element) to the next page when they
// would cross into the bottom margin. Returns the number of pages.
export function usePageFlow(bodyRef, enabled) {
  const [pages, setPages] = useState(1);

  const run = useCallback(() => {
    const body = bodyRef.current;
    if (!body || !enabled) return;
    const kids = Array.from(body.children);
    kids.forEach((k) => { k.style.paddingTop = ''; });
    const bases = kids.map((k) => parseFloat(getComputedStyle(k).paddingTop) || 0);
    const contentH = PAGE_H - PAD_TOP - PAD_BOTTOM;

    kids.forEach((k, i) => {
      const top = k.offsetTop;
      const h = k.offsetHeight;
      const page = Math.floor(top / PAGE_H);
      const limit = (page + 1) * PAGE_H - PAD_BOTTOM;
      const pageStart = page * PAGE_H + PAD_TOP;
      if (top + h > limit && h <= contentH && top > pageStart + 2) {
        k.style.paddingTop = `${(page + 1) * PAGE_H + PAD_TOP - top + bases[i]}px`;
      }
    });

    const last = kids[kids.length - 1];
    const end = last ? last.offsetTop + last.offsetHeight : PAD_TOP;
    const n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((end + PAD_BOTTOM) / PAGE_H));
    setPages((p) => (p === n ? p : n));
  }, [bodyRef, enabled]);

  // after every render (children's effects, e.g. auto-growing textareas, have already run)
  useEffect(() => { run(); });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled || !bodyRef.current || typeof ResizeObserver === 'undefined') return undefined;
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(() => run());
    ro.observe(bodyRef.current);
    return () => ro.disconnect();
  }, [enabled, bodyRef, run]);

  return pages;
}

// Logo + footer for every page, same coordinates as the PDF (fractions of the A4 page).
export function PageOverlays({ pages, logo, brand, footerPhone, footerWeb, footerAddress }) {
  const iconStyle = (top) => ({
    position: 'absolute', left: 0.14 * PAGE_W, top: top * PAGE_H, width: 19, height: 19, borderRadius: '50%',
    background: '#7b1113', color: '#fff', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
  });
  const textStyle = (top, color) => ({
    position: 'absolute', left: 0.185 * PAGE_W, top: top * PAGE_H, height: 0.028 * PAGE_H, lineHeight: `${0.028 * PAGE_H}px`,
    fontFamily: 'Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif', fontSize: 13, color, whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
  });
  return Array.from({ length: pages }, (_, i) => (
    <div key={i} aria-hidden="true"
      style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: i * PAGE_H, width: PAGE_W, height: PAGE_H, pointerEvents: 'none', zIndex: 1 }}>
      {logo && (
        <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 0.094 * PAGE_W, top: 0.028 * PAGE_H, width: 0.46 * PAGE_W, height: 0.105 * PAGE_H, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <img src={logo} alt="" style={{ maxWidth: '100%', maxHeight: '100%', objectFit: 'contain' }} />
        </div>
      )}
      <span style={iconStyle(0.842)}><FiPhone size={11} /></span>
      <span style={textStyle(0.838, '#111827')}>{footerPhone}</span>
      <span style={{ ...iconStyle(0.880), background: '#111827' }}><FiGlobe size={11} /></span>
      <span style={textStyle(0.876, '#1d4ed8')}>{footerWeb}</span>
      <span style={iconStyle(0.914)}><FiMapPin size={11} /></span>
      <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 0.185 * PAGE_W, top: 0.910 * PAGE_H, fontFamily: 'Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif', fontSize: 13, lineHeight: '16px', color: '#c8102e', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
        {footerAddress.slice(0, 3).map((line, k) => (<div key={k}>{line}</div>))}
      </div>
    </div>
  ));
}

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\pages\AttendanceAdmin\AttendanceAdmin.jsx =====
import React,{useCallback,useEffect,useMemo,useState} from 'react';
import {FiCalendar,FiCheckCircle,FiClock,FiEdit2,FiEye,FiLogIn,FiLogOut,FiRefreshCw,FiSearch,FiWifiOff,FiX} from 'react-icons/fi';
import attendanceAdminService from '../../services/attendanceAdminService';
import leavePermissionAdminService from '../../services/leavePermissionAdminService';
import './AttendanceAdmin.css';

const time=v=>v?new Date(v).toLocaleTimeString('en-IN',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',hour12:true}):'—';
const duration=s=>{s=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(s||0)));return `${String(Math.floor(s/3600)).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(Math.floor((s%3600)/60)).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(s%60).padStart(2,'0')}`};
const dateToday=()=>new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA');
export default function AttendanceAdmin(){
 const [date,setDate]=useState(dateToday()),[rows,setRows]=useState([]),[search,setSearch]=useState(''),[selected,setSelected]=useState(null),[edit,setEdit]=useState(null),[loading,setLoading]=useState(true),[error,setError]=useState(''),[saving,setSaving]=useState(false),[requestOpen,setRequestOpen]=useState(false),[request,setRequest]=useState({type:'Permission',permissionType:'1_HOUR',username:'',date:dateToday(),fromTime:'10:30',toTime:'11:30',reason:'',leaveType:'Full-day',fromDate:dateToday(),toDate:dateToday()});
 const load=useCallback(async()=>{setLoading(true);try{setRows(await attendanceAdminService.list(date));setError('')}catch(e){setError(e.message)}finally{setLoading(false)}},[date]);
 useEffect(()=>{load();const i=setInterval(load,15000);return()=>clearInterval(i)},[load]);
 useEffect(()=>{const on=()=>load();window.addEventListener('attendance:update',on);return()=>window.removeEventListener('attendance:update',on)},[load]);
 const filtered=useMemo(()=>rows.filter(r=>(r.fullName||'').toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())||(r.username||'').toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())),[rows,search]);
 const stats=useMemo(()=>({total:rows.length,present:rows.filter(r=>['Present','Permission','Late'].includes(r.status)).length,late:rows.filter(r=>r.status==='Late').length,leave:rows.filter(r=>r.status==='Leave').length,online:rows.filter(r=>r.online).length}),[rows]);
 const openEdit=async r=>{if(!r.id)return;const d=await attendanceAdminService.detail(r.id);setSelected(d);setEdit({status:d.status,firstLoginAt:d.firstLoginAt||'',lastLogoutAt:d.lastLogoutAt||'',workingSeconds:d.workingSeconds||0,remarks:d.remarks||'',reason:''})};
 const save=async()=>{setSaving(true);try{const d=await attendanceAdminService.update(selected.id,edit);setSelected(d);setEdit({...edit,reason:''});await load()}catch(e){alert(e.message)}finally{setSaving(false)}};
 const openRequest=r=>{setRequest({...request,username:r.username,employeeName:r.fullName,date:date,fromDate:date,toDate:date});setRequestOpen(true)};
 const submitRequest=async e=>{e.preventDefault();try{const payload={...request};if(payload.type==='Permission'){delete payload.fromDate;delete payload.toDate;payload.date=date;}else{delete payload.date;delete payload.fromTime;delete payload.toTime;}await leavePermissionAdminService.create(payload);setRequestOpen(false);alert('Request added. Approve it from Leave & Permissions.')}catch(e){alert(e.message)}};
 return <div className="aa-page"><div className="aa-head"><div><h1>Attendance Management</h1><p>Server-time attendance, sessions, network events and audit history.</p></div><div className="aa-controls"><input type="date" value={date} onChange={e=>setDate(e.target.value)}/><div className="aa-search"><FiSearch/><input placeholder="Search telecaller" value={search} onChange={e=>setSearch(e.target.value)}/></div><button onClick={load}><FiRefreshCw/> Refresh</button></div></div>
 {error&&<div className="aa-error">{error}</div>}<div className="aa-stats"><Stat label="Telecallers" value={stats.total}/><Stat label="Present / Permission" value={stats.present}/><Stat label="Late" value={stats.late}/><Stat label="Leave" value={stats.leave}/><Stat label="Online" value={stats.online}/></div>
 <div className="aa-table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Telecaller</th><th>First Login</th><th>Last Logout</th><th>Status</th><th>Permission</th><th>Work Time</th><th>Sessions</th><th>Network</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead><tbody>{loading?<tr><td colSpan="9">Loading…</td></tr>:filtered.map(r=><tr key={r.id||r.employeeId}><td><b>{r.fullName||r.username}</b><small>{r.username}</small></td><td>{time(r.firstLoginAt)}</td><td>{time(r.lastLogoutAt)}</td><td><span className={`aa-status ${String(r.status).toLowerCase()}`}>{r.status}</span></td><td>{r.permission?`${time(r.permission.start)} – ${time(r.permission.end)}`:'—'}</td><td>{duration(r.workingSeconds)}</td><td>{r.sessionCount||0}</td><td>{r.disconnected?<><FiWifiOff/> Disconnected</>:r.online?'Online':'Offline'}</td><td className="aa-actions">{r.id&&<button title="View/Edit" onClick={()=>openEdit(r)}><FiEye/></button>}<button title="Add Leave/Permission" onClick={()=>openRequest(r)}><FiCalendar/></button></td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div>
 {selected&&edit&&<div className="aa-modal" onClick={()=>setSelected(null)}><div className="aa-dialog" onClick={e=>e.stopPropagation()}><div className="aa-dialog-head"><div><h2>{selected.fullName}</h2><p>{selected.date} · {selected.username}</p></div><button onClick={()=>setSelected(null)}><FiX/></button></div><div className="aa-edit-grid"><label>Status<select value={edit.status} onChange={e=>setEdit({...edit,status:e.target.value})}>{['AUTO','Present','Late','Permission','Half Day','Leave','Absent','Offline/Disconnected'].map(x=><option key={x}>{x}</option>)}</select></label><label>First Login<input type="datetime-local" value={edit.firstLoginAt?new Date(edit.firstLoginAt).toLocaleString('sv-SE').replace(' ','T'):''} onChange={e=>setEdit({...edit,firstLoginAt:e.target.value?new Date(e.target.value).toISOString():''})}/></label><label>Last Logout<input type="datetime-local" value={edit.lastLogoutAt?new Date(edit.lastLogoutAt).toLocaleString('sv-SE').replace(' ','T'):''} onChange={e=>setEdit({...edit,lastLogoutAt:e.target.value?new Date(e.target.value).toISOString():''})}/></label><label>Working Seconds<input type="number" min="0" value={edit.workingSeconds} onChange={e=>setEdit({...edit,workingSeconds:e.target.value})}/></label><label className="wide">Remarks<textarea value={edit.remarks} onChange={e=>setEdit({...edit,remarks:e.target.value})}/></label><label className="wide">Correction Reason<input value={edit.reason} onChange={e=>setEdit({...edit,reason:e.target.value})} placeholder="Required for audit context"/></label></div><div className="aa-session"><h3>Session History</h3>{(selected.sessions||[]).map(s=><div key={s.id}><span><FiLogIn/> {time(s.loginAt)} {s.logoutAt&&<><FiLogOut/> {time(s.logoutAt)}</>}</span><span>{s.manuallyLoggedOut?'Manual Logout':s.connected?'Connected':'Disconnected'} · {duration(s.activeSeconds)}</span></div>)}</div><div className="aa-audit"><h3>Audit History</h3>{(selected.audits||[]).length?(selected.audits||[]).map(a=><div key={a.id}><b>{a.field}</b><span>{a.previousValue} → {a.newValue}</span><small>{a.changedBy} · {new Date(a.changedAt).toLocaleString('en-IN')} · {a.reason}</small></div>):<p>No manual changes.</p>}</div><div className="aa-footer"><button onClick={()=>setSelected(null)}>Cancel</button><button className="primary" disabled={saving} onClick={save}>{saving?'Saving…':'Save Attendance'}</button></div></div></div>}
 {requestOpen&&<div className="aa-modal" onClick={()=>setRequestOpen(false)}><form className="aa-dialog" onClick={e=>e.stopPropagation()} onSubmit={submitRequest}><div className="aa-dialog-head"><h2>Add Leave / Permission</h2><button type="button" onClick={()=>setRequestOpen(false)}><FiX/></button></div><label>Type<select value={request.type} onChange={e=>setRequest({...request,type:e.target.value})}><option>Permission</option><option>Leave</option></select></label>{request.type==='Permission'?<><label>Permission Type<select value={request.permissionType} onChange={e=>setRequest({...request,permissionType:e.target.value})}><option value="1_HOUR">1 Hour</option><option value="2_HOURS">2 Hours</option><option value="CUSTOM">Custom</option><option value="HALF_DAY">Half Day</option></select></label><label>Date<input type="date" value={request.date} onChange={e=>setRequest({...request,date:e.target.value})}/></label><div className="aa-two"><label>From<input type="time" value={request.fromTime} onChange={e=>setRequest({...request,fromTime:e.target.value})}/></label><label>To<input type="time" value={request.toTime} onChange={e=>setRequest({...request,toTime:e.target.value})}/></label></div></>:<><label>Leave Type<select value={request.leaveType} onChange={e=>setRequest({...request,leaveType:e.target.value})}><option>Full-day</option><option>Half-day</option><option>Morning Leave</option><option>Afternoon Leave</option></select></label><div className="aa-two"><label>From<input type="date" value={request.fromDate} onChange={e=>setRequest({...request,fromDate:e.target.value})}/></label><label>To<input type="date" value={request.toDate} onChange={e=>setRequest({...request,toDate:e.target.value})}/></label></div></>}<label>Reason<textarea required value={request.reason} onChange={e=>setRequest({...request,reason:e.target.value})}/></label><button className="primary" type="submit">Add Request</button></form></div>}
 </div>
}
const Stat=({label,value})=><div className="aa-stat"><span>{label}</span><b>{value}</b></div>;

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\pages\Calendar\Calendar.jsx =====
import React, {
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
} from 'react';

import {
  FiChevronLeft,
  FiChevronRight,
  FiPlus,
  FiVideo,
  FiX,
  FiTrash2,
} from 'react-icons/fi';

import '../../styles/AdminPages.css';
import calendarService from '../../services/calendarService';

const MONTH_NAMES = [
  'January',
  'February',
  'March',
  'April',
  'May',
  'June',
  'July',
  'August',
  'September',
  'October',
  'November',
  'December',
];

const DAYS_OF_WEEK = [
  'Mon',
  'Tue',
  'Wed',
  'Thu',
  'Fri',
  'Sat',
  'Sun',
];

const pad = (number) =>
  String(number).padStart(2, '0');

const Calendar = () => {

  const today = new Date();

  const [currentDate, setCurrentDate] = useState(
    new Date(
      today.getFullYear(),
      today.getMonth(),
      1
    )
  );

  const [events, setEvents] = useState([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const [showModal, setShowModal] =
    useState(false);

  const [saving, setSaving] =
    useState(false);

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    date: '',
    title: '',
    link: '',
    type: 'MEETING',
    assignedTo: [],
  });

  const [telecallers, setTelecallers] =
    useState([]);

  const year =
    currentDate.getFullYear();

  const month =
    currentDate.getMonth();

  // =====================================================
  // USER ROLE
  // =====================================================

  const user = useMemo(() => {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(
        localStorage.getItem('user') || '{}'
      );
    } catch {
      return {};
    }
  }, []);

  const role = String(
    user?.role || ''
  ).toUpperCase();

  const canManageEvents =
    role === 'SUPER_ADMIN' ||
    role === 'ADMIN';

  // =====================================================
  // MONTH RANGE
  // =====================================================

  const getDateRange = () => {

    const from =
      `${year}-${pad(month + 1)}-01`;

    const lastDay = new Date(
      year,
      month + 1,
      0
    ).getDate();

    const to =
      `${year}-${pad(month + 1)}-${pad(lastDay)}`;

    return {
      from,
      to,
    };
  };

  // =====================================================
  // LOAD CALENDAR
  // =====================================================

  const loadEvents = async () => {

    try {

      setLoading(true);
      setError('');

      const {
        from,
        to,
      } = getDateRange();

      console.log(
        'Loading calendar events:',
        { from, to }
      );

      const response =
        await calendarService.getEvents(
          from,
          to
        );

      console.log(
        'Calendar response:',
        response
      );

      setEvents(
        Array.isArray(response)
          ? response
          : []
      );

    } catch (err) {

      console.error(
        'Calendar load error:',
        err
      );

      setError(
        err?.message ||
        'Failed to load calendar'
      );

      setEvents([]);

    } finally {

      setLoading(false);

    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadEvents();
  }, [year, month]);

  // =====================================================
  // LOAD TELECALLERS
  // =====================================================

  useEffect(() => {

    if (!canManageEvents) {
      return;
    }

    const loadTelecallers =
      async () => {

        try {

          const response =
            await calendarService.getTelecallers();

          setTelecallers(
            Array.isArray(response)
              ? response
              : []
          );

        } catch (err) {

          console.error(
            'Failed to load telecallers:',
            err
          );

          setTelecallers([]);
        }
      };

    loadTelecallers();

  }, [canManageEvents]);

  // =====================================================
  // EVENTS BY DATE
  // =====================================================

  const eventsByDate = useMemo(() => {

    const eventMap = {};

    events.forEach((event) => {

      if (!event?.date) {
        return;
      }

      if (!eventMap[event.date]) {
        eventMap[event.date] = [];
      }

      eventMap[event.date].push(event);
    });

    return eventMap;

  }, [events]);

  // =====================================================
  // CALENDAR GRID
  // =====================================================

  const calendarGrid = useMemo(() => {

    const firstDayOfMonth =
      new Date(year, month, 1);

    const startOffset =
      (firstDayOfMonth.getDay() + 6) % 7;

    const daysInMonth =
      new Date(
        year,
        month + 1,
        0
      ).getDate();

    const cells = [];

    for (
      let i = 0;
      i < startOffset;
      i++
    ) {
      cells.push(null);
    }

    for (
      let day = 1;
      day <= daysInMonth;
      day++
    ) {
      cells.push(day);
    }

    while (
      cells.length % 7 !== 0
    ) {
      cells.push(null);
    }

    return cells;

  }, [year, month]);

  // =====================================================
  // DATE FORMAT
  // =====================================================

  const formatDate = (day) =>
    `${year}-${pad(month + 1)}-${pad(day)}`;

  const todayString =
    new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

  // =====================================================
  // NAVIGATION
  // =====================================================

  const goPreviousMonth = () => {

    setCurrentDate(
      new Date(
        year,
        month - 1,
        1
      )
    );
  };

  const goNextMonth = () => {

    setCurrentDate(
      new Date(
        year,
        month + 1,
        1
      )
    );
  };

  // =====================================================
  // ADD EVENT
  // =====================================================

  const openAddModal = () => {

    const todayValue =
      new Date()
        .toISOString()
        .slice(0, 10);

    setForm({
      date: todayValue,
      title: '',
      link: '',
      type: 'MEETING',
      assignedTo: [],
    });

    setShowModal(true);
  };

  const closeModal = () => {

    setShowModal(false);

    setForm({
      date: '',
      title: '',
      link: '',
      type: 'MEETING',
      assignedTo: [],
    });
  };

  // =====================================================
  // ASSIGNED TELECALLERS
  // =====================================================

  const toggleTelecaller = (
    username
  ) => {

    setForm((previous) => {

      const exists =
        previous.assignedTo.includes(
          username
        );

      return {
        ...previous,

        assignedTo: exists
          ? previous.assignedTo.filter(
              (item) =>
                item !== username
            )
          : [
              ...previous.assignedTo,
              username,
            ],
      };
    });
  };

  // =====================================================
  // SAVE EVENT
  // =====================================================

  const handleSave = async () => {

    if (!form.date) {
      alert(
        'Please select a date'
      );
      return;
    }

    if (
      !form.title ||
      !form.title.trim()
    ) {
      alert(
        'Please enter event title'
      );
      return;
    }

    try {

      setSaving(true);

      await calendarService.createEvent({
        date: form.date,
        title: form.title.trim(),
        link: form.link.trim() || null,
        type: form.type,
        assignedTo: form.assignedTo,
      });

      closeModal();

      await loadEvents();

    } catch (err) {

      console.error(
        'Calendar create error:',
        err
      );

      alert(
        err?.message ||
        'Failed to create event'
      );

    } finally {

      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  // =====================================================
  // DELETE EVENT
  // =====================================================

  const handleDelete = async (
    event,
    mouseEvent
  ) => {

    mouseEvent.stopPropagation();

    if (event.readOnly) {
      return;
    }

    const confirmed =
      window.confirm(
        `Delete "${event.title}"?`
      );

    if (!confirmed) {
      return;
    }

    try {

      await calendarService.deleteEvent(
        event.id
      );

      await loadEvents();

    } catch (err) {

      console.error(
        'Delete error:',
        err
      );

      alert(
        err?.message ||
        'Failed to delete event'
      );
    }
  };

  // =====================================================
  // EVENT CLICK
  // =====================================================

  const handleEventClick = (
    event
  ) => {

    if (event.link) {
      window.open(
        event.link,
        '_blank',
        'noopener,noreferrer'
      );
    }
  };

  // =====================================================
  // RENDER
  // =====================================================

  return (
    <div className="ap-page">

      {/* HEADER */}

      <div className="ap-header">

        <div>

          <div className="ap-title">
            Calendar
          </div>

          <div className="ap-subtitle">
            Live meetings, follow-ups and holidays
          </div>

        </div>

        {canManageEvents && (

          <button
            className="ap-btn-primary"
            onClick={openAddModal}
          >

            <FiPlus />

            Add Event

          </button>

        )}

      </div>

      {/* ERROR */}

      {error && (

        <div
          style={{
            marginBottom: 12,
            color: '#dc2626',
            fontWeight: 600,
          }}
        >

          {error}

        </div>

      )}

      {/* MONTH NAVIGATION */}

      <div
        className="ap-card"
        style={{
          marginBottom: 16,
        }}
      >

        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'space-between',
          }}
        >

          <button
            className="ap-btn-icon"
            onClick={goPreviousMonth}
            title="Previous month"
          >

            <FiChevronLeft />

          </button>

          <div
            style={{
              fontWeight: 700,
              fontSize: 15,
            }}
          >

            {MONTH_NAMES[month]} {year}

          </div>

          <button
            className="ap-btn-icon"
            onClick={goNextMonth}
            title="Next month"
          >

            <FiChevronRight />

          </button>

        </div>

      </div>

      {/* CALENDAR */}

      {loading ? (

        <div
          className="ap-card"
          style={{
            padding: 30,
            textAlign: 'center',
          }}
        >

          Loading calendar...

        </div>

      ) : (

        <div className="cal-grid">

          {DAYS_OF_WEEK.map(
            (day) => (

              <div
                key={day}
                className="cal-dow"
              >

                {day}

              </div>

            )
          )}

          {calendarGrid.map(
            (day, index) => {

              const dateString =
                day
                  ? formatDate(day)
                  : null;

              const dayEvents =
                dateString
                  ? eventsByDate[
                      dateString
                    ] || []
                  : [];

              const isToday =
                dateString ===
                todayString;

              return (

                <div
                  key={index}
                  className={`
                    cal-cell
                    ${!day ? 'outside' : ''}
                    ${isToday ? 'today' : ''}
                  `}
                >

                  {day && (

                    <div
                      className="cal-daynum"
                    >

                      {day}

                    </div>

                  )}

                  {dayEvents
                    .slice(0, 3)
                    .map(
                      (
                        event,
                        eventIndex
                      ) => (

                        <div
                          key={
                            event.id ||
                            eventIndex
                          }
                          className={`
                            cal-event
                            ${String(
                              event.type ||
                              ''
                            ).toLowerCase()}
                          `}
                          title={
                            event.title
                          }
                          onClick={() =>
                            handleEventClick(
                              event
                            )
                          }
                          style={{
                            cursor:
                              event.link
                                ? 'pointer'
                                : 'default',
                          }}
                        >

                          {String(
                            event.type
                          ).toUpperCase() ===
                            'MEETING' && (

                            <FiVideo
                              size={10}
                              style={{
                                marginRight: 3,
                              }}
                            />

                          )}

                          <span
                            style={{
                              overflow:
                                'hidden',
                              textOverflow:
                                'ellipsis',
                              whiteSpace:
                                'nowrap',
                              flex: 1,
                            }}
                          >

                            {event.title}

                          </span>

                          {canManageEvents &&
                            !event.readOnly && (

                              <FiTrash2
                                size={11}
                                onClick={(
                                  e
                                ) =>
                                  handleDelete(
                                    event,
                                    e
                                  )
                                }
                                style={{
                                  marginLeft: 4,
                                  cursor:
                                    'pointer',
                                }}
                              />

                            )}

                        </div>

                      )
                    )}

                  {dayEvents.length >
                    3 && (

                    <div
                      style={{
                        fontSize: 10,
                        color:
                          'var(--text-light)',
                        marginTop: 2,
                      }}
                    >

                      +
                      {dayEvents.length -
                        3}
                      {' '}more

                    </div>

                  )}

                </div>

              );
            }
          )}

        </div>

      )}

      {/* ADD EVENT MODAL */}

      {showModal && (

        <div
          className="ap-modal-overlay"
          onClick={closeModal}
        >

          <div
            className="ap-modal-box"
            onClick={(event) =>
              event.stopPropagation()
            }
          >

            <div
              className="ap-modal-title"
            >

              <span>
                Add Calendar Event
              </span>

              <button
                className="ap-btn-icon"
                onClick={closeModal}
              >

                <FiX />

              </button>

            </div>

            {/* DATE */}

            <div className="ap-form-group">

              <label>
                Date *
              </label>

              <input
                type="date"
                value={form.date}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setForm(
                    (previous) => ({
                      ...previous,
                      date:
                        event.target.value,
                    })
                  )
                }
              />

            </div>

            {/* TITLE */}

            <div className="ap-form-group">

              <label>
                Title *
              </label>

              <input
                value={form.title}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setForm(
                    (previous) => ({
                      ...previous,
                      title:
                        event.target.value,
                    })
                  )
                }
                placeholder="Event title"
              />

            </div>

            {/* TYPE */}

            <div className="ap-form-group">

              <label>
                Event Type
              </label>

              <select
                value={form.type}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setForm(
                    (previous) => ({
                      ...previous,
                      type:
                        event.target.value,
                    })
                  )
                }
              >

                <option value="MEETING">
                  Meeting
                </option>

                <option value="HOLIDAY">
                  Holiday
                </option>

                <option value="EVENT">
                  Event
                </option>

              </select>

            </div>

            {/* LINK */}

            <div className="ap-form-group">

              <label>
                Meeting Link
              </label>

              <input
                value={form.link}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setForm(
                    (previous) => ({
                      ...previous,
                      link:
                        event.target.value,
                    })
                  )
                }
                placeholder="https://meet.google.com/..."
              />

            </div>

            {/* TELECALLERS */}

            {telecallers.length > 0 && (

              <div className="ap-form-group">

                <label>
                  Assign Telecallers
                </label>

                <div
                  style={{
                    maxHeight: 150,
                    overflowY: 'auto',
                    border:
                      '1px solid #e5e7eb',
                    borderRadius: 8,
                    padding: 8,
                  }}
                >

                  {telecallers.map(
                    (telecaller) => {

                      const username =
                        telecaller.username;

                      const checked =
                        form.assignedTo.includes(
                          username
                        );

                      return (

                        <label
                          key={username}
                          style={{
                            display:
                              'flex',
                            alignItems:
                              'center',
                            gap: 8,
                            padding:
                              '6px 4px',
                            cursor:
                              'pointer',
                          }}
                        >

                          <input
                            type="checkbox"
                            checked={
                              checked
                            }
                            onChange={() =>
                              toggleTelecaller(
                                username
                              )
                            }
                          />

                          <span>

                            {telecaller.fullName ||
                              username}

                            {telecaller.employeeId &&
                              ` (${telecaller.employeeId})`}

                          </span>

                        </label>

                      );
                    }
                  )}

                </div>

                <small
                  style={{
                    color: '#64748b',
                  }}
                >

                  Leave empty to make
                  the event visible to all
                  telecallers.

                </small>

              </div>

            )}

            {/* ACTIONS */}

            <div
              className="ap-form-actions"
            >

              <button
                className="ap-btn-outline"
                onClick={closeModal}
                disabled={saving}
              >

                Cancel

              </button>

              <button
                className="ap-btn-primary"
                onClick={handleSave}
                disabled={saving}
              >

                {saving
                  ? 'Saving...'
                  : 'Add Event'}

              </button>

            </div>

          </div>

        </div>

      )}

    </div>
  );
};

export default Calendar;

const AVATAR_COLORS = ['#7b1113','#2563eb','#7c3aed','#d97706','#16a34a','#0891b2','#db2777','#ea580c'];
const avatarColor = n => AVATAR_COLORS[n.charCodeAt(0) % AVATAR_COLORS.length];
const fmtTime = s => {
  const h=Math.floor(s/3600), m=Math.floor((s%3600)/60), sec=s%60;
  return h>0
    ? `${String(h).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(m).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(sec).padStart(2,'0')}`
    : `${String(m).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(sec).padStart(2,'0')}`;
};

const IdleCountdown = ({ seconds, total, onCancel }) => {
  const pct = ((total - seconds) / total) * 100;
  return (
    <div className="idle-countdown">
      <div className="idle-top">
        <span className="idle-label">⏳ Auto-connecting next call in</span>
        <button className="idle-cancel" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</button>
      </div>
      <div className="idle-secs">{seconds}s</div>
      <div className="idle-bar-bg"><div className="idle-bar-fill" style={{ width:`${pct}%` }}></div></div>
    </div>
  );
};

const AgentStatusSelector = ({ status, onChange }) => {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const dropdownRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (event) => {
      if (dropdownRef.current && !dropdownRef.current.contains(event.target)) {
        setIsOpen(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  const currentStatus = AGENT_STATUSES[status] || AGENT_STATUSES['Available'];

  return (
    <div className="agent-status-selector" ref={dropdownRef}>
      <button 
        className="agent-status-trigger"
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)}
        style={{ 
          background: currentStatus.bg
        }}
      >
        <span className="status-label">{currentStatus.label}</span>
        <span className="status-arrow">▼</span>
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div className="agent-status-dropdown">
          {Object.entries(AGENT_STATUSES).map(([key, value]) => (
            <button
              key={key}
              className={`status-option ${status === key ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => {
                onChange(key);
                setIsOpen(false);
              }}
              style={{ 
                background: status === key ? value.bg : 'transparent',
                borderLeft: status === key ? '3px solid #7b1113' : '3px solid transparent'
              }}
            >
              <span className="status-option-label">{value.label}</span>
              {status === key && (
                <span className="status-check">✓</span>
              )}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

const DialpadModal = ({ onClose, onDial }) => {
  const [num, setNum] = useState('');
  const keys = ['1','2','3','4','5','6','7','8','9','*','0','#'];
  return (
    <div className="modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="cp-dialpad-modal" onClick={e=>e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="modal-header">
          <span className="modal-title">Dialpad</span>
          <button className="modal-close" onClick={onClose}>✕</button>
        </div>
        <div className="dp-display">{num||<span style={{color:'#aaa'}}>Enter number</span>}</div>
        <div className="dp-grid">
          {keys.map(k=><button key={k} className="dp-key" onClick={()=>setNum(n=>n+k)}>{k}</button>)}
        </div>
        <div className="dp-actions">
          <button className="dp-backspace" onClick={()=>setNum(n=>n.slice(0,-1))}>⌫</button>
          <button className="dp-call-btn" onClick={()=>{if(num){onDial(num);onClose();}}}>📞 Call</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="form-row">
            <div className="form-group"><label>Date</label><input type="date" value={date} onChange={e=>setDate(e.target.value)} /></div>
            <div className="form-group"><label>Time</label><input type="time" value={time} onChange={e=>setTime(e.target.value)} /></div>
          </div>
          <div className="form-group"><label>Note</label><textarea rows={3} value={note} onChange={e=>setNote(e.target.value)} placeholder="Describe the follow-up…" /></div>
          <div className="form-actions">
            <button className="btn-outline" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
            <button className="btn-primary" onClick={()=>{if(!date||!time){alert('Please pick date and time.');return;}onSave({type,date,time,note});onClose();}}>Schedule</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

  const [msg,setMsg]=useState('');
  return (

        <div className="modal-header">
          <span className="modal-title">🔔 Add Reminder</span>
          <button className="modal-close" onClick={onClose}>✕</button>
        </div>
        <div className="modal-body">
          <div className="form-group">
            <label>Remind me in</label>
            <select value={mins} onChange={e=>setMins(e.target.value)}>
              {['5','10','15','30','60','120'].map(m=><option key={m} value={m}>{m} minutes</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="form-group"><label>Message</label><textarea rows={2} value={msg} onChange={e=>setMsg(e.target.value)} placeholder="Reminder message…" /></div>
          <div className="form-actions">
            <button className="btn-outline" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
            <button className="btn-primary" onClick={()=>{onSave({mins,msg});onClose();}}>Set Reminder</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
