// ─── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const avatarColor = (name) =>
  AVATAR_COLORS[(name?.charCodeAt(0) || 0) % AVATAR_COLORS.length];

const initials = (name) =>
  name?.split(' ').slice(0, 2).map(w => w[0]?.toUpperCase()).join('') || '?';

const fmtDate = (d) => {
  if (!d) return '—';
  const dt = new Date(d);
  return dt.toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: '2-digit' });
};

const fmtTime = (t) => {
  if (!t) return '—';
  const [h, m] = t.split(':');
  const hr = parseInt(h, 10);
  return `${hr % 12 || 12}:${m} ${hr < 12 ? 'AM' : 'PM'}`;
};

const generateMeetLink = () => {
  const seg = () => Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 5);
  return `https://meet.google.com/${seg()}-${seg()}-${seg()}`;
};

const isToday = (d) => {
  const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  return d === today;
};

const isUpcoming = (d) => d > new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

// ─── Empty Demo Template ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const emptyDemo = () => ({
  id: '', clientName: '', leadId: '', email: '', phone: '',
  topic: '', date: '', time: '', duration: '60 min',
  executive: 'Hari Krishnan', meetType: 'meet', meetLink: '',
  status: 'Scheduled', notes: '', createdOn: '', timeline: [],
});

// ─── Toast ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const Toast = ({ msg, type, onClose }) => (
  <div className={`dm-toast dm-toast-${type}`} onClick={onClose}>
    {type === 'success' ? <FiCheck /> : type === 'error' ? <FiX /> : <FiZap />}
    {msg}
  </div>
);

// ─── Status Pill ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const StatusPill = ({ status }) => {
  const cfg = STATUS_CONFIG[status] || { cls: '', label: status };
  return (
    <span className={`dm-status-pill ${cfg.cls}`}>
      {status === 'Live' && <span className="dm-live-dot" />}
      {cfg.label}
    </span>
  );
};

// ─── Join / Meeting Button ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const MeetBtn = ({ demo }) => {
  if (demo.status === 'Cancelled') return null;
  if (demo.meetType === 'phone') {
    return (
      <a href={`tel:${demo.phone}`} className="dm-join-btn">
        <FiPhone size={12}/> Call
      </a>
    );
  }
  if (demo.meetType === 'office') {
    return <span className="dm-join-btn-disabled"><FiMapPin size={12}/> In-Person</span>;
  }
  if (demo.meetLink) {
    return (
      <a href={demo.meetLink} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="dm-join-btn">
        <FiVideo size={12}/> Join
      </a>
    );
  }
  return <span className="dm-join-btn-disabled"><FiVideo size={12}/> No Link</span>;
};

// ─── Schedule / Edit Modal ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const DemoFormModal = ({ initial, onSave, onClose, allDemos }) => {
  const isEdit = !!initial?.id;
  const [form, setForm] = useState(
    isEdit ? { ...initial } : emptyDemo()
  );
  const [linkCopied, setLinkCopied] = useState(false);

  const set = (k, v) => setForm(f => ({ ...f, [k]: v }));

  const handleGenerateLink = () => {
    set('meetLink', generateMeetLink());
    set('meetType', 'meet');
  };

  const handleCopyLink = () => {
    if (!form.meetLink) return;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(form.meetLink).catch(() => {});
    setLinkCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setLinkCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    if (!form.clientName.trim()) return;
    if (!form.date || !form.time) return;

    const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-IN', {
      day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric',
    });

    if (isEdit) {
      onSave({ ...form });
    } else {
      const newId = `DM-${1000 + allDemos.length + 1}`;
      onSave({
        ...form,
        id: newId,
        createdOn: today,
        timeline: [
          { action: 'Demo Scheduled', time: `${today} · ${fmtTime(form.time)}`, icon: '📅', bg: '#dbeafe', color: '#1d4ed8' },
          ...(form.meetLink ? [{ action: 'Meet link generated', time: `${today}`, icon: '🔗', bg: '#e0e7ff', color: '#4338ca' }] : []),
        ],
      });
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="dm-modal-overlay" onClick={(e) => e.target === e.currentTarget && onClose()}>
      <div className="dm-modal-box dm-modal-box-wide">
        <div className="dm-modal-header">
          <span className="dm-modal-title">{isEdit ? 'Edit Demo' : 'Schedule New Demo'}</span>
          <button className="dm-modal-close" onClick={onClose}><FiX /></button>
        </div>
        <div className="dm-modal-body">
          <div className="dm-form">

            {/* Client Info */}
            <div className="dm-form-section">
              <div className="dm-form-section-title">Client Information</div>
              <div className="dm-form-row">
                <div className="dm-form-group">
                  <label>Client Name *</label>
                  <input value={form.clientName} onChange={e => set('clientName', e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Aravind M" />
                </div>
                <div className="dm-form-group">
                  <label>Lead ID</label>
                  <input value={form.leadId} onChange={e => set('leadId', e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. LD-5261" />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="dm-form-row">
                <div className="dm-form-group">
                  <label>Email</label>
                  <input type="email" value={form.email} onChange={e => set('email', e.target.value)} placeholder="client@email.com" />
                </div>
                <div className="dm-form-group">
                  <label>Phone Number</label>
                  <input value={form.phone} onChange={e => set('phone', e.target.value)} placeholder="+91 XXXXX XXXXX" />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Demo Details */}
            <div className="dm-form-section">
              <div className="dm-form-section-title">Demo Details</div>
              <div className="dm-form-group">
                <label>Demo Topic</label>
                <input value={form.topic} onChange={e => set('topic', e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. School ERP – Admission & Fee Module" />
              </div>
              <div className="dm-form-row">
                <div className="dm-form-group">
                  <label>Demo Date *</label>
                  <input type="date" value={form.date} onChange={e => set('date', e.target.value)} />
                </div>
                <div className="dm-form-group">
                  <label>Demo Time *</label>
                  <input type="time" value={form.time} onChange={e => set('time', e.target.value)} />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="dm-form-row">
                <div className="dm-form-group">
                  <label>Duration</label>
                  <select value={form.duration} onChange={e => set('duration', e.target.value)}>
                    {['15 min','30 min','45 min','60 min','90 min','2 hrs'].map(d => <option key={d}>{d}</option>)}
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div className="dm-form-group">
                  <label>Assigned Executive</label>
                  <select value={form.executive} onChange={e => set('executive', e.target.value)}>
                    {EXECUTIVES.filter(e => e !== 'All').map(ex => <option key={ex}>{ex}</option>)}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>
              {isEdit && (
                <div className="dm-form-group">
                  <label>Status</label>
                  <select value={form.status} onChange={e => set('status', e.target.value)}>
                    {DEMO_STATUSES.map(s => <option key={s}>{s}</option>)}
                  </select>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Meeting Details */}
            <div className="dm-form-section">
              <div className="dm-form-section-title">Meeting Details</div>
              <div className="dm-form-group">
                <label>Meeting Type</label>
                <div className="dm-meeting-types">
                  {MEETING_TYPES.map(mt => (
                    <button
                      key={mt.id}
                      className={`dm-meeting-type-btn ${form.meetType === mt.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
                      onClick={() => set('meetType', mt.id)}
                      type="button"
                    >
                      {mt.icon} {mt.label}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              {form.meetType === 'meet' && (
                <div className="dm-form-group">
                  <label>Google Meet Link</label>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 8 }}>
                    <div className="dm-meet-link-row">
                      <input
                        className="dm-meet-link-input"
                        value={form.meetLink}
                        onChange={e => set('meetLink', e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="https://meet.google.com/xxx-xxxx-xxx"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div className="dm-meet-actions">
                      <button className="dm-generate-btn" onClick={handleGenerateLink} type="button">
                        <FiRefreshCw size={12}/> Generate Link
                      </button>
                      {form.meetLink && (
                        <>
                          <button className="dm-copy-btn" onClick={handleCopyLink} type="button">
                            {linkCopied ? <FiCheck size={12}/> : <FiCopy size={12}/>}
                            {linkCopied ? 'Copied!' : 'Copy Link'}
                          </button>
                          <a href={form.meetLink} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="dm-open-btn">
                            <FiExternalLink size={12}/> Open Meeting
                          </a>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Notes */}
            <div className="dm-form-section">
              <div className="dm-form-section-title">Notes</div>
              <div className="dm-form-group">
                <label>Internal Notes</label>
                <textarea
                  rows={3}
                  value={form.notes}
                  onChange={e => set('notes', e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Add any notes, preparation details, or client requirements..."
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="dm-form-actions">
              <button className="dm-btn-outline" onClick={onClose} type="button">Cancel</button>
              <button className="dm-btn-primary" onClick={handleSave} type="button">
                <FiCheck size={14}/> {isEdit ? 'Save Changes' : 'Schedule Demo'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// ─── View Demo Modal ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const DemoViewModal = ({ demo, onClose, onEdit }) => {
  const [linkCopied, setLinkCopied] = useState(false);

  const copyLink = () => {
    if (!demo.meetLink) return;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(demo.meetLink).catch(() => {});
    setLinkCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setLinkCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  const infoRows = [
    ['Lead ID',    demo.leadId  || '—'],
    ['Email',      demo.email   || '—'],
    ['Phone',      demo.phone   || '—'],
    ['Topic',      demo.topic   || '—'],
    ['Date',       fmtDate(demo.date)],
    ['Time',       fmtTime(demo.time)],
    ['Duration',   demo.duration || '—'],
    ['Executive',  demo.executive || '—'],
    ['Status',     <StatusPill key="s" status={demo.status} />],
    ['Meeting',    demo.meetType === 'meet' ? 'Google Meet' : demo.meetType === 'office' ? 'Office Visit' : 'Phone Call'],
    ['Created On', demo.createdOn || '—'],
    ['Demo ID',    demo.id],
  ];

  return (
    <div className="dm-modal-overlay" onClick={(e) => e.target === e.currentTarget && onClose()}>
      <div className="dm-modal-box dm-modal-box-wide">
        <div className="dm-modal-header">
          <span className="dm-modal-title">Demo Information</span>
          <button className="dm-modal-close" onClick={onClose}><FiX /></button>
        </div>

        <div className="dm-view-body">
          {/* Hero */}
          <div className="dm-view-hero">
            <div className="dm-view-avatar" style={{ background: avatarColor(demo.clientName) }}>
              {initials(demo.clientName)}
            </div>
            <div className="dm-view-hero-info">
              <div className="dm-view-client-name">{demo.clientName}</div>
              <div className="dm-view-demo-id">{demo.id} · {demo.topic}</div>
            </div>
            <StatusPill status={demo.status} />
          </div>

          {/* Info Grid */}
          <div className="dm-view-grid">
            {infoRows.map(([k, v]) => (
              <div className="dm-view-row" key={k}>
                <div className="dm-view-key">{k}</div>
                <div className="dm-view-val">{v}</div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Join Bar */}
          {demo.meetType === 'meet' && demo.meetLink && (
            <div className="dm-view-join-bar">
              <a href={demo.meetLink} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="dm-btn-primary" style={{ textDecoration: 'none' }}>
                <FiVideo size={14}/> Join Meeting
              </a>
              <button className="dm-btn-outline" onClick={copyLink}>
                {linkCopied ? <FiCheck size={13}/> : <FiCopy size={13}/>}
                {linkCopied ? 'Copied!' : 'Copy Link'}
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Notes */}
          {demo.notes && (
            <div className="dm-view-notes">
              <div className="dm-view-notes-title">Notes</div>
              <div className="dm-view-notes-text">{demo.notes}</div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Timeline */}
          {demo.timeline?.length > 0 && (
            <div className="dm-timeline">
              <div className="dm-timeline-title">Activity Timeline</div>
              <div className="dm-timeline-list">
                {demo.timeline.map((t, i) => (
                  <div className="dm-timeline-item" key={i}>
                    <div className="dm-timeline-left">
                      <div className="dm-timeline-dot" style={{ background: t.bg, color: t.color, fontSize: 14 }}>
                        {t.icon}
                      </div>
                      <div className="dm-timeline-line" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="dm-timeline-content">
                      <div className="dm-timeline-action">{t.action}</div>
                      <div className="dm-timeline-time">{t.time}</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Actions */}
          <div className="dm-form-actions" style={{ padding: '14px 24px 24px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
            <button className="dm-btn-outline" onClick={onClose}>Close</button>
            <button className="dm-btn-primary" onClick={() => { onClose(); onEdit(demo); }}>
              <FiEdit2 size={13}/> Edit Demo
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// ─── Main Demo Page ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const Demo = () => {
  const [demos, setDemos]         = useState(INITIAL_DEMOS);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('All');
  const [search, setSearch]       = useState('');
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState('All');
  const [filterExec, setFilterExec]     = useState('All');
  const [filterDate, setFilterDate]     = useState('');
  const [page, setPage]           = useState(1);
  const [rowsPerPage, setRowsPerPage] = useState(10);

  const [showSchedule, setShowSchedule] = useState(false);
  const [editDemo, setEditDemo]         = useState(null);
  const [viewDemo, setViewDemo]         = useState(null);
  const [toast, setToast]               = useState(null);

  const showToast = useCallback((msg, type = 'success') => {
    setToast({ msg, type });
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 3000);
  }, []);

  // ── Computed Stats ──
  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    return {
      total:    demos.length,
      todayDemos: demos.filter(d => d.date === today).length,
      upcoming: demos.filter(d => isUpcoming(d.date) && d.status === 'Scheduled').length,
      completed:demos.filter(d => d.status === 'Completed').length,
      cancelled:demos.filter(d => d.status === 'Cancelled').length,
    };
  }, [demos]);

  const TABS = [
    { id: 'All',       label: 'All',       count: demos.length },
    { id: 'Today',     label: "Today's",   count: demos.filter(d => isToday(d.date)).length },
    { id: 'Upcoming',  label: 'Upcoming',  count: demos.filter(d => isUpcoming(d.date) && d.status === 'Scheduled').length },
    { id: 'Completed', label: 'Completed', count: demos.filter(d => d.status === 'Completed').length },
    { id: 'Cancelled', label: 'Cancelled', count: demos.filter(d => d.status === 'Cancelled').length },
  ];

  // ── Filtered & Paginated ──
  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    let d = [...demos];
    if (activeTab === 'Today')     d = d.filter(x => isToday(x.date));
    if (activeTab === 'Upcoming')  d = d.filter(x => isUpcoming(x.date) && x.status === 'Scheduled');
    if (activeTab === 'Completed') d = d.filter(x => x.status === 'Completed');
    if (activeTab === 'Cancelled') d = d.filter(x => x.status === 'Cancelled');
    if (filterStatus !== 'All')   d = d.filter(x => x.status === filterStatus);
    if (filterExec   !== 'All')   d = d.filter(x => x.executive === filterExec);
    if (filterDate)               d = d.filter(x => x.date === filterDate);
    if (search.trim()) {
      const q = search.toLowerCase();
      d = d.filter(x =>
        x.clientName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        x.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        x.leadId.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        x.topic.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
    }
    return d;
  }, [demos, activeTab, filterStatus, filterExec, filterDate, search]);

  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / rowsPerPage));
  const paginated  = filtered.slice((page - 1) * rowsPerPage, page * rowsPerPage);

  const pageNums = () => {
    const nums = [];
    for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
      if (i === 1 || i === totalPages || Math.abs(i - page) <= 1) nums.push(i);
      else if (nums[nums.length - 1] !== '...') nums.push('...');
    }
    return nums;
  };

  // ── CRUD ──
  const handleAddSave = (d) => {
    setDemos(prev => [d, ...prev]);
    setShowSchedule(false);
    showToast(`Demo ${d.id} scheduled successfully!`);
  };

  const handleEditSave = (d) => {
    setDemos(prev => prev.map(x => x.id === d.id ? d : x));
    setEditDemo(null);
    showToast('Demo updated successfully!');
  };

  const handleDelete = (id) => {
    if (!window.confirm('Delete this demo permanently?')) return;
    setDemos(prev => prev.filter(x => x.id !== id));
    showToast('Demo deleted.', 'error');
  };

  const handleCancel = (id) => {
    setDemos(prev => prev.map(x =>
      x.id === id ? {
        ...x,
        status: 'Cancelled',
        timeline: [...(x.timeline || []), {
          action: 'Demo Cancelled',
          time: new Date().toLocaleString('en-IN'),
          icon: '❌', bg: '#fee2e2', color: '#dc2626',
        }],
      } : x
    ));
    showToast('Demo cancelled.', 'warn');
  };

  const handleCopyLink = (demo) => {
    if (!demo.meetLink) { showToast('No meet link for this demo.', 'error'); return; }
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(demo.meetLink).catch(() => {});
    showToast('Meet link copied!', 'info');
  };

  const handleCall = (demo) => {
    window.open(`tel:${demo.phone}`, '_self');
  };

  const handleTabChange = (t) => { setActiveTab(t); setPage(1); };
  const handleSearch = (e) => { setSearch(e.target.value); setPage(1); };

  return (
    <div className="dm-page">

      {/* Toast */}
      {toast && <Toast msg={toast.msg} type={toast.type} onClose={() => setToast(null)} />}

      {/* Header */}
      <div className="dm-page-header">
        <div>
          <div className="dm-page-title">Demo Management</div>
          
        </div>
        <div className="dm-header-actions">
          <button className="dm-btn-outline">
            <FiCalendar size={14}/> Export
          </button>
          <button className="dm-btn-primary" onClick={() => setShowSchedule(true)}>
            <FiPlus size={15}/> Schedule Demo
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Stats Row */}
      <div className="dm-stats-row">
        <div className="dm-stat-card" style={{ borderTop: '3px solid #7b1113' }}>
          <div className="dm-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#fce8e8', color: '#7b1113' }}><FiMonitor /></div>
          <div className="dm-stat-info">
            <div className="dm-stat-val">{stats.total}</div>
            <div className="dm-stat-label">Total Demos</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="dm-stat-card" style={{ borderTop: '3px solid #2563eb' }}>
          <div className="dm-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#dbeafe', color: '#2563eb' }}><FiCalendar /></div>
          <div className="dm-stat-info">
            <div className="dm-stat-val">{stats.todayDemos}</div>
            <div className="dm-stat-label">Today's Demos</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="dm-stat-card" style={{ borderTop: '3px solid #16a34a' }}>
          <div className="dm-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#dcfce7', color: '#16a34a' }}><FiClock /></div>
          <div className="dm-stat-info">
            <div className="dm-stat-val">{stats.upcoming}</div>
            <div className="dm-stat-label">Upcoming</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="dm-stat-card" style={{ borderTop: '3px solid #4338ca' }}>
          <div className="dm-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#e0e7ff', color: '#4338ca' }}><FiCheck /></div>
          <div className="dm-stat-info">
            <div className="dm-stat-val">{stats.completed}</div>
            <div className="dm-stat-label">Completed</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="dm-stat-card" style={{ borderTop: '3px solid #dc2626' }}>
          <div className="dm-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#fee2e2', color: '#dc2626' }}><FiX /></div>
          <div className="dm-stat-info">
            <div className="dm-stat-val">{stats.cancelled}</div>
            <div className="dm-stat-label">Cancelled</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Table Card */}
      <div className="dm-table-card">

        {/* Tabs */}
        <div className="dm-tabs-row">
          {TABS.map(t => (
            <button
              key={t.id}
              className={`dm-tab ${activeTab === t.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => handleTabChange(t.id)}
            >
              {t.label}
              <span className="dm-tab-badge">{t.count}</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Filters */}
        <div className="dm-filter-bar">
          <div className="dm-search-wrap">
            <FiSearch className="dm-search-ico" />
            <input
              className="dm-search-input"
              placeholder="Search demo, client, lead ID…"
              value={search}
              onChange={handleSearch}
            />
          </div>
          <select
            className="dm-filter-select"
            value={filterStatus}
            onChange={e => { setFilterStatus(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}
          >
            <option value="All">All Status</option>
            {DEMO_STATUSES.map(s => <option key={s}>{s}</option>)}
          </select>
          <select
            className="dm-filter-select"
            value={filterExec}
            onChange={e => { setFilterExec(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}
          >
            {EXECUTIVES.map(e => (
              <option key={e} value={e}>{e === 'All' ? 'All Executives' : e}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <input
            type="date"
            className="dm-filter-select"
            value={filterDate}
            onChange={e => { setFilterDate(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}
            style={{ minWidth: 140 }}
          />
          {(filterStatus !== 'All' || filterExec !== 'All' || filterDate || search) && (
            <button className="dm-btn-ghost" onClick={() => {
              setFilterStatus('All'); setFilterExec('All');
              setFilterDate(''); setSearch(''); setPage(1);
            }}>
              <FiX size={13}/> Clear
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Schedule button row (table header) */}
        <div className="dm-schedule-row">
          <span className="dm-schedule-label">
            Demo List
            <span style={{ fontWeight: 400, color: 'var(--text-light)', fontSize: 12.5, marginLeft: 8 }}>
              ({filtered.length} record{filtered.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''})
            </span>
          </span>
          <button className="dm-btn-primary" style={{ fontSize: 12.5, padding: '7px 14px' }} onClick={() => setShowSchedule(true)}>
            <FiPlus size={13}/> Schedule Demo
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Table */}
        <div className="dm-table-wrap">
          <table className="dm-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Demo ID</th>
                <th>Client</th>
                <th>Date</th>
                <th>Time</th>
                <th>Executive</th>
                <th>Meeting</th>
                <th>Status</th>
                <th>Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {paginated.length === 0 ? (
                <tr className="dm-empty-row">
                  <td colSpan={8}>
                    {search ? `No demos found matching "${search}"` : 'No demos found. Try adjusting your filters.'}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : paginated.map(demo => (
                <tr key={demo.id}>
                  <td><span className="dm-id">{demo.id}</span></td>
                  <td>
                    <div className="dm-client-cell">
                      <div className="dm-client-avatar" style={{ background: avatarColor(demo.clientName) }}>
                        {initials(demo.clientName)}
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <div className="dm-client-name">{demo.clientName}</div>
                        <div className="dm-client-lead">{demo.leadId}</div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <div className="dm-date-cell">{fmtDate(demo.date)}</div>
                    <div className="dm-time-cell" style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--text-light)' }}>{demo.duration}</div>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <div className="dm-date-cell">{fmtTime(demo.time)}</div>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <div className="dm-exec-cell">{demo.executive}</div>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <MeetBtn demo={demo} />
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <StatusPill status={demo.status} />
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <div className="dm-action-group">
                      <button className="dm-act-btn view" title="View" onClick={() => setViewDemo(demo)}>
                        <FiEye />
                      </button>
                      <button className="dm-act-btn edit" title="Edit" onClick={() => setEditDemo(demo)}>
                        <FiEdit2 />
                      </button>
                      {demo.meetLink && (
                        <button className="dm-act-btn copy" title="Copy Link" onClick={() => handleCopyLink(demo)}>
                          <FiCopy />
                        </button>
                      )}
                      <button className="dm-act-btn call" title="Call Client" onClick={() => handleCall(demo)}>
                        <FiPhone />
                      </button>
                      {demo.meetLink && demo.meetType === 'meet' && demo.status !== 'Cancelled' && (
                        <button className="dm-act-btn join" title="Join Meet"
                          onClick={() => window.open(demo.meetLink, '_blank')}>
                          <FiVideo />
                        </button>
                      )}
                      {demo.status !== 'Cancelled' && demo.status !== 'Completed' && (
                        <button className="dm-act-btn cancel" title="Cancel Demo"
                          onClick={() => handleCancel(demo.id)}>
                          <FiX />
                        </button>
                      )}
                      <button className="dm-act-btn del" title="Delete" onClick={() => handleDelete(demo.id)}>
                        <FiTrash2 />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        {/* Pagination */}
        <div className="dm-pagination-bar">
          <div className="dm-rows-select">
            Rows per page:
            <select value={rowsPerPage} onChange={e => { setRowsPerPage(Number(e.target.value)); setPage(1); }}>
              {ROWS_OPTIONS.map(r => <option key={r}>{r}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="dm-page-nums">
            <button className="dm-pg-btn" disabled={page === 1} onClick={() => setPage(p => p - 1)}>
              <FiChevronLeft size={13}/>
            </button>
            {pageNums().map((p, i) =>
              p === '...'
                ? <span key={i} style={{ padding: '0 4px', color: 'var(--text-light)' }}>…</span>
                : <button key={p} className={`dm-pg-btn ${page === p ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setPage(p)}>{p}</button>
            )}
            <button className="dm-pg-btn" disabled={page === totalPages} onClick={() => setPage(p => p + 1)}>
              <FiChevronRight size={13}/>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Modals */}
      {showSchedule && (
        <DemoFormModal
          allDemos={demos}
          onSave={handleAddSave}
          onClose={() => setShowSchedule(false)}
        />
      )}

      {editDemo && (
        <DemoFormModal
          initial={editDemo}
          allDemos={demos}
          onSave={handleEditSave}
          onClose={() => setEditDemo(null)}
        />
      )}

      {viewDemo && (
        <DemoViewModal
          demo={viewDemo}
          onClose={() => setViewDemo(null)}
          onEdit={(d) => setEditDemo(d)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default Demo;

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\pages\Districts\Districts.jsx =====
import React, { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { FiMapPin, FiRefreshCw, FiSave, FiCheck } from 'react-icons/fi';
import { superAdminService } from '../../services/superAdminService';
import '../SuperAdmin/SuperAdmin.css';
import './Districts.css';

const Districts = () => {
  const [districts, setDistricts] = useState([]);
  const [employees, setEmployees] = useState([]);
  const [selectedEmployee, setSelectedEmployee] = useState('');
  const [selectedDistricts, setSelectedDistricts] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true); setError('');
    try {
      const [d, e] = await Promise.all([superAdminService.getDistricts(), superAdminService.getEmployees()]);
      setDistricts(Array.isArray(d) ? d : []);
      setEmployees(Array.isArray(e) ? e.filter(x => !['SUPER_ADMIN','ADMIN'].includes(String(x.role || '').toUpperCase())) : []);
    } catch (err) { setError(err.message || 'Failed to load assignment data'); }
    finally { setLoading(false); }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  const selectEmployee = async (username) => {
    setSelectedEmployee(username); setMessage(''); setError('');
    if (!username) { setSelectedDistricts([]); return; }
    try {
      const data = await superAdminService.getTelecallerDistrictAssignments();
      const tc = data.find(x => x.username === username);
      setSelectedDistricts(tc?.districts || []);
    } catch (err) { setError(err.message || 'Failed to load telecaller districts'); }
  };

  const toggleDistrict = (name) => setSelectedDistricts(prev => prev.includes(name) ? prev.filter(x => x !== name) : [...prev, name]);

  const save = async () => {
    if (!selectedEmployee) { setError('Select a telecaller first.'); return; }
    setSaving(true); setError(''); setMessage('');
    try {
      await superAdminService.setTelecallerDistricts(selectedEmployee, selectedDistricts);
      setMessage('District assignment updated successfully.');
      await load();
    } catch (err) { setError(err.message || 'Failed to update district assignment'); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  };

  if (loading) return <div className="sa-loading">Loading district assignments…</div>;

  return (
    <div className="ad-dashboard">
      <div className="ad-card" style={{ marginBottom: 18 }}>
        <div className="card-header"><span className="card-title">District Assignment</span><button className="btn-outline" onClick={load}><FiRefreshCw /> Refresh</button></div>
        {error && <div className="sa-alert sa-alert-error">{error}</div>}
        {message && <div className="sa-alert sa-alert-success"><FiCheck /> {message}</div>}
        <div style={{ marginBottom: 18 }}>
          <label style={{ display: 'block', fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 8 }}>Telecaller</label>
          <select value={selectedEmployee} onChange={e => selectEmployee(e.target.value)} style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: 520, padding: 12, border: '1px solid #ddd', borderRadius: 10 }}>
            <option value="">Select Telecaller…</option>
            {employees.map(e => <option key={e.id || e.username} value={e.username}>{e.fullName || e.username} ({e.username})</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill,minmax(220px,1fr))', gap: 12 }}>
          {districts.map(d => { const checked = selectedDistricts.includes(d.name); return (
            <label key={d.id || d.name} style={{ border: checked ? '2px solid #7b1113' : '1px solid #e5e7eb', borderRadius: 12, padding: 16, cursor: 'pointer', background: checked ? '#fff7f7' : '#fff' }}>
              <input type="checkbox" checked={checked} onChange={() => toggleDistrict(d.name)} disabled={!selectedEmployee} />
              <span style={{ marginLeft: 10, fontWeight: 700 }}>{d.name}</span>
              <div style={{ marginLeft: 25, color: '#6b7280', fontSize: 12 }}>{Number(d.leadCount || 0).toLocaleString()} leads</div>
            </label>
          ); })}
        </div>
        <div style={{ marginTop: 20, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
          <button className="btn-primary" disabled={!selectedEmployee || saving} onClick={save}><FiSave /> {saving ? 'Saving…' : `Save Assignment (${selectedDistricts.length})`}</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Districts;

const TYPE_STYLE = {
  'Call Back':      { bg: '#e0f2fe', color: '#0369a1' },
  'Follow-Up Call': { bg: '#ede9fe', color: '#6d28d9' },
  'Demo':           { bg: '#fce7f3', color: '#9d174d' },
  'Meeting':        { bg: '#ecfdf5', color: '#065f46' },
  'Send Quotation': { bg: '#fef3c7', color: '#92400e' },
  'Send Email':     { bg: '#dbeafe', color: '#1e40af' },
  'Other':          { bg: '#f3f4f6', color: '#374151' },
};

const STATUS_STYLE = {
  'Pending':   { bg: '#fef9c3', color: '#92400e' },
  'Completed': { bg: '#dcfce7', color: '#15803d' },
  'Missed':    { bg: '#fee2e2', color: '#b91c1c' },
  'Cancelled': { bg: '#f1f5f9', color: '#64748b' },
};

const PRIORITY_STYLE = {
  'High':   { bg: '#fee2e2', color: '#b91c1c' },
  'Medium': { bg: '#fef3c7', color: '#b45309' },
  'Low':    { bg: '#dcfce7', color: '#15803d' },
};

const TYPES    = ['All Types', 'Call Back', 'Follow-Up Call', 'Demo', 'Meeting', 'Send Quotation', 'Send Email', 'Other'];
const STATUSES = ['All Status', 'Pending', 'Completed', 'Missed', 'Cancelled'];
const AGENTS   = ['All Agents', 'Arun Kumar', 'Priya Sharma'];
const PRIORITIES = ['All Priority', 'High', 'Medium', 'Low'];
const ROWS_OPTIONS = [5, 10, 15, 20];

const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];

// ─── Modal Component ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const Modal = ({ show, title, onClose, children, wide }) => {
  if (!show) return null;
  return (
    <div className="fu-modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className={`fu-modal-box${wide ? ' fu-modal-box-wide' : ''}`} onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="fu-modal-header">
          <span className="fu-modal-title">{title}</span>
          <button className="fu-modal-close" onClick={onClose}>✕</button>
        </div>
        <div className="fu-modal-body">{children}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// ─── Follow-Up Form ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const FollowUpForm = ({ initial, onSave, onClose }) => {
  const [form, setForm] = useState(initial || {
    leadName: '', mobile: '', email: '', campaign: 'Health Insurance',
    type: 'Call Back', date: '', time: '', note: '',
    priority: 'Medium', status: 'Pending',
  });
  const set = (k, v) => setForm(f => ({ ...f, [k]: v }));

  return (
    <div className="fu-lead-form">
      <div className="fu-form-row">
        <div className="fu-form-group">
          <label>Lead Name</label>
          <input type="text" value={form.leadName} onChange={e => set('leadName', e.target.value)} placeholder="Lead Name" />
        </div>
        <div className="fu-form-group">
          <label>Mobile</label>
          <input type="tel" value={form.mobile} onChange={e => set('mobile', e.target.value)} placeholder="+91 XXXXX XXXXX" />
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="fu-form-row">
        <div className="fu-form-group">
          <label>Email</label>
          <input type="email" value={form.email} onChange={e => set('email', e.target.value)} placeholder="email@example.com" />
        </div>
        <div className="fu-form-group">
          <label>Campaign</label>
          <select value={form.campaign} onChange={e => set('campaign', e.target.value)}>
            {['Health Insurance', 'Investment Plan', 'Mutual Fund', 'Term Insurance'].map(c => <option key={c}>{c}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="fu-form-row">
        <div className="fu-form-group">
          <label>Follow-Up Type</label>
          <select value={form.type} onChange={e => set('type', e.target.value)}>
            {TYPES.slice(1).map(t => <option key={t}>{t}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="fu-form-group">
          <label>Priority</label>
          <select value={form.priority} onChange={e => set('priority', e.target.value)}>
            {['High', 'Medium', 'Low'].map(p => <option key={p}>{p}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="fu-form-row">
        <div className="fu-form-group">
          <label>Date</label>
          <input type="date" value={form.date} onChange={e => set('date', e.target.value)} min={today} />
        </div>
        <div className="fu-form-group">
          <label>Time</label>
          <input type="time" value={form.time} onChange={e => set('time', e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      </div>
     
      <div className="fu-form-group">
        <label>Note</label>
        <textarea rows={3} value={form.note} onChange={e => set('note', e.target.value)} placeholder="Describe the follow-up…" />
      </div>
      <div className="fu-form-actions">
        <button className="fu-btn-outline" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
        <button className="fu-btn-primary" onClick={() => onSave(form)}>Save Follow-Up</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// ─── View Follow-Up Detail ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const ViewFollowUp = ({ fu, onClose, onCall }) => (
  <div className="fu-view-lead">
    <div className="fu-view-header">
      <div className="fu-view-id">{fu.id}</div>
      <span className="fu-status-pill" style={STATUS_STYLE[fu.status]}>{fu.status}</span>
    </div>
    {[
      ['Lead', fu.leadName],
      ['Lead ID', fu.leadId],
      ['Mobile', fu.mobile],
      ['Email', fu.email],
      ['Campaign', fu.campaign],
      ['Type', fu.type],
      ['Date', fu.date],
      ['Time', fu.time],
      ['Priority', fu.priority],
   
      ['Created On', fu.createdOn],
    ].map(([k, v]) => (
      <div key={k} className="fu-view-row">
        <span className="fu-view-key">{k}</span>
        <span className="fu-view-val">{v}</span>
      </div>
    ))}
    {fu.note && (
      <div className="fu-view-row" style={{ flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
        <span className="fu-view-key">Note</span>
        <span className="fu-view-val" style={{ fontWeight: 500, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>{fu.note}</span>
      </div>
    )}
    <div className="fu-form-actions" style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
      <button className="fu-btn-outline" onClick={onClose}>Close</button>
      <button className="fu-btn-primary" onClick={() => onCall(fu)}>
        <FiPhone style={{ marginRight: 6 }} /> Call Now
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
);

// ─── Toast ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const Toast = ({ msg, onClose }) => (
  <div className="fu-toast" onClick={onClose}>{msg}</div>
);

// ─── Main Component ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const FollowUps = ({ onNavigate }) => {
  const [followUps, setFollowUps]     = useState(INITIAL_FOLLOWUPS);
  const [search, setSearch]           = useState('');
  const [filterType, setFilterType]   = useState('All Types');
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState('All Status');
  
  const [filterPriority, setFilterPriority] = useState('All Priority');
  const [page, setPage]               = useState(1);
  const [rowsPerPage, setRowsPerPage] = useState(10);
  const [selected, setSelected]       = useState([]);
  const [showAdd, setShowAdd]         = useState(false);
  const [editFu, setEditFu]           = useState(null);
  const [viewFu, setViewFu]           = useState(null);
  const [toast, setToast]             = useState(null);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab]     = useState('All');

  const showToast = msg => {
    setToast(msg);
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 2500);
  };

  // ─── Stats ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const today_ = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
    return {
      total:     followUps.length,
      pending:   followUps.filter(f => f.status === 'Pending').length,
      today_:    followUps.filter(f => f.date === today_ && f.status === 'Pending').length,
      completed: followUps.filter(f => f.status === 'Completed').length,
      missed:    followUps.filter(f => f.status === 'Missed').length,
      overdue:   followUps.filter(f => f.date < today_ && f.status === 'Pending').length,
    };
  }, [followUps]);

  // ─── Filtering ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const today_ = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
    let data = [...followUps];

    // Tab filter
    if (activeTab === 'Today')    data = data.filter(f => f.date === today_);
    if (activeTab === 'Upcoming') data = data.filter(f => f.date > today_ && f.status === 'Pending');
    if (activeTab === 'Overdue')  data = data.filter(f => f.date < today_ && f.status === 'Pending');
    if (activeTab === 'Completed')data = data.filter(f => f.status === 'Completed');

    if (search)                           data = data.filter(f => f.leadName.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || f.mobile.includes(search) || f.id.includes(search));
    if (filterType !== 'All Types')       data = data.filter(f => f.type === filterType);
    if (filterStatus !== 'All Status')    data = data.filter(f => f.status === filterStatus);
    
    if (filterPriority !== 'All Priority')data = data.filter(f => f.priority === filterPriority);

    return data;
  }, [followUps, search, filterType, filterStatus, filterPriority, activeTab]);

  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / rowsPerPage));
  const paginated  = filtered.slice((page - 1) * rowsPerPage, page * rowsPerPage);

  // ─── Selection ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const allSelected   = paginated.length > 0 && paginated.every(f => selected.includes(f.id));
  const toggleAll     = () => allSelected ? setSelected(s => s.filter(id => !paginated.map(f => f.id).includes(id))) : setSelected(s => [...new Set([...s, ...paginated.map(f => f.id)])]);
  const toggleRow     = id => setSelected(s => s.includes(id) ? s.filter(x => x !== id) : [...s, id]);

  // ─── CRUD ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const handleAdd = form => {
    const newFu = {
      ...form,
      id: `FU-${1011 + followUps.length}`,
      leadId: `LD-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000) + 5000}`,
      createdOn: new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' }),
    };
    setFollowUps(prev => [newFu, ...prev]);
    setShowAdd(false);
    showToast('✅ Follow-up scheduled successfully!');
  };

  const handleEdit = form => {
    setFollowUps(prev => prev.map(f => f.id === editFu.id ? { ...f, ...form } : f));
    setEditFu(null);
    showToast('✏️ Follow-up updated successfully!');
  };

  const handleDelete = id => {
    setFollowUps(prev => prev.filter(f => f.id !== id));
    setSelected(s => s.filter(x => x !== id));
    showToast('🗑️ Follow-up deleted.');
  };

  const handleMarkComplete = id => {
    setFollowUps(prev => prev.map(f => f.id === id ? { ...f, status: 'Completed' } : f));
    showToast('✅ Marked as Completed!');
  };

  const handleMarkMissed = id => {
    setFollowUps(prev => prev.map(f => f.id === id ? { ...f, status: 'Missed' } : f));
    showToast('⚠️ Marked as Missed.');
  };

  const handleBulkDelete = () => {
    setFollowUps(prev => prev.filter(f => !selected.includes(f.id)));
    showToast(`🗑️ Deleted ${selected.length} follow-up(s).`);
    setSelected([]);
  };

  const handleBulkComplete = () => {
    setFollowUps(prev => prev.map(f => selected.includes(f.id) ? { ...f, status: 'Completed' } : f));
    showToast(`✅ Marked ${selected.length} as Completed.`);
    setSelected([]);
  };

  const handleCallNow = fu => {
    showToast(`📞 Calling ${fu.leadName}…`);
  };

  const fmtDate = d => {
    if (!d) return '--';
    const dt = new Date(d + 'T00:00:00');
    return dt.toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });
  };

  const fmtTime = t => {
    if (!t) return '--';
    const [h, m] = t.split(':');
    const hr = parseInt(h);
    return `${hr % 12 || 12}:${m} ${hr < 12 ? 'AM' : 'PM'}`;
  };

  const today_ = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
  const isOverdue  = fu => fu.date < today_ && fu.status === 'Pending';
  const isToday    = fu => fu.date === today_;

  // ─── Pagination ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const pgRange = () => {
    if (totalPages <= 5) return Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, i) => i + 1);
    if (page <= 3)       return [1, 2, 3, 4, '...', totalPages];
    if (page >= totalPages - 2) return [1, '...', totalPages - 3, totalPages - 2, totalPages - 1, totalPages];
    return [1, '...', page - 1, page, page + 1, '...', totalPages];
  };

  const TABS = [
    { key: 'All',       label: `All (${followUps.length})` },
    { key: 'Today',     label: `Today (${stats.today_})` },
    { key: 'Upcoming',  label: `Upcoming (${followUps.filter(f => f.date > today_ && f.status === 'Pending').length})` },
    { key: 'Overdue',   label: `Overdue (${stats.overdue})`, warn: stats.overdue > 0 },
    { key: 'Completed', label: `Completed (${stats.completed})` },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="fu-page">
      {toast && <Toast msg={toast} onClose={() => setToast(null)} />}

      {/* ─── Header ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div className="fu-page-header">
        <div>
          <h1 className="fu-page-title">Follow-Ups</h1>
          
        </div>
        <div className="fu-header-actions">
          <button className="fu-btn-outline-primary" onClick={() => showToast('📥 Exporting…')}>
            <FiDownload className="fu-btn-icon" /> Export
          </button>
          <button className="fu-btn-primary" onClick={() => setShowAdd(true)}>
            <FiPlus className="fu-btn-ico" /> Schedule Follow-Up
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ─── Stats Row ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div className="fu-stats-row">
        <div className="fu-stat-card">
          <div className="fu-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#ede9fe', color: '#7c3aed' }}><FiCalendar /></div>
          <div className="fu-stat-info">
            <div className="fu-stat-val">{stats.total}</div>
            <div className="fu-stat-label">Total Follow-Ups</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="fu-stat-card">
          <div className="fu-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#fef9c3', color: '#92400e' }}><FiClock /></div>
          <div className="fu-stat-info">
            <div className="fu-stat-val">{stats.pending}</div>
            <div className="fu-stat-label">Pending</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="fu-stat-card">
          <div className="fu-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#e0f2fe', color: '#0369a1' }}><FiBell /></div>
          <div className="fu-stat-info">
            <div className="fu-stat-val">{stats.today_}</div>
            <div className="fu-stat-label">Due Today</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="fu-stat-card">
          <div className="fu-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#dcfce7', color: '#15803d' }}><FiCheckCircle /></div>
          <div className="fu-stat-info">
            <div className="fu-stat-val">{stats.completed}</div>
            <div className="fu-stat-label">Completed</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="fu-stat-card">
          <div className="fu-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#fee2e2', color: '#b91c1c' }}><FiAlertCircle /></div>
          <div className="fu-stat-info">
            <div className="fu-stat-val">{stats.overdue}</div>
            <div className="fu-stat-label">Overdue</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="fu-stat-card">
          <div className="fu-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#fce7f3', color: '#9d174d' }}><FiRefreshCw /></div>
          <div className="fu-stat-info">
            <div className="fu-stat-val">{stats.missed}</div>
            <div className="fu-stat-label">Missed</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ─── Table Card ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div className="fu-table-card">

        {/* Tabs */}
        <div className="fu-tabs-row">
          {TABS.map(tab => (
            <button
              key={tab.key}
              className={`fu-tab${activeTab === tab.key ? ' fu-active' : ''}${tab.warn ? ' fu-tab-warn' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => { setActiveTab(tab.key); setPage(1); }}
            >
              {tab.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Filter bar */}
        <div className="fu-filter-bar">
          <div className="fu-search-wrap">
            <FiSearch className="fu-search-ico" />
            <input
              className="fu-search-input"
              placeholder="Search by name, mobile, ID…"
              value={search}
              onChange={e => { setSearch(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}
            />
          </div>
          <select className="fu-filter-select" value={filterType} onChange={e => { setFilterType(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}>
            {TYPES.map(t => <option key={t}>{t}</option>)}
          </select>
          <select className="fu-filter-select" value={filterStatus} onChange={e => { setFilterStatus(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}>
            {STATUSES.map(s => <option key={s}>{s}</option>)}
          </select>
          <select className="fu-filter-select" value={filterPriority} onChange={e => { setFilterPriority(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}>
            {PRIORITIES.map(p => <option key={p}>{p}</option>)}
          </select>
          
        </div>

        {/* Toolbar */}
        <div className="fu-table-toolbar">
          <span className="fu-total-count">
            Showing <strong>{paginated.length}</strong> of <strong>{filtered.length}</strong> follow-ups
            {selected.length > 0 && <span className="fu-selected-badge"> · {selected.length} selected</span>}
          </span>
          <div className="fu-toolbar-right">
            {selected.length > 0 && (
              <>
                <button className="fu-btn-outline-sm" onClick={handleBulkComplete}><FiCheck style={{marginRight:4}}/> Mark Completed</button>
                <button className="fu-btn-danger-sm" onClick={handleBulkDelete}><FiTrash2 style={{marginRight:4}}/> Delete</button>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Table */}
        <div className="fu-table-wrap">
          <table className="fu-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th style={{ width: '30px' }}><input type="checkbox" checked={allSelected} onChange={toggleAll} /></th>
                <th style={{ width: '100px' }}>Follow-Up ID</th>
                <th style={{ width: '140px' }}>Lead</th>
                <th style={{ width: '120px' }}>Type</th>
                <th style={{ width: '140px' }}>Date &amp; Time</th>
                <th style={{ width: '80px' }}>Priority</th>
                <th style={{ width: '100px' }}>Status</th>
                <th>Note</th>
                <th style={{ width: '200px' }}>Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {paginated.length === 0 ? (
                <tr><td colSpan={10} className="fu-empty-row">No follow-ups found matching your filters.</td></tr>
              ) : paginated.map(fu => (
                <tr key={fu.id} className={selected.includes(fu.id) ? 'fu-row-selected' : ''}>
                  <td><input type="checkbox" checked={selected.includes(fu.id)} onChange={() => toggleRow(fu.id)} /></td>
                  <td>
                    <div className="fu-lead-id">{fu.id}</div>
                    <div className="fu-lead-id fu-lead-id-sub">{fu.leadId}</div>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <div className="fu-lead-name">{fu.leadName}</div>
                    <div className="fu-lead-mobile">{fu.mobile}</div>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <span className="fu-type-badge" style={{ background: TYPE_STYLE[fu.type]?.bg, color: TYPE_STYLE[fu.type]?.color }}>
                      <span className="fu-type-icon">{TYPE_ICON[fu.type]}</span>
                      {fu.type}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <div className={`fu-date-val${isOverdue(fu) ? ' fu-overdue' : ''}${isToday(fu) ? ' fu-today' : ''}`}>
                      <FiCalendar style={{ marginRight: 4, verticalAlign: 'middle' }} />
                      {fmtDate(fu.date)}
                    </div>
                    <div className="fu-time-val">
                      <FiClock style={{ marginRight: 4, verticalAlign: 'middle' }} />
                      {fmtTime(fu.time)}
                    </div>
                    {isOverdue(fu) && <span className="fu-overdue-badge">Overdue</span>}
                    {isToday(fu)   && <span className="fu-today-badge">Today</span>}
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <span className="fu-status-pill" style={PRIORITY_STYLE[fu.priority]}>{fu.priority}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <span className="fu-status-pill" style={STATUS_STYLE[fu.status]}>{fu.status}</span>
                  </td>
                  
                  <td>
                    <div className="fu-note-cell">{fu.note || '--'}</div>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <div className="fu-action-group">
                      <button className="fu-act-btn" title="View" onClick={() => setViewFu(fu)}><FiEye /></button>
                      <button className="fu-act-btn" title="Edit" onClick={() => setEditFu(fu)}><FiEdit2 /></button>
                      <button className="fu-act-btn" title="Call Now" onClick={() => handleCallNow(fu)}><FiPhone /></button>
                      {fu.status === 'Pending' && (
                        <button className="fu-act-btn fu-act-complete" title="Mark Completed" onClick={() => handleMarkComplete(fu.id)}><FiCheck /></button>
                      )}
                      {fu.status === 'Pending' && (
                        <button className="fu-act-btn fu-act-missed" title="Mark Missed" onClick={() => handleMarkMissed(fu.id)}><FiX /></button>
                      )}
                      <button className="fu-act-btn fu-act-danger" title="Delete" onClick={() => handleDelete(fu.id)}><FiTrash2 /></button>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        {/* Pagination */}
        <div className="fu-pagination-bar">
          <div className="fu-rows-select">
            Rows per page:
            <select value={rowsPerPage} onChange={e => { setRowsPerPage(Number(e.target.value)); setPage(1); }}>
              {ROWS_OPTIONS.map(n => <option key={n}>{n}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="fu-page-nums">
            <button className="fu-pg-btn" onClick={() => setPage(p => p - 1)} disabled={page === 1}><FiChevronLeft /></button>
            {pgRange().map((p, i) =>
              p === '...'
                ? <span key={`dots-${i}`} className="fu-pg-dots">…</span>
                : <button key={p} className={`fu-pg-btn${page === p ? ' fu-active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setPage(p)}>{p}</button>
            )}
            <button className="fu-pg-btn" onClick={() => setPage(p => p + 1)} disabled={page === totalPages}><FiChevronRight /></button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="fu-footer">
        <span>© 2024 CRM Call Center. All rights reserved.</span>
        <span>Version 1.0.0</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default FollowUps;

import React from "react";
import "./Help.css";

const Help = () => {
  return (
    <div>
      <h1>Help & Support</h1>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Help;

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\pages\Leads\Leads.jsx =====
import React, { useState, useMemo, useRef, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import * as XLSX from 'xlsx';

  FaUsers, FaUserPlus, FaUserCheck, FaUserTimes, FaCheckCircle, FaThumbsUp,
  FaUpload, FaPlus, FaDownload, FaEdit, FaEye, FaTrash, FaSearch,
  FaExchangeAlt, FaHistory, FaArrowLeft, FaMapMarkerAlt,

const STATUSES = ['New', 'Contacted', 'Interested', 'Not Interested', 'Follow-up', 'Converted', 'No Response'];
const SOURCES = ['Web', 'Excel', 'Manual', 'Referral'];

  'New': { bg: '#e0f2fe', color: '#0369a1' },
  'Contacted': { bg: '#fef9c3', color: '#92400e' },

  'Follow-up': { bg: '#fef3c7', color: '#b45309' },

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/*  Dynamic Excel column engine                                       */
/*  ---------------------------------------------------------------   */
/*  The application never hard-codes any Excel columns. Whatever      */
/*  headings/columns are present in an uploaded file — including      */
/*  files with multi-row / merged-cell headers (e.g. a group header   */
/*  like "TELECALLING UPDATES" sitting above "UP1, UP2, UP3…") — are  */
/*  detected automatically and become the schema used to render,      */
/*  edit, export and search leads.                                    */
/*                                                                      */
/*  IMPORTANT: the schema — including COLUMN ORDER — is stored         */
/*  PER DISTRICT (not globally). Every time a file is uploaded for a  */
/*  district, that file's own column order becomes the new source of  */
/*  truth for that district, overwriting whatever was stored before.  */
/*  This means adding/renaming/reordering columns in a later upload   */
/*  is picked up automatically with zero code changes.                 */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

const SCHEMA_KEY_PREFIX = 'crm_lead_columns_schema';
const LOCAL_HISTORY_KEY = 'crm_upload_history';

// Column keys reserved by the CRM itself (never treated as "data columns")
const META_KEYS = new Set([
  'id', 'leadId', 'status', 'source', 'assignedTo', 'district', 'area',
  'campaign', 'uploadBatch', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt', 'notes',
]);

const NAME_HINTS = ['name', 'organization', 'school', 'company', 'student', 'contact person', 'institution'];
const MOBILE_HINTS = ['mobile', 'phone', 'contact no', 'contact number', 'whatsapp', 'ph no', 'tel', 'contact'];

// Schema is now keyed per-district so different districts (different
// uploaded files) never clobber each other's column order.
const getStoredSchema = (district) => {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(`${SCHEMA_KEY_PREFIX}:${district}`);
    const parsed = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : null;
  } catch { return null; }
};

const setStoredSchema = (district, columns) => {
  try {
    // Store ONLY the uploaded Excel headers, in their original order.
    // Remove blank/repeated headers so legacy/duplicate columns cannot return.
    const seen = new Set();
    const cleanColumns = (Array.isArray(columns) ? columns : [])
      .map(c => String(c ?? '').trim())
      .filter(c => {
        const key = c.toLowerCase();
        if (!c || seen.has(key)) return false;
        seen.add(key);
        return true;
      });

    localStorage.setItem(`${SCHEMA_KEY_PREFIX}:${district}`, JSON.stringify(cleanColumns));
  } catch { /* ignore */ }
};

const recordLocalUploadHistory = (district, entry) => {
  try {
    const all = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LOCAL_HISTORY_KEY) || '[]');
    all.unshift({ ...entry, district });
    localStorage.setItem(LOCAL_HISTORY_KEY, JSON.stringify(all.slice(0, 300)));
  } catch { /* ignore */ }
};

const getLocalUploadHistory = (district) => {
  try {
    const all = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LOCAL_HISTORY_KEY) || '[]');
    return all.filter(h => h.district === district);
  } catch { return []; }
};

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*  Header detection — merge-aware, multi-row capable                */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- */

// Reads the top of a worksheet into a plain string grid, then "fills"
// every cell that belongs to a merged range with that range's own value
// (SheetJS only stores the value on the merge's top-left cell). This
// lets us reconstruct group headers such as:
//   Row1: "TELECALLING UPDATES" merged across 11 columns
//   Row3: "UP1" | "UP2" | "UP3" | … (one real sub-header per column)
// without ever hard-coding a column name or position.
const buildFilledHeaderGrid = (ws, maxScanRows = 20) => {
  if (!ws || !ws['!ref']) return { grid: [], merges: [], nCols: 0, nRows: 0 };
  const range = XLSX.utils.decode_range(ws['!ref']);
  const nRows = Math.min(maxScanRows, range.e.r + 1);
  const nCols = range.e.c + 1;
  const grid = [];
  for (let r = 0; r < nRows; r++) {
    const row = [];
    for (let c = 0; c <= range.e.c; c++) {
      const cell = ws[XLSX.utils.encode_cell({ r, c })];
      const v = cell && cell.v !== undefined && cell.v !== null
        ? String(cell.v).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
        : '';
      row.push(v);
    }
    grid.push(row);
  }
  const merges = (ws['!merges'] || []).filter(m => m.s.r < nRows);
  merges.forEach(m => {
    const topVal = grid[m.s.r] ? grid[m.s.r][m.s.c] : '';
    for (let r = m.s.r; r <= Math.min(m.e.r, nRows - 1); r++) {
      for (let c = m.s.c; c <= m.e.c; c++) {
        if (!grid[r][c]) grid[r][c] = topVal;
      }
    }
  });
  return { grid, merges, nCols, nRows };
};

// Finds the header block: an "anchor" row (the row with the most filled
// cells within the first 15 rows — almost always the main header row),
// then extends downward only as far as the file's own merged cells say
// the header actually spans (e.g. a 3-row-tall header). This is driven
// entirely by the workbook's own formatting, so it never mistakes a
// short data row or a section-divider row for part of the header.
const detectHeaderBlock = (grid, merges) => {
  let anchor = 0, bestCount = -1;
  const scanLimit = Math.min(grid.length, 15);
  for (let r = 0; r < scanLimit; r++) {
    const count = grid[r].filter(v => v !== '').length;
    if (count > bestCount) { bestCount = count; anchor = r; }
  }
  let blockEnd = anchor;
  merges.forEach(m => {
    if (m.s.r <= anchor && m.e.r >= anchor && m.e.r > blockEnd) {
      blockEnd = Math.min(m.e.r, grid.length - 1);
    }
  });
  return { anchor, blockEnd };
};

// Turns a raw header row into clean, unique column names, exactly as found
// in the file (blank/duplicate headers are safely auto-labeled).
const normalizeColumns = (headerRow) => {
  const seen = {};
  return (headerRow || []).map((h, i) => {
    let name = (h === null || h === undefined) ? '' : String(h).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    if (!name) name = `Column ${i + 1}`;
    if (seen[name] != null) {
      seen[name] += 1;
      name = `${name} (${seen[name]})`;
    } else {
      seen[name] = 0;
    }
    return name;
  });
};

// For every column, walks the header block bottom-up and takes the
// deepest (most specific) non-empty label — e.g. "UP1" rather than the
// group label "TELECALLING UPDATES" above it — falling back to the
// nearest ancestor label when a column has no sub-header of its own.
// Columns are returned in their exact left-to-right order from the file.
const buildLeafColumns = (grid, anchor, blockEnd, nCols) => {
  const raw = [];
  for (let c = 0; c < nCols; c++) {
    let leaf = '';
    for (let r = blockEnd; r >= anchor; r--) {
      if (grid[r] && grid[r][c] && grid[r][c].trim()) {
        leaf = grid[r][c];
        break;
      }
    }
    raw.push(leaf || `Column ${c + 1}`);
  }
  return normalizeColumns(raw);
};

// Drops only the columns that have BOTH no real header text (i.e. they
// fell back to "Column N") AND no data in any row — these are genuinely
// blank spacer columns in the sheet. Any column with a real heading, or
// any column that actually carries data (even without a heading), is
// always kept.
// NOTE: This function is no longer used in the main flow to preserve all
// detected columns, including empty fallbacks. Keeping it for reference.
const trimBlankFallbackColumns = (columns, records) => {
  const keep = columns.filter((col) => {
    if (!/^Column \d+$/.test(col)) return true;
    return records.some(rec => (rec[col] || '').toString().trim() !== '');
  });
  if (keep.length === columns.length) return { columns, records };
  const keepSet = new Set(keep);
  const trimmedRecords = records.map(rec => {
    const r = {};
    keep.forEach(c => { r[c] = rec[c]; });
    return r;
  });
  return { columns: keep, records: trimmedRecords };
};

const sheetRowsToRecords = (rows, headerIdx, columns) => {
  const records = [];
  for (let i = headerIdx + 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
    const row = rows[i] || [];
    const hasData = row.some(c => c !== null && c !== undefined && String(c).trim() !== '');
    if (!hasData) continue;
    const rec = {};
    columns.forEach((col, ci) => {
      let v = row[ci];
      if (v === null || v === undefined) v = '';
      rec[col] = String(v).trim();
    });
    records.push(rec);
  }
  return records;
};

// End-to-end: given a workbook + a sheet name, returns the dynamically
// detected columns — IN THE EXACT ORDER THEY APPEAR IN THE FILE — and the
// row records. Nothing about the shape of the sheet is assumed in advance:
// single row headers, multi-row headers, and merged group headers all
// work, and whatever order the file's own columns are in is preserved
// end-to-end (parsing → storage → table → export → forms).
const parseWorkbookSheet = (wb, sheetName) => {
  const ws = wb.Sheets[sheetName];
  if (!ws || !ws['!ref']) return { error: 'This sheet is empty.' };

  const { grid, merges, nCols, nRows } = buildFilledHeaderGrid(ws, 20);
  if (nRows === 0 || nCols === 0) return { error: 'This sheet is empty.' };

  const { anchor, blockEnd } = detectHeaderBlock(grid, merges);
  const columns = buildLeafColumns(grid, anchor, blockEnd, nCols);

  const allRows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws, { header: 1, defval: '', raw: false });
  let records = sheetRowsToRecords(allRows, blockEnd, columns);

  if (records.length === 0) {
    return { error: 'No data rows were found below the detected header row(s).' };
  }

  // IMPORTANT: Do NOT trim blank fallback columns and do NOT reorder them.
  // Keep every column exactly as detected, in exactly the order the file
  // presents them, even if a column currently has no data.
  // const trimmed = trimBlankFallbackColumns(columns, records);
  // return { columns: trimmed.columns, records: trimmed.records };

  return { columns, records };
};

const guessColumn = (columns, hints) => {
  const lower = columns.map(c => c.toLowerCase());
  for (const hint of hints) {
    const idx = lower.findIndex(c => c.includes(hint));
    if (idx !== -1) return columns[idx];
  }
  return null;
};

const cleanPhone = (val) => {
  if (!val) return '';
  const s = String(val);
  const m = s.match(/[0-9][0-9\s\-/]{6,}[0-9]/);
  const token = m ? m[0] : s;
  return token.replace(/[\s\-]/g, '').split('/')[0].trim();
};

// ─── Telecaller "UP" updates (UP1, UP2, …) ───────────────────────────────────
// The telecaller Call Panel saves every call update as UPn inside the lead's
// notes JSON. These helpers make sure every UPn - even UP6, UP7… that was not in
// the uploaded Excel - shows up as a column in the district table.
const UP_KEY_RE = /^up\s*[-_ ]?\s*(\d{1,4})$/i;
const upNumber = (key) => {
  const m = String(key ?? '').trim().match(UP_KEY_RE);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : null;
};
const hasValue = (v) => v !== null && v !== undefined && String(v).trim() !== '';

// Recovers the dynamic field values for a lead, whether they were stored
// as a JSON record inside `notes`, or returned directly as extra keys on
// the lead object by the backend.
const getLeadRecord = (lead) => {
  let record = {};

  // The Excel row is stored as JSON in notes during import.
  // Read ONLY that JSON object for dynamic Excel fields.
  // Do not merge backend fields such as name, mobile, email, status,
  // district, etc. into the Excel record because that creates duplicate
  // or unexpected columns in the table.
  if (lead?.notes) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(lead.notes);
      if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        record = { ...parsed };
      }
    } catch {
      // notes is not JSON; leave the Excel record empty.
    }
  }

  // Only UPn values are filled in from other places, and only when the notes JSON
  // does not already have that UPn (notes JSON > excelData > lead.up1..up5).
  if (Object.keys(record).length > 0) {
    const extra = {};
    const collect = (key, value) => {
      const n = upNumber(key);
      if (n !== null && hasValue(value) && !hasValue(extra[n])) extra[n] = String(value).trim();
    };
    if (lead?.excelData && typeof lead.excelData === 'object') {
      Object.entries(lead.excelData).forEach(([k, v]) => collect(k, v));
    }
    [1, 2, 3, 4, 5].forEach((n) => collect(`up${n}`, lead?.[`up${n}`]));

    Object.entries(extra).forEach(([num, value]) => {
      const n = Number(num);
      const existingKey = Object.keys(record).find((k) => upNumber(k) === n);
      if (existingKey === undefined) record[`UP${n}`] = value;
      else if (!hasValue(record[existingKey])) record[existingKey] = value;
    });
  }

  return record;
};

// Read one cell. UP columns match by number ("UP1" = "up1" = "UP 1").
const getCellValue = (record, column) => {
  const n = upNumber(column);
  if (n === null) return record[column];
  const key = Object.keys(record).find((k) => upNumber(k) === n);
  return key === undefined ? undefined : record[key];
};

// Adds a column for every UPn found in the leads that the Excel schema does not have yet.
// New UP columns are placed right after the last existing UP column (or at the end).
const withUpdateColumns = (baseColumns, leads) => {
  const have = new Set(baseColumns.map((c) => upNumber(c)).filter((n) => n !== null));
  const missing = new Set();
  (leads || []).forEach((lead) => {
    Object.keys(getLeadRecord(lead)).forEach((key) => {
      const n = upNumber(key);
      if (n !== null && !have.has(n) && hasValue(getCellValue(getLeadRecord(lead), key))) missing.add(n);
    });
  });
  if (missing.size === 0) return baseColumns;

  const extra = [...missing].sort((x, y) => x - y).map((n) => `UP${n}`);
  let lastUp = -1;
  baseColumns.forEach((c, i) => { if (upNumber(c) !== null) lastUp = i; });
  if (lastUp === -1) return [...baseColumns, ...extra];
  return [...baseColumns.slice(0, lastUp + 1), ...extra, ...baseColumns.slice(lastUp + 1)];
};

// Columns created by saving a call update (not part of the uploaded Excel) always go LAST:
//   Excel columns  ->  Legacy Note / TC_NEXT_FOLLOWUP_NOTE  ->  UP1, UP2, UP3 ...
const isCallNoteColumn = (name) =>
  /^(legacy\s*note|tc[_\s-]*next[_\s-]*(re)?followup[_\s-]*note)$/i.test(String(name ?? '').trim());

const updateColumnsLast = (columns) => {
  const base = [];
  const notes = [];
  const ups = [];
  (columns || []).forEach((c) => {
    if (upNumber(c) !== null) ups.push(c);
    else if (isCallNoteColumn(c)) notes.push(c);
    else base.push(c);
  });
  ups.sort((a, b) => upNumber(a) - upNumber(b));
  return [...base, ...notes, ...ups];
};

// The per-district column derivation now happens inside DistrictView.
// We no longer rely on a global schema for the column list — see
// getStoredSchema()/setStoredSchema() above, and the `columns` useMemo
// inside DistrictView below.
const getCurrentUserName = () => {
  try {
    const u = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('user') || '{}');
    return u.username || u.fullName || 'Super Admin';
  } catch { return 'Super Admin'; }
};

const Modal = ({ show, title, onClose, children, wide }) => {
  if (!show) return null;
  return (
    <div className="modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className={`modal-box ${wide ? 'modal-wide' : ''}`} onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="modal-header">
          <span className="modal-title">{title}</span>
          <button className="modal-close" onClick={onClose}><FaTimes size={14} /></button>
        </div>
        <div className="modal-body">{children}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

      <div className="kpi-value">{value}</div>

    </div>
  </div>

const DistrictCard = ({ district, onClick }) => (
  <button
    className="district-card"
    onClick={onClick}
    title={`${district.leadCount || 0} leads · ${district.assignedByName ? `Assigned to ${district.assignedByName}` : 'Not assigned'}`}
  >
    <div className="district-card-icon"><FaMapMarkerAlt size={18} /></div>
    <div className="district-card-name">{district.name}</div>
    <span className={`district-card-badge ${district.assignedByName ? 'badge-assigned' : 'badge-unassigned'}`}>

    </span>
  </button>
