};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  EDIT PAYMENT MODAL (Super Admin — org + amount corrections)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const EditPaymentModal = ({ payment, onClose, onSave }) => {
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ gstPercent: 0, discountAmount: 0, paymentMethod: '', transactionReferenceNumber: '', ...payment });
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const set = (k, v) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [k]: v }));

  const handleSave = async () => {
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      await onSave(payment.id, {
        organizationName: form.organizationName,
        address: form.address,
        phone: form.phone,
        email: form.email,
        paymentType: form.paymentType,
        totalAmount: Number(form.totalAmount) || 0,
        amountPaid: Number(form.amountPaid) || 0,
        gstPercent: Number(form.gstPercent) || 0,
        discountAmount: Number(form.discountAmount) || 0,
        paymentMethod: form.paymentMethod || '',
        transactionReferenceNumber: form.transactionReferenceNumber || '',
        paymentDate: form.paymentDate,
        notes: form.notes,
      });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="pay-modal-overlay" onClick={(e) => e.target === e.currentTarget && onClose()}>
      <div className="pay-modal-box pay-modal-lg">
        <div className="pay-modal-header">
          <span className="pay-modal-title"><FiEdit2 size={16} style={{ marginRight: 6 }} /> Edit Payment</span>
          <button className="pay-modal-close" onClick={onClose}><FiX /></button>
        </div>
        <div className="pay-modal-body">
          <div className="pay-add-grid">
            <div className="pay-form-group">
              <label className="pay-form-label">Organization Name</label>
              <input className="pay-form-input" value={form.organizationName || ''} onChange={(e) => set('organizationName', e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="pay-form-group">
              <label className="pay-form-label">Address</label>
              <input className="pay-form-input" value={form.address || ''} onChange={(e) => set('address', e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="pay-form-group">
              <label className="pay-form-label">Phone Number</label>
              <input className="pay-form-input" value={form.phone || ''} onChange={(e) => set('phone', e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="pay-form-group">
              <label className="pay-form-label">Email Address</label>
              <input className="pay-form-input" value={form.email || ''} onChange={(e) => set('email', e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="pay-form-group">
              <label className="pay-form-label">Payment Type</label>
              <select className="pay-form-select" value={form.paymentType} onChange={(e) => set('paymentType', e.target.value)}>
                {['Full', 'Partial', 'Advance'].map((t) => <option key={t}>{t}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div className="pay-form-group">
              <label className="pay-form-label">Payment Date</label>
              <input className="pay-form-input" type="date" value={form.paymentDate || ''} onChange={(e) => set('paymentDate', e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="pay-form-group">
              <label className="pay-form-label">Total Amount</label>
              <input className="pay-form-input" type="number" value={form.totalAmount ?? ''} onChange={(e) => set('totalAmount', e.target.value)} />
              {payment.status === 'Fully Paid' && (
                <span className="pay-form-hint">This client is Fully Paid. Raise the total amount to reopen the balance and allow more payment records — for the telecaller too.</span>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="pay-form-group">
              <label className="pay-form-label">GST (%)</label>
              <input className="pay-form-input" type="number" min="0" max="100" step="0.01" value={form.gstPercent ?? ''} onChange={(e) => set('gstPercent', e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="pay-form-group">
              <label className="pay-form-label">Discount</label>
              <input className="pay-form-input" type="number" min="0" step="0.01" value={form.discountAmount ?? ''} onChange={(e) => set('discountAmount', e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="pay-form-group">
              <label className="pay-form-label">Payment Method</label>
              <input className="pay-form-input" value={form.paymentMethod || ''} onChange={(e) => set('paymentMethod', e.target.value)} placeholder="UPI / Cash / Bank Transfer" />
            </div>
            <div className="pay-form-group">
              <label className="pay-form-label">Transaction / Reference No.</label>
              <input className="pay-form-input" value={form.transactionReferenceNumber || ''} onChange={(e) => set('transactionReferenceNumber', e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="pay-form-group">
              <label className="pay-form-label">Amount Paid</label>
              <input className="pay-form-input" type="number" value={form.amountPaid ?? ''} onChange={(e) => set('amountPaid', e.target.value)} />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="pay-form-group">
            <label className="pay-form-label">Notes</label>
            <textarea className="pay-form-textarea" value={form.notes || ''} onChange={(e) => set('notes', e.target.value)} rows={3} />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="pay-form-actions" style={{ padding: '14px 24px 24px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
          <button className="pay-btn-outline" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
          <button className="pay-btn-primary" disabled={saving} onClick={handleSave}>
            <FiCheckCircle size={13} /> {saving ? 'Saving…' : 'Save Changes'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  ADD PAYMENT RECORD MODAL (Super Admin)
//  Shows the client's previous payment info before a new record is added,
//  per the "Payment History and Remaining Balance" requirement.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const PaymentRecordModal = ({ payment, onClose, onSave }) => {
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ paymentAmount: '', paymentType: 'Partial', paymentDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), paymentMethod: 'UPI', transactionReferenceNumber: '', notes: '', status: 'Paid' });
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const set = (k, v) => setForm((x) => ({ ...x, [k]: v }));

  const history = payment.paymentHistory || [];
  const lastRecord = history.length ? history[history.length - 1] : null;

  const save = async () => {
    if (!(Number(form.paymentAmount) > 0)) return alert('Enter a valid payment amount');
    if (Number(form.paymentAmount) > Number(payment.balanceAmount || 0) && form.status !== 'Pending') return alert('Amount cannot exceed remaining balance');
    setSaving(true);
    try { await onSave({ ...form, paymentAmount: Number(form.paymentAmount) }); onClose(); } finally { setSaving(false); }
  };

  return (
    <div className="pay-modal-overlay" onClick={(e) => e.target === e.currentTarget && onClose()}>
      <div className="pay-modal-box pay-modal-lg">
        <div className="pay-modal-header">
          <span className="pay-modal-title"><FiEdit2 size={16} style={{ marginRight: 6 }} /> Add Payment Record — {payment.organizationName}</span>
          <button className="pay-modal-close" onClick={onClose}><FiX /></button>
        </div>
        <div className="pay-modal-body">
          <div className="pay-view-note-label">Previous Payment Information</div>
          <div className="pay-amount-summary" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
            <div className="pay-summary-item">
              <span className="pay-summary-label">Total Amount</span>
              <span className="pay-summary-value">{fmtINR(payment.totalAmount)}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="pay-summary-item">
              <span className="pay-summary-label">Total Paid So Far</span>
              <span className="pay-summary-value pay-summary-paid">{fmtINR(payment.amountPaid)}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="pay-summary-item">
              <span className="pay-summary-label">Remaining Balance</span>
              <span className={`pay-summary-value ${payment.balanceAmount > 0 ? 'pay-summary-balance' : ''}`}>{fmtINR(payment.balanceAmount)}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          {lastRecord && (
            <div className="pay-amount-summary" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
              <div className="pay-summary-item">
                <span className="pay-summary-label">Previous Payment Amount</span>
                <span className="pay-summary-value">{fmtINR(lastRecord.amount)}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="pay-summary-item">
                <span className="pay-summary-label">Previous Payment Date</span>
                <span className="pay-summary-value">{lastRecord.paymentDate || '--'}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="pay-summary-item">
                <span className="pay-summary-label">Previous Payment Type</span>
                <span className="pay-summary-value">{lastRecord.paymentType || '--'}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
          {history.length > 0 && (
            <div style={{ marginBottom: 18 }}>
              <div className="pay-view-note-label">Payment History</div>
              <table className="pay-table">
                <thead><tr><th>Amount</th><th>Type</th><th>Date</th><th>Method</th><th>Reference</th><th>Status</th></tr></thead>
                <tbody>
                  {history.map((r, i) => (
                    <tr key={r.id || i}>
                      <td>{fmtINR(r.amount)}</td><td>{r.paymentType}</td><td>{r.paymentDate || '--'}</td>
                      <td>{r.paymentMethod || '--'}</td><td>{r.transactionReferenceNumber || '--'}</td><td>{r.status || 'Paid'}</td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="pay-view-note-label">New Payment</div>
          <div className="pay-add-grid">
            <div className="pay-form-group"><label className="pay-form-label">Payment Amount *</label><input className="pay-form-input" type="number" value={form.paymentAmount} onChange={(e) => set('paymentAmount', e.target.value)} /></div>
            <div className="pay-form-group"><label className="pay-form-label">Payment Type</label><select className="pay-form-select" value={form.paymentType} onChange={(e) => set('paymentType', e.target.value)}><option>Advance</option><option>Partial</option><option>Full</option></select></div>
            <div className="pay-form-group"><label className="pay-form-label">Payment Date</label><input className="pay-form-input" type="date" value={form.paymentDate} onChange={(e) => set('paymentDate', e.target.value)} /></div>
            <div className="pay-form-group"><label className="pay-form-label">Payment Method</label><input className="pay-form-input" value={form.paymentMethod} onChange={(e) => set('paymentMethod', e.target.value)} placeholder="UPI / Cash / Bank Transfer" /></div>
            <div className="pay-form-group"><label className="pay-form-label">Transaction / Reference No.</label><input className="pay-form-input" value={form.transactionReferenceNumber} onChange={(e) => set('transactionReferenceNumber', e.target.value)} /></div>
            <div className="pay-form-group"><label className="pay-form-label">Status</label><select className="pay-form-select" value={form.status} onChange={(e) => set('status', e.target.value)}><option>Paid</option><option>Pending</option></select></div>
          </div>
          <div className="pay-form-group"><label className="pay-form-label">Payment Notes</label><textarea className="pay-form-textarea" value={form.notes} onChange={(e) => set('notes', e.target.value)} /></div>
        </div>
        <div className="pay-form-actions" style={{ padding: '14px 24px 24px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
          <button className="pay-btn-outline" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
          <button className="pay-btn-primary" disabled={saving} onClick={save}>{saving ? 'Saving…' : 'Save'}</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  VIEW / VERIFY PAYMENT MODAL
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const ViewPaymentModal = ({ payment, onClose, onVerify, onGenerateInvoice, onGenerateRecordInvoice, onSendToTelecaller, onDownloadInvoice, onDownloadRecordInvoice, onAddRecord }) => {
  const pct = payment.totalAmount > 0 ? Math.round((payment.amountPaid / payment.totalAmount) * 100) : 0;
  const progressColor = pct === 100 ? '#15803d' : pct > 0 ? '#d97706' : '#b91c1c';

  const infoRows = [
    ['Organization', payment.organizationName],
    ['Address', payment.address || '--'],
    ['Phone', payment.phone || '--'],
    ['Email', payment.email || '--'],
    ['Payment Type', payment.paymentType],
    ['Payment Date', payment.paymentDate || '--'],
    ['Telecaller', payment.telecallerName || '--'],
    ['Status', <StatusBadge key="s" status={payment.status} />],
  ];

  return (
    <div className="pay-modal-overlay" onClick={(e) => e.target === e.currentTarget && onClose()}>
      <div className="pay-modal-box pay-modal-lg">
        <div className="pay-modal-header">
          <span className="pay-modal-title"><FiCreditCard size={16} style={{ marginRight: 6 }} /> Payment Verification</span>
          <button className="pay-modal-close" onClick={onClose}><FiX /></button>
        </div>

        <div className="pay-view-hero">
          <div className="pay-view-avatar" style={{ background: avatarColor(payment.organizationName) }}>
            {initials(payment.organizationName)}
          </div>
          <div className="pay-view-hero-info">
            <div className="pay-view-client-name">{payment.organizationName}</div>
            <div className="pay-view-company">{payment.address}</div>
          </div>
          <StatusBadge status={payment.status} />
        </div>

        <div className="pay-amount-summary">
          {[
            { label: 'Total Amount', val: fmtINR(payment.totalAmount), cls: '' },
            { label: 'Amount Paid', val: fmtINR(payment.amountPaid), cls: 'pay-summary-paid' },
            { label: 'Balance Due', val: fmtINR(payment.balanceAmount), cls: payment.balanceAmount > 0 ? 'pay-summary-balance' : '' },
          ].map((c) => (
            <div className="pay-summary-item" key={c.label}>
              <span className="pay-summary-label">{c.label}</span>
              <span className={`pay-summary-value ${c.cls}`}>{c.val}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="pay-progress-wrap">
          <div className="pay-progress-label">
            <span>Payment Progress</span>
            <span style={{ color: progressColor }}>{pct}% Paid</span>
          </div>
          <div className="pay-progress-track">
            <div className="pay-progress-fill" style={{ width: `${pct}%`, background: progressColor }} />
          </div>
        </div>

        {Number(payment.balanceAmount) > 0 && payment.status !== 'Fully Paid' && <button className="pay-btn-primary" style={{margin:'0 24px 14px'}} onClick={onAddRecord}><FiEdit2 size={13}/> Add Payment Record</button>}
        {payment.paymentHistory?.length > 0 && <div style={{padding:'0 24px 14px'}}><div className="pay-view-note-label">Payment History — each transaction has its own invoice</div><table className="pay-table"><thead><tr><th>Amount</th><th>Type</th><th>Date</th><th>Method</th><th>Reference</th><th>Status</th><th>Invoice</th></tr></thead><tbody>{payment.paymentHistory.map((r,i)=><tr key={r.id||i}><td>{fmtINR(r.amount)}</td><td>{r.paymentType}</td><td>{r.paymentDate||'--'}</td><td>{r.paymentMethod||'--'}</td><td>{r.transactionReferenceNumber||'--'}</td><td>{r.status||'Paid'}</td><td>{r.invoiceId ? <button className="pay-btn-outline" onClick={()=>onDownloadRecordInvoice(payment,r)}><FiDownload size={13}/> Download Invoice</button> : <button className="pay-btn-primary" disabled={payment.status!=='Verified' && payment.status!=='Invoice Generated' && payment.status!=='Fully Paid'} onClick={()=>onGenerateRecordInvoice(payment,r)}><FiFileText size={13}/> Generate Invoice</button>}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div>}
        <div className="pay-view-section">
  <div className="pay-section-heading">
    <div>
      <div className="pay-section-title">Customer Details</div>
      <div className="pay-section-subtitle">
        Organization and payment information
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div className="pay-view-grid">
    {infoRows.map(([k, v]) => (
      <div className="pay-view-row" key={k}>
        <div className="pay-view-key">{k}</div>
        <div className="pay-view-val">{v}</div>
      </div>
    ))}
  </div>
</div>

        {payment.notes && (
          <div className="pay-view-note">
            <div className="pay-view-note-label"><FiInfo size={12} /> Notes</div>
            <div className="pay-view-note-text">{payment.notes}</div>
          </div>
        )}

        {payment.timeline?.length > 0 && (
          <div className="pay-timeline">
            <div className="pay-timeline-title"><FiClock size={14} style={{ marginRight: 6 }} /> Activity Timeline</div>
            <div className="pay-timeline-list">
              {payment.timeline.map((t, i) => (
                <div className="pay-timeline-item" key={i}>
                  <div className="pay-timeline-left">
                    <div className="pay-timeline-dot" style={{ background: t.bg, color: t.color }}>{getTimelineIcon(t.icon)}</div>
                    <div className="pay-timeline-line" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="pay-timeline-content">
                    <div className="pay-timeline-action">{t.action}</div>
                    <div className="pay-timeline-time">{t.time}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="pay-form-actions" style={{ padding: '14px 24px 24px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
          <button className="pay-btn-outline" onClick={onClose}>Close</button>
          {payment.status === 'Recorded' && (
            <button className="pay-btn-primary" onClick={onVerify}><FiShield size={13} /> Verify Payment</button>
          )}
          {(payment.status === 'Verified' || payment.status === 'Invoice Generated') && (
            <button className="pay-btn-primary" onClick={onGenerateInvoice}>
              <FiFileText size={13} /> Generate Next Transaction Invoice
            </button>
          )}
          {payment.invoiceId && (
            <button className="pay-btn-outline" onClick={() => onDownloadInvoice(payment)}>
              <FiDownload size={13} /> Download Invoice
            </button>
          )}
          {payment.status === 'Invoice Generated' && (
            <button className="pay-btn-primary" onClick={onSendToTelecaller}>
              <FiSend size={13} /> Send Invoice to Telecaller
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  MAIN COMPONENT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const Payments = () => {
  const [payments, setPayments] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState('All Status');
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [rowsPerPage, setRowsPerPage] = useState(10);

  const [viewPay, setViewPay] = useState(null);
  const [editPay, setEditPay] = useState(null);
  const [recordPay, setRecordPay] = useState(null);
  const [invoiceEditor, setInvoiceEditor] = useState(null);
  const qrInputRef = useRef(null);
  const logoInputRef = useRef(null);
  const signatureInputRef = useRef(null);
  const thankYouInputRef = useRef(null);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState(null);

  const showToast = useCallback((msg, type = 'success') => {
    setToast({ msg, type });
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 3000);
  }, []);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const data = await paymentAdminService.getAll();
      setPayments(data || []);
    } catch (e) {
      showToast(e.message || 'Failed to load payments', 'error');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [showToast]);

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  const stats = useMemo(() => ({
    total: payments.length,
    completed: payments.filter((p) => p.status === 'Completed').length,
    awaitingVerification: payments.filter((p) => p.status === 'Recorded').length,
    totalCollected: payments.reduce((s, p) => s + Number(p.amountPaid || 0), 0),
    totalBalance: payments.reduce((s, p) => s + Number(p.balanceAmount || 0), 0),
  }), [payments]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    let d = [...payments];
    if (filterStatus !== 'All Status') d = d.filter((p) => p.status === filterStatus);
    if (search.trim()) {
      const q = search.toLowerCase();
      d = d.filter((x) =>
        (x.organizationName || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (x.phone || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (x.email || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (x.telecallerName || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
    }
    return d;
  }, [payments, filterStatus, search]);

  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / rowsPerPage));
  const paginated = filtered.slice((page - 1) * rowsPerPage, page * rowsPerPage);

  const pgRange = () => {
    const nums = [];
    for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
      if (i === 1 || i === totalPages || Math.abs(i - page) <= 1) nums.push(i);
      else if (nums[nums.length - 1] !== '...') nums.push('...');
    }
    return nums;
  };

  const refreshViewed = (updated) => {
    setPayments((prev) => prev.map((p) => (p.id === updated.id ? updated : p)));
    setViewPay(updated);
  };

  const handleVerify = async (payment) => {
    try {
      const updated = await paymentAdminService.verify(payment.id);
      refreshViewed(updated);
      showToast('Payment verified.');
    } catch (e) { showToast(e.message || 'Failed to verify', 'error'); }
  };

  const handleGenerateInvoice = async (payment, record) => {
    try {
      const updated = await paymentAdminService.generateInvoice(payment.id, record?.id);
      refreshViewed(updated);
      showToast('Invoice generated.');
    } catch (e) { showToast(e.message || 'Failed to generate invoice', 'error'); }
  };

  const handleSendToTelecaller = async (payment) => {
    try {
      const updated = await paymentAdminService.sendToTelecaller(payment.id);
      refreshViewed(updated);
      showToast(`Invoice sent to ${updated.telecallerName || 'telecaller'}.`);
    } catch (e) { showToast(e.message || 'Failed to send invoice', 'error'); }
  };

  // PDF is generated from the React + CSS invoice DOM. The old backend PDF/image
  // endpoint is intentionally not used here. Browser Print -> Save as PDF preserves
  // the same dynamic CSS template used by the invoice preview.
  const openInvoiceEditor = (payment, record = null) => {
    setInvoiceEditor({ payment, record });
  };

  const handlePrintInvoice = async (invoiceData) => {
    try {
      const [qr, assets] = await Promise.all([
        paymentAdminService.getSharedQr().catch(() => null),
        invoiceAssetService.getAssets().catch(() => ({})),
      ]);

      await printInvoicePdf(invoiceData, {
        qrDataUrl: qr?.dataUrl,
        ...assets,
      });

      showToast('Invoice PDF downloaded.');
    } catch (e) {
      showToast(e.message || 'Unable to download the invoice PDF.', 'error');
    }
  };

  const handleDownloadInvoice = (payment) => {
    openInvoiceEditor(payment);
  };

  const handleDownloadRecordInvoice = (payment, record) => {
    openInvoiceEditor(payment, record);
  };

  const handleSaveEdit = async (id, payload) => {
    try {
      const updated = await paymentAdminService.update(id, payload);
      setPayments((prev) => prev.map((p) => (p.id === id ? updated : p)));
      setEditPay(null);
      showToast('Payment updated.');
    } catch (e) { showToast(e.message || 'Failed to update payment', 'error'); }
  };

  const handleAddRecord = async (payment, payload) => {
    try { const updated = await paymentAdminService.addPaymentRecord(payment.id, payload); setPayments(prev=>prev.map(x=>x.id===payment.id?updated:x)); setRecordPay(null); setViewPay(updated); showToast('Payment record saved and totals recalculated.'); } catch(e){ showToast(e.message || 'Failed to save payment record','error'); }
  };
  const handleQrUpload = async (e) => { const file=e.target.files?.[0]; if(!file) return; try { await paymentAdminService.uploadQr(file); showToast('Shared payment QR code uploaded.'); } catch(err){showToast(err.message || 'QR upload failed','error');} finally {e.target.value='';} };
  const handleLogoUpload = async e => { const f=e.target.files?.[0]; if(!f)return; try{await invoiceAssetService.uploadLogo(f);showToast('Invoice logo uploaded.')}catch(err){showToast(err.message||'Logo upload failed','error')}finally{e.target.value=''}};
  const handleSignatureUpload = async e => { const f=e.target.files?.[0]; if(!f)return; try{await invoiceAssetService.uploadSignature(f);showToast('Invoice signature uploaded.')}catch(err){showToast(err.message||'Signature upload failed','error')}finally{e.target.value=''}};
  const handleThankYouUpload = async e => { const f=e.target.files?.[0]; if(!f)return; try{await invoiceAssetService.uploadThankYou(f);showToast('Thank You image uploaded.')}catch(err){showToast(err.message||'Thank You upload failed','error')}finally{e.target.value=''}};

  return (
    <div className="pay-page">
      {toast && <Toast msg={toast.msg} type={toast.type} onClose={() => setToast(null)} />}

      <div className="pay-page-header">
        <div><h1 className="pay-page-title">Payments</h1></div><div className="pay-header-actions"><input ref={qrInputRef} type="file" accept="image/*" hidden onChange={handleQrUpload}/><input ref={logoInputRef} type="file" accept="image/*" hidden onChange={handleLogoUpload}/><input ref={signatureInputRef} type="file" accept="image/*" hidden onChange={handleSignatureUpload}/><input ref={thankYouInputRef} type="file" accept="image/*" hidden onChange={handleThankYouUpload}/><button className="pay-btn-primary" onClick={()=>qrInputRef.current?.click()}>Upload QR</button><button className="pay-btn-primary" onClick={()=>logoInputRef.current?.click()}>Upload Invoice Logo</button><button className="pay-btn-primary" onClick={()=>signatureInputRef.current?.click()}>Upload Signature</button><button className="pay-btn-primary" onClick={()=>thankYouInputRef.current?.click()}>Upload Thank You</button></div>
      </div>

      <div className="pay-stats-row">
        {[
          { icon: <FiCreditCard />, label: 'Total Payments', val: stats.total, color: '#7b1113', bg: '#fce8e8' },
          { icon: <FiAlertCircle />, label: 'Awaiting Verification', val: stats.awaitingVerification, color: '#b91c1c', bg: '#fee2e2' },
          { icon: <FiCheckCircle />, label: 'Completed', val: stats.completed, color: '#15803d', bg: '#dcfce7' },
          { icon: <FiDollarSign />, label: 'Collected', val: fmtINR(stats.totalCollected), color: '#1d4ed8', bg: '#dbeafe' },
          { icon: <FaRupeeSign />, label: 'Balance Due', val: fmtINR(stats.totalBalance), color: '#b91c1c', bg: '#fee2e2' },
        ].map((s) => (
          <div className="pay-stat-card" key={s.label}>
            <div className="pay-stat-icon" style={{ background: s.bg, color: s.color }}>{s.icon}</div>
            <div className="pay-stat-info">
              <div className="pay-stat-val">{s.val}</div>
              <div className="pay-stat-label">{s.label}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="pay-table-card">
        <div className="pay-filter-bar">
          <div className="pay-search-wrap">
            <FiSearch className="pay-search-ico" />
            <input
              className="pay-search-input"
              placeholder="Search by organization, phone, email, telecaller…"
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => { setSearch(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}
            />
          </div>
          <select className="pay-filter-select" value={filterStatus} onChange={(e) => { setFilterStatus(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}>
            {STATUSES.map((s) => <option key={s}>{s}</option>)}
          </select>
          {(filterStatus !== 'All Status' || search) && (
            <button className="pay-btn-ghost" onClick={() => { setFilterStatus('All Status'); setSearch(''); setPage(1); }}>
              <FiX size={13} /> Clear
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="pay-table-header-row">
          <div className="pay-table-label">
            Payment List <span className="pay-record-count">({filtered.length} record{filtered.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''})</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="pay-table-wrap">
          <table className="pay-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Organization</th><th>Telecaller</th><th>Type</th>
                <th>Total</th><th>Paid</th><th>Balance</th><th>Status</th><th>Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {loading ? (
                <tr><td colSpan={8} className="pay-empty-row">Loading payments…</td></tr>
              ) : paginated.length === 0 ? (
                <tr><td colSpan={8} className="pay-empty-row">No payment records found.</td></tr>
              ) : paginated.map((pay) => (
                <tr key={pay.id}>
                  <td>
                    <div className="pay-client-cell">
                      <div className="pay-client-avatar" style={{ background: avatarColor(pay.organizationName) }}>{initials(pay.organizationName)}</div>
                      <div><div className="pay-client-name">{pay.organizationName}</div></div>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td>{pay.telecallerName || '--'}</td>
                  <td>{pay.paymentType}</td>
                  <td><span className="pay-amount">{fmtINR(pay.totalAmount)}</span></td>
                  <td><span className={pay.amountPaid > 0 ? 'pay-amount-paid' : 'pay-amount-zero'}>{fmtINR(pay.amountPaid)}</span></td>
                  <td><span className={pay.balanceAmount > 0 ? 'pay-amount-balance' : 'pay-amount-zero'}>{fmtINR(pay.balanceAmount)}</span></td>
                  <td><StatusBadge status={pay.status} /></td>
                  <td>
                    <div className="pay-action-group">
                      <button className="pay-act-btn view" title="View / Verify" onClick={() => setViewPay(pay)}><FiEye /></button>
                      <button className="pay-act-btn edit" title="Edit" onClick={() => setEditPay(pay)}><FiEdit2 /></button>
                      {pay.status === 'Recorded' && (
                        <button className="pay-act-btn verify" title="Verify" onClick={() => handleVerify(pay)}><FiShield /></button>
                      )}
                      {(pay.status === 'Verified' || pay.status === 'Invoice Generated') && (
                        <button className="pay-act-btn invoice" title="Generate Invoice" onClick={() => handleGenerateInvoice(pay)}><FiFileText /></button>
                      )}
                      {pay.invoiceId && (
                        <>
                          <button className="pay-act-btn edit" title="Edit Invoice" onClick={() => openInvoiceEditor(pay)}><FiEdit2 /></button>
                          <button className="pay-act-btn download" title="Open / Print Invoice" onClick={() => handleDownloadInvoice(pay)}><FiDownload /></button>
                        </>
                      )}
                      {pay.status === 'Invoice Generated' && (
                        <button className="pay-act-btn send" title="Send to Telecaller" onClick={() => handleSendToTelecaller(pay)}><FiSend /></button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        <div className="pay-pagination-bar">
          <div className="pay-rows-select">
            Rows per page:
            <select value={rowsPerPage} onChange={(e) => { setRowsPerPage(Number(e.target.value)); setPage(1); }}>
              {ROWS_OPTIONS.map((r) => <option key={r}>{r}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="pay-page-nums">
            <button className="pay-pg-btn" disabled={page === 1} onClick={() => setPage((p) => p - 1)}><FiChevronLeft size={13} /></button>
            {pgRange().map((p, i) => p === '...'
              ? <span key={`d${i}`} style={{ padding: '0 4px', color: 'var(--text-light)' }}>…</span>
              : <button key={p} className={`pay-pg-btn${page === p ? ' active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setPage(p)}>{p}</button>)}
            <button className="pay-pg-btn" disabled={page === totalPages} onClick={() => setPage((p) => p + 1)}><FiChevronRight size={13} /></button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {viewPay && (
        <ViewPaymentModal
          payment={viewPay}
          onClose={() => setViewPay(null)}
          onVerify={() => handleVerify(viewPay)}
          onGenerateInvoice={() => handleGenerateInvoice(viewPay)}
          onGenerateRecordInvoice={(payment, record) => handleGenerateInvoice(payment, record)}
          onSendToTelecaller={() => handleSendToTelecaller(viewPay)}
          onDownloadInvoice={handleDownloadInvoice}
          onDownloadRecordInvoice={handleDownloadRecordInvoice}
          onAddRecord={() => setRecordPay(viewPay)}
        />
      )}

      {recordPay && <PaymentRecordModal payment={recordPay} onClose={() => setRecordPay(null)} onSave={(payload)=>handleAddRecord(recordPay,payload)} />}

      {editPay && (
        <EditPaymentModal payment={editPay} onClose={() => setEditPay(null)} onSave={handleSaveEdit} />
      )}

      {invoiceEditor && (
        <InvoiceEditorModal
          payment={invoiceEditor.payment}
          record={invoiceEditor.record}
          onClose={() => setInvoiceEditor(null)}
          onPrint={handlePrintInvoice}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default Payments;


===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\pages\Quotations\Quotations.jsx =====
// src/pages/Quotations/Quotations.jsx
import React, { useState, useMemo, useCallback, useEffect } from 'react';
import './Quotations.css';
import quotationService from '../../services/quotationService';
import quotationAdminService from '../../services/quotationAdminService';
import {
  FiFileText, FiSearch, FiDownload, FiEye, FiX, FiCheck,
  FiChevronLeft, FiChevronRight, FiRefreshCw, FiDollarSign,
  FiClock, FiCheckCircle, FiAlertCircle, FiUser, FiXCircle,
  FiSend, FiImage, FiUpload, FiTrash2,
} from 'react-icons/fi';

// ─── Constants ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Mirrors the backend workflow exactly (see QuotationService):
// Draft -> Pending Approval -> Approved | Rejected | Revision Required
// Approved -> Sent (done by the telecaller; Super Admin only sees it).
const ROWS_OPTIONS = [10, 20, 50];
const AVATAR_COLORS = ['#7b1113', '#2563eb', '#16a34a', '#7c3aed', '#c2410c', '#0369a1', '#4338ca', '#be185d'];

const STATUS_CONFIG = {
  'Draft':                { cls: 'qt-status-draft',    label: 'Draft' },
  'Pending Approval':  { cls: 'qt-status-sent',      label: 'Pending Approval' },
  'Approved':              { cls: 'qt-status-approved',  label: 'Approved' },
  'Rejected':              { cls: 'qt-status-rejected',  label: 'Rejected' },
  'Revision Required':     { cls: 'qt-status-revised',   label: 'Revision Required' },
  'Sent':        { cls: 'qt-status-viewed',    label: 'Sent' },
};

const avatarColor = (name) => AVATAR_COLORS[(name?.charCodeAt(0) || 0) % AVATAR_COLORS.length];
const initials = (name) => name?.split(' ').slice(0, 2).map(w => w[0]?.toUpperCase()).join('') || '?';
const fmtDateTime = (iso) => {
  if (!iso) return '—';
  const d = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return '—';
  return d.toLocaleString('en-IN', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
};

const Toast = ({ msg, type, onClose }) => (
  <div className={`qt-toast qt-toast-${type}`} onClick={onClose}>
    {type === 'success' ? <FiCheck /> : type === 'error' ? <FiX /> : <FiRefreshCw />}
    {msg}
  </div>
);

const StatusPill = ({ status }) => {
  const cfg = STATUS_CONFIG[status] || { cls: '', label: status || '—' };
  return <span className={`qt-status-pill ${cfg.cls}`}>{cfg.label}</span>;
};

// ─── Approve Modal ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const ApproveModal = ({ quotation, onClose, onConfirm }) => {
  const [remark, setRemark] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const submit = async () => { setBusy(true); try { await onConfirm(quotation.id, remark); onClose(); } finally { setBusy(false); } };
  return (
    <div className="qt-modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="qt-modal-box qt-modal-box-sm" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="qt-modal-header">
          <div className="qt-modal-title">Approve {quotation.id}</div>
          <button className="qt-modal-close" onClick={onClose}><FiX /></button>
        </div>
        <div className="qt-modal-body">
          <div className="qt-form">
            <div className="qt-form-group">
              <label>Remark (optional)</label>
              <textarea placeholder="Any note for the telecaller…" value={remark} onChange={e => setRemark(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="qt-form-actions">
              <button className="qt-btn-outline" onClick={onClose} disabled={busy}>Cancel</button>
              <button className="qt-btn-primary" onClick={submit} disabled={busy}><FiCheck size={13} /> {busy ? 'Approving…' : 'Approve Quotation'}</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// ─── Reject / Revision Modal (reason required) ──────────────────────────────
const ReasonModal = ({ quotation, mode, onClose, onConfirm }) => {
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const isReject = mode === 'reject';
  const submit = async () => {
    if (!reason.trim()) { setError(`A ${isReject ? 'rejection' : 'revision'} reason is required.`); return; }
    setBusy(true);
    try { await onConfirm(quotation.id, reason); onClose(); }
    catch (e) { setError(e.message || 'Unable to submit.'); }
    finally { setBusy(false); }
  };
  return (
    <div className="qt-modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="qt-modal-box qt-modal-box-sm" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="qt-modal-header">
          <div className="qt-modal-title">{isReject ? 'Reject' : 'Request Revision'} {quotation.id}</div>
          <button className="qt-modal-close" onClick={onClose}><FiX /></button>
        </div>
        <div className="qt-modal-body">
          <div className="qt-form">
            {error && (
              <div className="qt-client-autofill" style={{ borderColor: '#fecaca', background: '#fef2f2' }}>
                <div className="qt-client-autofill-label" style={{ color: '#b91c1c' }}><FiAlertCircle size={12} /> {error}</div>
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="qt-form-group">
              <label>{isReject ? 'Rejection reason *' : 'What needs to change? *'}</label>
              <textarea placeholder={isReject ? 'Explain why this quotation is being rejected…' : 'Tell the telecaller what to revise…'} value={reason} onChange={e => setReason(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="qt-form-actions">
              <button className="qt-btn-outline" onClick={onClose} disabled={busy}>Cancel</button>
              <button className="qt-btn-primary" style={isReject ? { background: '#dc2626' } : undefined} onClick={submit} disabled={busy}>
                <FiXCircle size={13} /> {busy ? 'Submitting…' : isReject ? 'Reject Quotation' : 'Request Revision'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// ─── View / Timeline Drawer ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
const ViewModal = ({ quotation, onClose, onDownload }) => (
  <div className="qt-modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
    <div className="qt-modal-box qt-modal-box-wide" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
      <div className="qt-modal-header">
        <div className="qt-modal-title">{quotation.id}</div>
        <button className="qt-modal-close" onClick={onClose}><FiX /></button>
      </div>
      <div className="qt-view-hero">
        <div className="qt-view-avatar" style={{ background: avatarColor(quotation.clientName) }}>{initials(quotation.clientName)}</div>
        <div className="qt-view-hero-info">
          <div className="qt-view-client-name">{quotation.clientName}</div>
          <div className="qt-view-quote-id">{quotation.organization}</div>
        </div>
        <StatusPill status={quotation.status} />
      </div>
      <div className="qt-view-grid">
        <div className="qt-view-row"><span className="qt-view-key">Prepared By</span><span className="qt-view-val">{quotation.createdByName || quotation.createdBy}</span></div>
        <div className="qt-view-row"><span className="qt-view-key">Subject</span><span className="qt-view-val">{quotation.subject}</span></div>
        <div className="qt-view-row"><span className="qt-view-key">Amount</span><span className="qt-view-val">{quotation.currency}{Number(quotation.amount || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN')}</span></div>
        <div className="qt-view-row"><span className="qt-view-key">Valid Till</span><span className="qt-view-val">{quotation.validTill || '—'}</span></div>
        <div className="qt-view-row"><span className="qt-view-key">Email</span><span className="qt-view-val">{quotation.email || '—'}</span></div>
        <div className="qt-view-row"><span className="qt-view-key">Phone</span><span className="qt-view-val">{quotation.phone || '—'}</span></div>
        <div className="qt-view-row"><span className="qt-view-key">Submitted</span><span className="qt-view-val">{fmtDateTime(quotation.submittedAt)}</span></div>
        {quotation.approvedAt && <div className="qt-view-row"><span className="qt-view-key">Approved</span><span className="qt-view-val">{quotation.approvedBy} · {fmtDateTime(quotation.approvedAt)}</span></div>}
        {quotation.rejectedAt && <div className="qt-view-row"><span className="qt-view-key">Rejected</span><span className="qt-view-val">{quotation.rejectedBy} · {fmtDateTime(quotation.rejectedAt)}</span></div>}
        {quotation.rejectionReason && <div className="qt-view-row"><span className="qt-view-key">Rejection Reason</span><span className="qt-view-val">{quotation.rejectionReason}</span></div>}
        {quotation.revisionReason && <div className="qt-view-row"><span className="qt-view-key">Revision Notes</span><span className="qt-view-val">{quotation.revisionReason}</span></div>}
        {quotation.approvalRemark && <div className="qt-view-row"><span className="qt-view-key">Approval Remark</span><span className="qt-view-val">{quotation.approvalRemark}</span></div>}
        {quotation.sent && (
          <>
            <div className="qt-view-row"><span className="qt-view-key">Sent To Client</span><span className="qt-view-val">{quotation.sentTo}</span></div>
            <div className="qt-view-row"><span className="qt-view-key">Channel / When</span><span className="qt-view-val">{quotation.sentChannel} · {fmtDateTime(quotation.sentAt)}</span></div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
      {quotation.notes && (
        <div className="qt-view-note">
          <div className="qt-view-note-label">Notes</div>
          <div className="qt-view-note-text">{quotation.notes}</div>
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="qt-timeline">
        <div className="qt-timeline-title">History</div>
        <div className="qt-timeline-list">
          {(quotation.timeline || []).map((t, i) => (
            <div className="qt-timeline-item" key={i}>
              <div className="qt-timeline-left">
                <div className="qt-timeline-dot" style={{ background: t.bg, color: t.color }}>•</div>
                {i < quotation.timeline.length - 1 && <div className="qt-timeline-line" />}
              </div>
              <div className="qt-timeline-content">
                <div className="qt-timeline-action">{t.action}</div>
                <div className="qt-timeline-time">{t.time}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="qt-form-actions" style={{ padding: '0 24px 24px' }}>
        {quotation.generatedPdfPath && (
          <button className="qt-btn-outline" onClick={() => onDownload(quotation)}><FiDownload size={13} /> Download PDF</button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
);

// ─── Letterhead / Logo / Footer settings (used by every generated quotation PDF) ─────────
const BrandingModal = ({ onClose, showToast }) => {
  const [b, setB] = useState(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState('');
  const [footer, setFooter] = useState({ phone: '', website: '', address: '' });

  const apply = (data) => {
    setB(data);
    setFooter({ phone: data.phone || '', website: data.website || '', address: data.address || '' });
  };
  useEffect(() => {
    quotationAdminService.getBranding().then(apply).catch(e => showToast(e.message || 'Unable to load letterhead.', 'error'));
  }, [showToast]);

  const upload = async (type, file) => {
    if (!file) return;
    setBusy(type);
    try { apply(await quotationAdminService.uploadBranding(type, file)); showToast(type === 'logo' ? 'Logo updated.' : 'Letterpad updated.'); }
    catch (e) { showToast(e.message || 'Upload failed.', 'error'); }
    finally { setBusy(''); }
  };
  const remove = async (type) => {
    setBusy(type);
    try { apply(await quotationAdminService.deleteBranding(type)); showToast(type === 'logo' ? 'Logo removed.' : 'Default letterpad restored.'); }
    catch (e) { showToast(e.message || 'Unable to remove.', 'error'); }
    finally { setBusy(''); }
  };
  const saveSettings = async (patch) => {
    setBusy('settings');
    try { apply(await quotationAdminService.saveBrandingSettings(patch)); showToast('Saved.'); }
    catch (e) { showToast(e.message || 'Unable to save.', 'error'); }
    finally { setBusy(''); }
  };

  const box = { border: '1px dashed #cbd5e1', borderRadius: 10, padding: 12, display: 'flex', gap: 14, alignItems: 'center' };
  const FileBtn = ({ type, label }) => (
    <label className="qt-btn-outline" style={{ cursor: 'pointer', display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
      <FiUpload size={13} /> {busy === type ? 'Uploading…' : label}
      <input type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg" hidden disabled={!!busy}
        onChange={e => { upload(type, e.target.files?.[0]); e.target.value = ''; }} />
    </label>
  );

  return (
    <div className="qt-modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="qt-modal-box qt-modal-box-wide" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="qt-modal-header">
          <div className="qt-modal-title">Quotation Letterhead — Logo, Letterpad &amp; Footer</div>
          <button className="qt-modal-close" onClick={onClose}><FiX /></button>
        </div>
        <div className="qt-modal-body">
          {!b ? <div style={{ padding: 24 }}>Loading…</div> : (
            <div className="qt-form">
              <div className="qt-form-section">
                <div className="qt-form-section-title">Company Logo</div>
                <div style={box}>
                  <div style={{ width: 190, height: 80, background: '#f8fafc', borderRadius: 8, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
                    {b.logoDataUrl ? <img src={b.logoDataUrl} alt="Logo" style={{ maxWidth: '100%', maxHeight: '100%' }} /> : <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 12 }}>No logo uploaded</span>}
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 8 }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
                      <FileBtn type="logo" label={b.hasLogo ? 'Replace logo' : 'Upload logo'} />
                      {b.hasLogo && <button className="qt-btn-outline" disabled={!!busy} onClick={() => remove('logo')}><FiTrash2 size={13} /> Remove</button>}
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#64748b' }}>PNG / JPG. Appears at the top-left of every quotation PDF.</div>
                    <label style={{ fontSize: 13, display: 'flex', gap: 6, alignItems: 'center' }}>
                      <input type="checkbox" checked={!!b.showBrandText} disabled={!!busy}
                        onChange={e => saveSettings({ showBrandText: e.target.checked })} />
                      Show “CAMPAS TECH – Private Limited” text next to the logo
                    </label>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="qt-form-section">
                <div className="qt-form-section-title">Letterpad (full-page background)</div>
                <div style={box}>
                  <div style={{ width: 130, height: 184, background: '#f8fafc', borderRadius: 6, overflow: 'hidden', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0' }}>
                    {b.letterpadDataUrl && <img src={b.letterpadDataUrl} alt="Letterpad" style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover' }} />}
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 8 }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
                      <FileBtn type="letterpad" label={b.hasLetterpad ? 'Replace letterpad' : 'Upload letterpad'} />
                      {b.hasLetterpad && <button className="qt-btn-outline" disabled={!!busy} onClick={() => remove('letterpad')}><FiTrash2 size={13} /> Use default</button>}
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#64748b' }}>
                      A4 portrait image (e.g. 2480 × 3508 px) with the ribbons / border only — keep the middle empty.
                      {b.usingDefaultLetterpad && <> <b>Currently using the built-in default letterpad.</b></>}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="qt-form-section">
                <div className="qt-form-section-title">Footer (bottom-left of every page)</div>
                <div className="qt-form-group"><label>Phone</label><input value={footer.phone} onChange={e => setFooter(f => ({ ...f, phone: e.target.value }))} /></div>
                <div className="qt-form-group"><label>Website</label><input value={footer.website} onChange={e => setFooter(f => ({ ...f, website: e.target.value }))} /></div>
                <div className="qt-form-group"><label>Address</label><textarea rows={2} value={footer.address} onChange={e => setFooter(f => ({ ...f, address: e.target.value }))} /></div>
                <div className="qt-form-actions" style={{ justifyContent: 'flex-start' }}>
                  <button className="qt-btn-primary" disabled={!!busy} onClick={() => saveSettings(footer)}><FiCheck size={13} /> Save footer</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// ─── Main Super Admin Quotations Page ───────────────────────────────────────
const Quotations = () => {
  const [quotations, setQuotations] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState('Pending Approval');
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [rowsPerPage, setRowsPerPage] = useState(10);

  const [viewQt, setViewQt] = useState(null);
  const [approveQt, setApproveQt] = useState(null);
  const [reasonModal, setReasonModal] = useState(null); // { quotation, mode }
  const [toast, setToast] = useState(null);
  const [showBranding, setShowBranding] = useState(false);

  const showToast = useCallback((msg, type = 'success') => {
    setToast({ msg, type });
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 3000);
  }, []);

  const loadAll = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const list = await quotationService.getAdminAll();
      setQuotations(Array.isArray(list) ? list : []);
    } catch (e) {
      showToast(e.message || 'Failed to load quotations.', 'error');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [showToast]);

  useEffect(() => { loadAll(); }, [loadAll]);

  const upsert = (q) => setQuotations(prev => prev.map(x => x.id === q.id ? q : x));

  const stats = useMemo(() => ({
    total: quotations.length,
    pending: quotations.filter(q => q.status === 'Pending Approval').length,
    approved: quotations.filter(q => q.status === 'Approved').length,
    sent: quotations.filter(q => q.status === 'Sent').length,
    sentValue: quotations.filter(q => q.status === 'Sent').reduce((s, q) => s + (q.amount || 0), 0),
  }), [quotations]);

  const TABS = [
    { id: 'All', label: 'All', count: quotations.length },
    { id: 'Pending Approval', label: 'Pending Approval', count: stats.pending },
    { id: 'Approved', label: 'Approved', count: stats.approved },
    { id: 'Rejected', label: 'Rejected', count: quotations.filter(q => q.status === 'Rejected').length },
    { id: 'Revision Required', label: 'Revision Required', count: quotations.filter(q => q.status === 'Revision Required').length },
    { id: 'Sent', label: 'Sent', count: stats.sent },
  ];

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    let d = [...quotations];
    if (filterStatus !== 'All') d = d.filter(q => q.status === filterStatus);
    if (search.trim()) {
      const q = search.toLowerCase();
      d = d.filter(x =>
        (x.id || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (x.clientName || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (x.subject || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (x.createdByName || x.createdBy || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
    }
    return d;
  }, [quotations, filterStatus, search]);

  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / rowsPerPage));
  const paginated = filtered.slice((page - 1) * rowsPerPage, page * rowsPerPage);
  const pageNums = () => {
    const nums = [];
    for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
      if (i === 1 || i === totalPages || Math.abs(i - page) <= 1) nums.push(i);
      else if (nums[nums.length - 1] !== '...') nums.push('...');
    }
    return nums;
  };

  const handleApprove = async (id, remark) => {
    try {
      const saved = await quotationAdminService.review(id, 'Approved', remark);
      upsert(saved);
      showToast(`${id} approved. The telecaller can now send it to the client.`);
    } catch (e) { showToast(e.message || 'Unable to approve.', 'error'); throw e; }
  };
  const handleReject = async (id, reason) => {
    try {
      const saved = await quotationAdminService.review(id, 'Rejected', reason);
      upsert(saved);
      showToast(`${id} rejected.`, 'error');
    } catch (e) { showToast(e.message || 'Unable to reject.', 'error'); throw e; }
  };
  const handleRevision = async (id, reason) => {
    try {
      const saved = await quotationAdminService.review(id, 'Revision Required', reason);
      upsert(saved);
      showToast(`Revision requested for ${id}.`);
    } catch (e) { showToast(e.message || 'Unable to request revision.', 'error'); throw e; }
  };
  const handleDownload = async (q) => {
    try {
      const blob = await quotationAdminService.downloadPdfBlob(q.id);
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url; a.download = q.generatedPdfFileName || `Quotation_${q.id}.pdf`;
      document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    } catch (e) { showToast(e.message || 'Unable to download PDF.', 'error'); }
  };

  return (
    <div className="qt-page">
      {toast && <Toast msg={toast.msg} type={toast.type} onClose={() => setToast(null)} />}
      {showBranding && <BrandingModal onClose={() => setShowBranding(false)} showToast={showToast} />}

      <div className="qt-page-header">
        <div>
          <div className="qt-page-title">Quotations — Approvals</div>
          <div className="qt-breadcrumb">Review quotations submitted by telecallers. Approve, reject, or request a revision — sending to the client stays with the telecaller.</div>
        </div>
        <div className="qt-header-actions">
          <button className="qt-btn-outline" onClick={() => setShowBranding(true)}><FiImage size={13} /> Letterhead &amp; Logo</button>
          <button className="qt-btn-outline" onClick={loadAll}><FiRefreshCw size={13} /> Refresh</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="qt-stats-row">
        <div className="qt-stat-card" style={{ borderTop: '3px solid #7b1113' }}>
          <div className="qt-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#fce8e8', color: '#7b1113' }}><FiFileText /></div>
          <div className="qt-stat-info"><div className="qt-stat-val">{stats.total}</div><div className="qt-stat-label">Total Quotes</div></div>
        </div>
        <div className="qt-stat-card" style={{ borderTop: '3px solid #1d4ed8' }}>
          <div className="qt-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#dbeafe', color: '#1d4ed8' }}><FiClock /></div>
          <div className="qt-stat-info"><div className="qt-stat-val">{stats.pending}</div><div className="qt-stat-label">Awaiting My Confirmation</div></div>
        </div>
        <div className="qt-stat-card" style={{ borderTop: '3px solid #15803d' }}>
          <div className="qt-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#dcfce7', color: '#15803d' }}><FiCheckCircle /></div>
          <div className="qt-stat-info"><div className="qt-stat-val">{stats.approved}</div><div className="qt-stat-label">Approved, Not Yet Sent</div></div>
        </div>
        <div className="qt-stat-card" style={{ borderTop: '3px solid #4338ca' }}>
          <div className="qt-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#e0e7ff', color: '#4338ca' }}><FiDollarSign /></div>
          <div className="qt-stat-info"><div className="qt-stat-val">₹{stats.sentValue.toLocaleString('en-IN')}</div><div className="qt-stat-label">Sent to Clients ({stats.sent})</div></div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="qt-table-card">
        <div className="qt-tabs-row">
          {TABS.map(t => (
            <div key={t.id} className={`qt-tab ${filterStatus === t.id ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => { setFilterStatus(t.id); setPage(1); }}>
              {t.label} <span className="qt-tab-badge">{t.count}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="qt-filter-bar">
          <div className="qt-search-wrap">
            <FiSearch className="qt-search-ico" />
            <input className="qt-search-input" placeholder="Search by client, subject, telecaller or ID…" value={search} onChange={e => { setSearch(e.target.value); setPage(1); }} />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="qt-table-wrap">
          {loading ? (
            <div style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-light)' }}>Loading quotations…</div>
          ) : (
            <table className="qt-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Quote #</th><th>Client</th><th>Telecaller</th><th>Amount</th><th>Status</th><th>Sent</th><th>Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {paginated.length === 0 && (
                  <tr className="qt-empty-row"><td colSpan={7}>No quotations found.</td></tr>
                )}
                {paginated.map(q => (
                  <tr key={q.id}>
                    <td className="qt-quote-no">{q.id}</td>
                    <td>
                      <div className="qt-client-cell">
                        <div className="qt-client-avatar" style={{ background: avatarColor(q.clientName) }}>{initials(q.clientName)}</div>
                        <div>
                          <div className="qt-client-name">{q.clientName}</div>
                          <div className="qt-client-email">{q.email}</div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td><div className="qt-client-cell"><FiUser size={12} />&nbsp;{q.createdByName || q.createdBy}</div></td>
                    <td className="qt-amount">{q.currency}{Number(q.amount || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN')}</td>
                    <td><StatusPill status={q.status} /></td>
                    <td>{q.sent ? `${q.sentChannel} · ${fmtDateTime(q.sentAt)}` : '—'}</td>
                    <td>
                      <div className="qt-action-group">
                        <button className="qt-act-btn view" title="View" onClick={() => setViewQt(q)}><FiEye size={14} /></button>
                        {q.status === 'Pending Approval' && (
                          <>
                            <button className="qt-act-btn download" title="Approve" onClick={() => setApproveQt(q)}><FiCheck size={14} /></button>
                            <button className="qt-act-btn del" title="Reject" onClick={() => setReasonModal({ quotation: q, mode: 'reject' })}><FiX size={14} /></button>
                            <button className="qt-act-btn edit" title="Request Revision" onClick={() => setReasonModal({ quotation: q, mode: 'revision' })}><FiRefreshCw size={14} /></button>
                          </>
                        )}
                        {q.generatedPdfPath && (
                          <button className="qt-act-btn download" title="Download PDF" onClick={() => handleDownload(q)}><FiDownload size={14} /></button>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="qt-pagination-bar">
          <div className="qt-rows-select">
            Rows per page:
            <select value={rowsPerPage} onChange={e => { setRowsPerPage(Number(e.target.value)); setPage(1); }}>
              {ROWS_OPTIONS.map(n => <option key={n} value={n}>{n}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="qt-page-nums">
            <button className="qt-pg-btn" disabled={page === 1} onClick={() => setPage(p => p - 1)}><FiChevronLeft /></button>
            {pageNums().map((n, i) => n === '...'
              ? <span key={`e${i}`}>…</span>
              : <button key={n} className={`qt-pg-btn ${page === n ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setPage(n)}>{n}</button>)}
            <button className="qt-pg-btn" disabled={page === totalPages} onClick={() => setPage(p => p + 1)}><FiChevronRight /></button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {viewQt && <ViewModal quotation={viewQt} onClose={() => setViewQt(null)} onDownload={handleDownload} />}
      {approveQt && <ApproveModal quotation={approveQt} onClose={() => setApproveQt(null)} onConfirm={handleApprove} />}
      {reasonModal && (
        <ReasonModal
          quotation={reasonModal.quotation}
          mode={reasonModal.mode}
          onClose={() => setReasonModal(null)}
          onConfirm={reasonModal.mode === 'reject' ? handleReject : handleRevision}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default Quotations;

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  CartesianGrid,
  Tooltip,
  Legend,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  Cell,
  LabelList,
} from "recharts";
import {

  FaPhone,
  FaRedo,
  FaTrophy,
  FaSearch,
  FaFileExcel,
  FaPrint,

  FaBullseye,

const PERIODS = [
  { value: "TODAY", label: "Today" },
  { value: "WEEK", label: "This Week" },
  { value: "MONTH", label: "This Month" },
  { value: "ALL", label: "All Time" },
];

const CHART_COLORS = [
  "#7b1113",
  "#2563eb",
  "#16a34a",
  "#d97706",
  "#7c3aed",
  "#0891b2",
  "#db2777",

const exportCsv = (rows, filename) => {
  if (!rows?.length) return;

  const keys = Object.keys(rows[0] || {});

  const csv = [
    keys.join(","),
    ...rows.map((row) =>
      keys
        .map((key) => {
          const value = String(row[key] ?? "").replaceAll('"', '""');
          return `"${value}"`;
        })
        .join(",")
    ),
  ].join("\n");

  const blob = new Blob(
    [csv],
    { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;" }
  );

  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

  const link = document.createElement("a");
  link.href = url;
  link.download = filename;

  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  document.body.removeChild(link);

  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
};

const ChartCard = ({
  title,
  description,
  icon,
  children,
  className = "",
}) => {
  return (
    <div className={`rp-panel rp-chart-card ${className}`}>
      <div className="rp-chart-header">
        <div className="rp-chart-heading">
          <div className="rp-chart-icon">
            {icon}
          </div>

          <div>
            <h3>{title}</h3>
            <p>{description}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {children}
    </div>
  );
};

const CustomTooltip = ({ active, payload, label }) => {
  if (!active || !payload?.length) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div className="rp-custom-tooltip">
      <div className="rp-tooltip-title">
        {label}
      </div>

      {payload.map((item, index) => (
        <div
          className="rp-tooltip-row"
          key={`${item.name}-${index}`}
        >
          <span>{item.name}</span>
          <b>{item.value ?? 0}</b>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
};

const ReportView = ({ admin = true }) => {
  const [period, setPeriod] = useState("MONTH");
  const [report, setReport] = useState(null);
  const [telecallers, setTelecallers] = useState([]);
  const [selectedUsername, setSelectedUsername] = useState("");
  const [selectedReport, setSelectedReport] = useState(null);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [loadingDetail, setLoadingDetail] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const load = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      if (admin) {
        const [overall, rows] = await Promise.all([
          reportService.getOverallReport(period),
          reportService.getTelecallerReports(period),
        ]);

        setReport(overall);
        setTelecallers(
          Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []
        );

        setSelectedUsername("");
        setSelectedReport(null);
      } else {
        const myReport =
          await reportService.getMyReport(period);

        setReport(myReport);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setError(
        e.message || "Unable to load report data."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [period]);

  const openTelecaller = async (username) => {
    if (!username) {
      setSelectedUsername("");
      setSelectedReport(null);
      return;
    }

    setLoadingDetail(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      setSelectedUsername(username);

      const detail =
        await reportService.getTelecallerReport(
          username,
          period
        );

      setSelectedReport(detail);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(
        e.message ||
          "Unable to load telecaller report."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoadingDetail(false);
    }
  };

  const activeReport =
    selectedReport || report;

  const summary =
    activeReport?.summary || {};

  const today =
    activeReport?.today || {};

  const leadStatus =
    Array.isArray(activeReport?.leadStatus)
      ? activeReport.leadStatus
      : [];

  const outcomes =
    Array.isArray(activeReport?.callOutcomes)
      ? activeReport.callOutcomes
      : [];

  /*
   * --------------------------------------------------
   * MAIN PERFORMANCE DATA
   * Used by professional bar chart
   * --------------------------------------------------
   */
  const performanceData = [
    {
      metric: "Total Leads",
      value:
        summary.totalLeads ??
        summary.assignedLeads ??
        0,
    },
    {
      metric: "Calls Made",
      value: summary.callsMade ?? 0,
    },
    {
      metric: "Connected",
      value: summary.connectedCalls ?? 0,
    },
    {
      metric: "Follow-ups",
      value: summary.followUps ?? 0,
    },
    {
      metric: "Interested",
      value: summary.interestedLeads ?? 0,
    },
    {
      metric: "Converted",
      value: summary.clientsConverted ?? 0,
    },
  ];

  /*
   * --------------------------------------------------
   * TODAY ACTIVITY DATA
   * --------------------------------------------------
   */
  const todayPerformanceData = [
    {
      metric: "Calls Made",
      value: today.callsMade ?? 0,
    },
    {
      metric: "Connected",
      value: today.connectedCalls ?? 0,
    },
    {
      metric: "Missed",
      value: today.missedCalls ?? 0,
    },
    {
      metric: "New Leads",
      value:
        today.newLeadsContacted ?? 0,
    },
    {
      metric: "Follow-ups",
      value:
        today.followUpsCompleted ?? 0,
    },
    {
      metric: "Interested",
      value: today.interestedLeads ?? 0,
    },
  ];

  /*
   * --------------------------------------------------
   * SUPER ADMIN TELECALLER COMPARISON
   * --------------------------------------------------
   */
  const telecallerPerformanceData =
    useMemo(() => {
      return telecallers.map((row) => {
        const item =
          row.report?.summary ||
          row.summary ||
          {};

        return {
          name:
            row.fullName ||
            row.username ||
            "Unknown",
          calls:
            item.callsMade ?? 0,
          connected:
            item.connectedCalls ?? 0,
          converted:
            item.clientsConverted ?? 0,
          conversionRate:
            item.conversionRate ?? 0,
        };
      });
    }, [telecallers]);

  /*
   * --------------------------------------------------
   * TELECALLER TABLE FILTER
   * --------------------------------------------------
   */
  const filteredTelecallers =
    useMemo(() => {
      const needle =
        search.trim().toLowerCase();

      if (!needle) {
        return telecallers;
      }

      return telecallers.filter((row) =>
        [
          row.fullName,
          row.username,
          row.employeeId,
        ]
          .filter(Boolean)
          .some((value) =>
            String(value)
              .toLowerCase()
              .includes(needle)
          )
      );
    }, [search, telecallers]);

  const cards = [
    {
      label: "Total Leads",
      value:
        summary.totalLeads ??
        summary.assignedLeads ??
        0,
      icon: <FaUsers />,
      color: "#7b1113",
    },
    {
      label: "Calls Made",
      value:
        summary.callsMade ?? 0,
      icon: <FaPhone />,
      color: "#2563eb",
    },
    {
      label: "Follow-ups",
      value:
        summary.followUps ?? 0,
      icon: <FaRedo />,
      color: "#d97706",
    },
    {
      label: "Connected Calls",
      value:
        summary.connectedCalls ?? 0,
      icon: <FaCheckCircle />,

    },
    {

      value:
        summary.missedCalls ?? 0,
      icon: <FaPhoneSlash />,

    {

          </div>

      </div>
    );
  }

  return (

          PAGE HEADER
      ============================================ */}

              <FaChartBar />
            </div>

                {admin
                  ? "Reports & Performance Analytics"
                  : "My Performance Reports"}
              </h1>

              <p className="rp-breadcrumb">
                {admin
                  ? "Monitor, compare and analyse telecaller performance from one centralized dashboard."
                  : "Track your activity, lead performance and conversion results."}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="rp-header-actions">

          <select
            className="rp-select"
            value={period}

              setPeriod(e.target.value)
            }

          <button
            className="rp-btn rp-btn-outline"

              exportCsv(
                cards.map((card) => ({
                  Metric: card.label,
                  Value: card.value,
                })),

              )
            }

            className="stats-card"

        ))}
      </div>

      {/* ============================================
          MAIN PERFORMANCE CHART
      ============================================ */}
      <ChartCard
        className="rp-full-chart"
        icon={<FaChartBar />}
        title="Overall Performance Breakdown"
        description="This chart provides a complete view of lead activity, calling performance, follow-up activity, interested prospects and final conversions."
      >
        <div className="rp-chart">
          <ResponsiveContainer
            width="100%"

              data={performanceData}
              margin={{
                top: 30,

                left: 0,
                bottom: 20,
              }}
            >
              <CartesianGrid
                strokeDasharray="3 3"
                vertical={false}
              />

              <XAxis
                dataKey="metric"
              />

              <YAxis
                allowDecimals={false}
              />

              <Tooltip
                content={<CustomTooltip />}
              />

              <Bar
                dataKey="value"
                name="Performance"
                radius={[8, 8, 0, 0]}
                maxBarSize={70}
              >
                <LabelList
                  dataKey="value"
                  position="top"
                />

                {performanceData.map(
                  (entry, index) => (
                    <Cell
                      key={`${entry.metric}-${index}`}
                      fill={
                        CHART_COLORS[
                          index %
                            CHART_COLORS.length
                        ]
                      }
                    />
                  )

              </Bar>

        </div>

        <div className="rp-chart-insight">

      <div className="rp-two-col">

        >
          <div className="rp-chart">
            <ResponsiveContainer
              width="100%"
              height={320}
            >
              <BarChart data={leadStatus}>
                <CartesianGrid
                  strokeDasharray="3 3"
                  vertical={false}
                />

                <XAxis
                  dataKey="status"
                />

                <YAxis
                  allowDecimals={false}
                />

                <Tooltip
                  content={<CustomTooltip />}
                />

                <Bar
                  dataKey="count"
                  name="Lead Count"
                  fill="#7b1113"
                  radius={[6, 6, 0, 0]}
                >
                  <LabelList
                    dataKey="count"
                    position="top"
                  />
                </Bar>
              </BarChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>

          <div className="rp-chart-insight">

              progress towards conversion.
            </span>
          </div>
        </ChartCard>

        <ChartCard
          icon={<FaPhone />}
          title="Call Outcome Analysis"
          description="Shows the results of all completed calling activity, including successful connections and unsuccessful attempts."
        >
          <div className="rp-chart">
            <ResponsiveContainer
              width="100%"
              height={320}
            >
              <BarChart data={outcomes}>
                <CartesianGrid
                  strokeDasharray="3 3"
                  vertical={false}
                />

                <XAxis
                  dataKey="outcome"
                />

                <YAxis
                  allowDecimals={false}
                />

                <Tooltip
                  content={<CustomTooltip />}
                />

                <Bar
                  dataKey="count"
                  name="Calls"
                  fill="#2563eb"
                  radius={[6, 6, 0, 0]}
                >
                  <LabelList
                    dataKey="count"
                    position="top"
                  />
                </Bar>
              </BarChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>

          <div className="rp-chart-insight">
            <FaPhone />

            <span>
              Use this report to understand call
              quality and identify opportunities
              to improve successful connections.
            </span>
          </div>
        </ChartCard>

      </div>

      {/* ============================================
          TODAY'S PERFORMANCE
      ============================================ */}
      <ChartCard
        className="rp-full-chart"
        icon={<FaArrowUp />}
        title="Today's Activity Performance"
        description="A detailed breakdown of today's calling, connection, follow-up and lead engagement activity."
      >
        <div className="rp-chart">
          <ResponsiveContainer
            width="100%"
            height={350}
          >
            <BarChart
              data={todayPerformanceData}

              <CartesianGrid
                strokeDasharray="3 3"

              />

                dataKey="metric"
              />

                allowDecimals={false}
              />

                dataKey="value"

                fill="#16a34a"
                radius={[8, 8, 0, 0]}
                maxBarSize={75}
              >
                <LabelList
                  dataKey="value"
                  position="top"
                />
              </Bar>
            </BarChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </div>

        <div className="rp-chart-insight">
          <FaCheckCircle />

          <span>
            This report gives an immediate view of
            today's productivity and helps monitor
            daily activity targets.
          </span>
        </div>
      </ChartCard>

    </div>
  );
