export default function Reports() {

// src/pages/Settings/Settings.jsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import './Settings.css';
import {
  FiUser,
  FiLock,
  FiBell,
  FiPhone,
  FiFileText,
  FiInfo,
  FiSave,
  FiEdit2,
  FiCheck,
  FiX,
  FiBriefcase,
  FiUsers,
  FiShield,
  FiSliders,
  FiRefreshCw,
  FiPlus,
  FiTrash2,
  FiArrowLeft,
} from 'react-icons/fi';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';

const Settings = ({ activeTab: propActiveTab, onNavigate }) => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState(propActiveTab || 'profile');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [saveSuccess, setSaveSuccess] = useState(false);

  // Update active tab when prop changes (from sidebar navigation)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (propActiveTab) {
      setActiveTab(propActiveTab);
    }
  }, [propActiveTab]);

  // --- Company Profile ---
  const [companyProfile, setCompanyProfile] = useState({
    name: 'Indocampus Tech Private Limited',
    email: 'indocampuspvt@gmail.com',
    phone: '+91 98765 43210',
    address: 'Chennai, Tamil Nadu, India',
    website: 'www.indocampus.com',
    gst: '33AABCU1234D1Z1',
    pan: 'AABCU1234D',
  });

  // --- User Management ---
  const [users, setUsers] = useState([
    { id: 1, name: 'Aravinda Raj', email: 'aravinda@example.com', role: 'Admin', status: 'Active', lastActive: '2026-07-03' },
    { id: 2, name: 'Priya Sharma', email: 'priya@example.com', role: 'Agent', status: 'Active', lastActive: '2026-07-02' },
    { id: 3, name: 'Karthik Kumar', email: 'karthik@example.com', role: 'Agent', status: 'Inactive', lastActive: '2026-06-28' },
  ]);

  // --- Security ---
  const [securitySettings, setSecuritySettings] = useState({
    twoFactorAuth: true,
    sessionTimeout: 30,
    passwordPolicy: 'medium',
    loginAttempts: 5,
    ipWhitelisting: false,
  });

  // --- Notifications ---
  const [notificationSettings, setNotificationSettings] = useState({
    emailNotifications: true,
    pushNotifications: true,
    leadAssigned: true,
    followUpReminders: true,
    paymentReceived: true,
    quotationApproved: true,
    agreementSigned: true,
    systemUpdates: false,
  });

  // --- Call Settings ---
  const [callSettings, setCallSettings] = useState({
    autoDial: true,
    callRecording: true,
    maxCallDuration: 300,
    callTimeout: 60,
    disableCallerId: false,
    defaultCallerId: '+91 98765 43210',
  });

  // --- Quotation Settings ---
  const [quotationSettings, setQuotationSettings] = useState({
    defaultValidity: 30,
    autoNumbering: true,
    prefix: 'QUO-',
    suffix: '',
    termsAndConditions: 'Payment due within 15 days of invoice date.',
    footerText: 'Thank you for your business!',
  });

  // --- API Integration ---
  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchSettings = async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      try {
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 500));
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error fetching settings:', error);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };
    fetchSettings();
  }, []);

  const handleSave = async (section) => {
    setSaving(true);
    setSaveSuccess(false);
    try {
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 800));
      setSaveSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => setSaveSuccess(false), 3000);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error saving settings:', error);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const toggleSwitch = (setting, value, setter) => {
    setter(prev => ({ ...prev, [setting]: !value }));
  };

  const updateField = (setter, field, value) => {
    setter(prev => ({ ...prev, [field]: value }));
  };

  // Tab configuration - these match sidebar sub-menu IDs
  const tabItems = [
    { id: 'profile', label: 'Company Profile', icon: FiBriefcase },
    { id: 'users', label: 'User Management', icon: FiUsers },
    { id: 'security', label: 'Security', icon: FiShield },
    { id: 'notifications', label: 'Notifications', icon: FiBell },
    { id: 'calls', label: 'Call Settings', icon: FiPhone },
    { id: 'quotations', label: 'Quotation Settings', icon: FiFileText },
    { id: 'about', label: 'About', icon: FiInfo },
  ];

  // Handle tab change
  const handleTabChange = (tabId) => {
    setActiveTab(tabId);
    if (onNavigate) {
      onNavigate(`settings-${tabId}`);
    }
  };

  // Go back to main settings page
  const handleBackToSettings = () => {
    if (onNavigate) {
      onNavigate('settings');
    }
  };

  // Get current tab label
  const currentTabLabel = tabItems.find(tab => tab.id === activeTab)?.label || 'Settings';

  const renderTabContent = () => {
    switch (activeTab) {
      case 'profile':
        return renderCompanyProfile();
      case 'users':
        return renderUserManagement();
      case 'security':
        return renderSecurity();
      case 'notifications':
        return renderNotifications();
      case 'calls':
        return renderCallSettings();
      case 'quotations':
        return renderQuotationSettings();
      case 'about':
        return renderAbout();
      default:
        return renderCompanyProfile();
    }
  };

  // --- Company Profile Render ---
  const renderCompanyProfile = () => (
    <div className="settings-section">
      <div className="section-header">
        <h3>Company Profile</h3>
        <p className="section-desc">Manage your company information and branding</p>
      </div>
      <div className="settings-form">
        <div className="form-grid">
          <div className="form-group">
            <label>Company Name</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={companyProfile.name}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(setCompanyProfile, 'name', e.target.value)}
              className="form-input"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="form-group">
            <label>Email Address</label>
            <input
              type="email"
              value={companyProfile.email}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(setCompanyProfile, 'email', e.target.value)}
              className="form-input"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="form-group">
            <label>Phone Number</label>
            <input
              type="tel"
              value={companyProfile.phone}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(setCompanyProfile, 'phone', e.target.value)}
              className="form-input"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="form-group">
            <label>Website</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={companyProfile.website}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(setCompanyProfile, 'website', e.target.value)}
              className="form-input"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="form-group full-width">
            <label>Address</label>
            <textarea
              value={companyProfile.address}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(setCompanyProfile, 'address', e.target.value)}
              className="form-input"
              rows="2"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="form-group">
            <label>GST Number</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={companyProfile.gst}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(setCompanyProfile, 'gst', e.target.value)}
              className="form-input"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="form-group">
            <label>PAN Number</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={companyProfile.pan}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(setCompanyProfile, 'pan', e.target.value)}
              className="form-input"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          className="btn-primary settings-save-btn"
          onClick={() => handleSave('profile')}
          disabled={saving}
        >
          <FiSave /> {saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Company Profile'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  // --- User Management Render ---
  const renderUserManagement = () => (
    <div className="settings-section">
      <div className="section-header">
        <h3>User Management</h3>
        <p className="section-desc">Manage users and their access permissions</p>
        <button className="btn-primary btn-sm">
          <FiPlus /> Add User
        </button>
      </div>
      <div className="users-table-wrapper">
        <table className="settings-table">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Name</th>
              <th>Email</th>
              <th>Role</th>
              <th>Status</th>
              <th>Last Active</th>
              <th>Actions</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {users.map((user) => (
              <tr key={user.id}>
                <td className="user-name-cell">
                  <div className="user-avatar-small">
                    {user.name.split(' ').map(n => n[0]).join('')}
                  </div>
                  {user.name}
                </td>
                <td>{user.email}</td>
                <td>
                  <span className={`role-badge role-${user.role.toLowerCase()}`}>
                    {user.role}
                  </span>
                </td>
                <td>
                  <span className={`status-badge status-${user.status.toLowerCase()}`}>
                    {user.status}
                  </span>
                </td>
                <td>{user.lastActive}</td>
                <td>
                  <div className="action-buttons">
                    <button className="btn-icon" title="Edit">
                      <FiEdit2 />
                    </button>
                    <button className="btn-icon" title="Delete">
                      <FiTrash2 />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  // --- Security Render ---
  const renderSecurity = () => (
    <div className="settings-section">
      <div className="section-header">
        <h3>Security Settings</h3>
        <p className="section-desc">Configure security policies and authentication</p>
      </div>
      <div className="settings-form">
        <div className="security-grid">
          <div className="security-item">
            <div className="security-item-left">
              <div className="security-icon-wrapper">
                <FiShield />
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="security-label">Two-Factor Authentication</div>
                <div className="security-desc">Require 2FA for all admin users</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <button
              className={`toggle-btn ${securitySettings.twoFactorAuth ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => toggleSwitch('twoFactorAuth', securitySettings.twoFactorAuth, setSecuritySettings)}
            >
              {securitySettings.twoFactorAuth ? <FiCheck /> : <FiX />}
              {securitySettings.twoFactorAuth ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled'}
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="security-item">
            <div className="security-item-left">
              <div className="security-icon-wrapper">
                <FiLock />
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="security-label">Session Timeout</div>
                <div className="security-desc">Auto logout after inactivity</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="security-input-group">
              <input
                type="number"
                value={securitySettings.sessionTimeout}
                onChange={(e) => updateField(setSecuritySettings, 'sessionTimeout', parseInt(e.target.value))}
                className="form-input"
                style={{ width: '80px' }}
              />
              <span className="input-suffix">minutes</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="security-item">
            <div className="security-item-left">
              <div className="security-icon-wrapper">
                <FiSliders />
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="security-label">Password Policy</div>
                <div className="security-desc">Minimum password strength requirements</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <select
              value={securitySettings.passwordPolicy}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(setSecuritySettings, 'passwordPolicy', e.target.value)}
              className="form-input"
              style={{ width: '160px' }}
            >
              <option value="low">Low</option>
              <option value="medium">Medium</option>
              <option value="high">High</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="security-item">
            <div className="security-item-left">
              <div className="security-icon-wrapper">
                <FiRefreshCw />
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="security-label">Login Attempts</div>
                <div className="security-desc">Max failed attempts before lockout</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="security-input-group">
              <input
                type="number"
                value={securitySettings.loginAttempts}
                onChange={(e) => updateField(setSecuritySettings, 'loginAttempts', parseInt(e.target.value))}
                className="form-input"
                style={{ width: '80px' }}
              />
              <span className="input-suffix">attempts</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          className="btn-primary settings-save-btn"
          onClick={() => handleSave('security')}
          disabled={saving}
        >
          <FiSave /> {saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Security Settings'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  // --- Notifications Render ---
  const renderNotifications = () => (
    <div className="settings-section">
      <div className="section-header">
        <h3>Notification Settings</h3>
        <p className="section-desc">Configure what notifications you receive and how</p>
      </div>
      <div className="settings-form">
        <div className="notification-grid">
          <div className="notification-group">
            <h4 className="notification-group-title">Channels</h4>
            <div className="notification-item">
              <span>Email Notifications</span>
              <button
                className={`toggle-small ${notificationSettings.emailNotifications ? 'active' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => toggleSwitch('emailNotifications', notificationSettings.emailNotifications, setNotificationSettings)}
              >
                <span className="toggle-track">
                  <span className="toggle-thumb" />
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="notification-item">
              <span>Push Notifications</span>
              <button
                className={`toggle-small ${notificationSettings.pushNotifications ? 'active' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => toggleSwitch('pushNotifications', notificationSettings.pushNotifications, setNotificationSettings)}
              >
                <span className="toggle-track">
                  <span className="toggle-thumb" />
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="notification-group">
            <h4 className="notification-group-title">Events</h4>
            <div className="notification-item">
              <span>Lead Assigned</span>
              <button
                className={`toggle-small ${notificationSettings.leadAssigned ? 'active' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => toggleSwitch('leadAssigned', notificationSettings.leadAssigned, setNotificationSettings)}
              >
                <span className="toggle-track">
                  <span className="toggle-thumb" />
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="notification-item">
              <span>Follow-up Reminders</span>
              <button
                className={`toggle-small ${notificationSettings.followUpReminders ? 'active' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => toggleSwitch('followUpReminders', notificationSettings.followUpReminders, setNotificationSettings)}
              >
                <span className="toggle-track">
                  <span className="toggle-thumb" />
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="notification-item">
              <span>Payment Received</span>
              <button
                className={`toggle-small ${notificationSettings.paymentReceived ? 'active' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => toggleSwitch('paymentReceived', notificationSettings.paymentReceived, setNotificationSettings)}
              >
                <span className="toggle-track">
                  <span className="toggle-thumb" />
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="notification-item">
              <span>Quotation Approved</span>
              <button
                className={`toggle-small ${notificationSettings.quotationApproved ? 'active' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => toggleSwitch('quotationApproved', notificationSettings.quotationApproved, setNotificationSettings)}
              >
                <span className="toggle-track">
                  <span className="toggle-thumb" />
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="notification-item">
              <span>Agreement Signed</span>
              <button
                className={`toggle-small ${notificationSettings.agreementSigned ? 'active' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => toggleSwitch('agreementSigned', notificationSettings.agreementSigned, setNotificationSettings)}
              >
                <span className="toggle-track">
                  <span className="toggle-thumb" />
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="notification-item">
              <span>System Updates</span>
              <button
                className={`toggle-small ${notificationSettings.systemUpdates ? 'active' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => toggleSwitch('systemUpdates', notificationSettings.systemUpdates, setNotificationSettings)}
              >
                <span className="toggle-track">
                  <span className="toggle-thumb" />
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          className="btn-primary settings-save-btn"
          onClick={() => handleSave('notifications')}
          disabled={saving}
        >
          <FiSave /> {saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Notification Settings'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  // --- Call Settings Render ---
  const renderCallSettings = () => (
    <div className="settings-section">
      <div className="section-header">
        <h3>Call Settings</h3>
        <p className="section-desc">Configure call handling and telephony preferences</p>
      </div>
      <div className="settings-form">
        <div className="call-grid">
          <div className="call-item">
            <span>Auto Dial</span>
            <button
              className={`toggle-small ${callSettings.autoDial ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => toggleSwitch('autoDial', callSettings.autoDial, setCallSettings)}
            >
              <span className="toggle-track">
                <span className="toggle-thumb" />
              </span>
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="call-item">
            <span>Call Recording</span>
            <button
              className={`toggle-small ${callSettings.callRecording ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => toggleSwitch('callRecording', callSettings.callRecording, setCallSettings)}
            >
              <span className="toggle-track">
                <span className="toggle-thumb" />
              </span>
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="call-item">
            <span>Disable Caller ID</span>
            <button
              className={`toggle-small ${callSettings.disableCallerId ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => toggleSwitch('disableCallerId', callSettings.disableCallerId, setCallSettings)}
            >
              <span className="toggle-track">
                <span className="toggle-thumb" />
              </span>
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="call-item">
            <label>Max Call Duration</label>
            <div className="call-input-group">
              <input
                type="number"
                value={callSettings.maxCallDuration}
                onChange={(e) => updateField(setCallSettings, 'maxCallDuration', parseInt(e.target.value))}
                className="form-input"
                style={{ width: '100px' }}
              />
              <span className="input-suffix">seconds</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="call-item">
            <label>Call Timeout</label>
            <div className="call-input-group">
              <input
                type="number"
                value={callSettings.callTimeout}
                onChange={(e) => updateField(setCallSettings, 'callTimeout', parseInt(e.target.value))}
                className="form-input"
                style={{ width: '100px' }}
              />
              <span className="input-suffix">seconds</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="call-item">
            <label>Default Caller ID</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={callSettings.defaultCallerId}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(setCallSettings, 'defaultCallerId', e.target.value)}
              className="form-input"
              style={{ width: '200px' }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          className="btn-primary settings-save-btn"
          onClick={() => handleSave('calls')}
          disabled={saving}
        >
          <FiSave /> {saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Call Settings'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  // --- Quotation Settings Render ---
  const renderQuotationSettings = () => (
    <div className="settings-section">
      <div className="section-header">
        <h3>Quotation Settings</h3>
        <p className="section-desc">Configure quotation generation and formatting</p>
      </div>
      <div className="settings-form">
        <div className="quotation-grid">
          <div className="quotation-item">
            <label>Default Validity (days)</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={quotationSettings.defaultValidity}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(setQuotationSettings, 'defaultValidity', parseInt(e.target.value))}
              className="form-input"
              style={{ width: '100px' }}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="quotation-item">
            <span>Auto Numbering</span>
            <button
              className={`toggle-small ${quotationSettings.autoNumbering ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => toggleSwitch('autoNumbering', quotationSettings.autoNumbering, setQuotationSettings)}
            >
              <span className="toggle-track">
                <span className="toggle-thumb" />
              </span>
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="quotation-item">
            <label>Prefix</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={quotationSettings.prefix}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(setQuotationSettings, 'prefix', e.target.value)}
              className="form-input"
              style={{ width: '100px' }}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="quotation-item">
            <label>Suffix</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={quotationSettings.suffix}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(setQuotationSettings, 'suffix', e.target.value)}
              className="form-input"
              style={{ width: '100px' }}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="quotation-item full-width">
            <label>Terms & Conditions</label>
            <textarea
              value={quotationSettings.termsAndConditions}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(setQuotationSettings, 'termsAndConditions', e.target.value)}
              className="form-input"
              rows="3"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="quotation-item full-width">
            <label>Footer Text</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={quotationSettings.footerText}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(setQuotationSettings, 'footerText', e.target.value)}
              className="form-input"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          className="btn-primary settings-save-btn"
          onClick={() => handleSave('quotations')}
          disabled={saving}
        >
          <FiSave /> {saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Quotation Settings'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  // --- About Render ---
  const renderAbout = () => (
    <div className="settings-section">
      <div className="section-header">
        <h3>About IndoCRM</h3>
        <p className="section-desc">Software information and legal details</p>
      </div>
      <div className="about-grid">
        <div className="about-card">
          <div className="about-card-icon">🏢</div>
          <div className="about-card-content">
            <h4>Software Version</h4>
            <p className="version-text">v2.0.0</p>
            <p className="version-date">Released: July 2026</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="about-card">
          <div className="about-card-icon">📄</div>
          <div className="about-card-content">
            <h4>Terms & Conditions</h4>
            <p className="about-text">
              By using this CRM Software, you confirm acceptance of these Terms and Conditions. 
              All business data entered remains the property of the Client.
            </p>
            <button className="btn-outline btn-sm">View Full Terms</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="about-card">
          <div className="about-card-icon">🔒</div>
          <div className="about-card-content">
            <h4>Data Security</h4>
            <p className="about-text">
              Reasonable security measures are implemented; however, no internet-based system is completely secure.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="about-card">
          <div className="about-card-icon">📧</div>
          <div className="about-card-content">
            <h4>Contact Support</h4>
            <p className="about-text">
              For any queries or support, please reach out to us at:
            </p>
            <a href="mailto:indocampuspvt@gmail.com" className="support-email">
              indocampuspvt@gmail.com
            </a>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="about-footer">
        <p>© 2024 Indocampus Tech Private Limited. All rights reserved.</p>
        <p>Governing Law: Laws of India. Disputes subject to competent courts at Chennai, Tamil Nadu.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="settings-page">
      <div className="settings-header">
        <div className="settings-header-left">
          {/* Back button when in sub-tab */}
          {propActiveTab && (
            <button 
              className="btn-back-settings" 
              onClick={handleBackToSettings}
            >
              <FiArrowLeft /> Back
            </button>
          )}
          <div className="settings-title-group">
            <h1 className="page-title">Settings</h1>
            {propActiveTab && (
              <span className="settings-breadcrumb">/ {currentTabLabel}</span>
            )}
          </div>
          <p className="page-subtitle">
            {propActiveTab 
              ? `Configure ${currentTabLabel.toLowerCase()} preferences`
              : 'Configure your CRM application preferences'}
          </p>
        </div>
        {saveSuccess && (
          <div className="save-success-banner">
            <FiCheck /> Settings saved successfully!
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Single Column Content - No Left Tabs */}
      <div className="settings-content-wrapper">
        {loading ? (
          <div className="settings-loading">Loading settings...</div>
        ) : (
          renderTabContent()
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Settings;

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\pages\SuperAdmin\SuperAdmin.jsx =====
// src/pages/SuperAdmin/SuperAdmin.jsx
//
// This file did not exist before, even though src/App.jsx imports it
// (`import SuperAdmin from './pages/SuperAdmin/SuperAdmin';`). That import
// alone is enough to break the whole Vite build with a
// "Failed to resolve import ./pages/SuperAdmin/SuperAdmin" error the moment
// this route is reached — this was one of the "one error after another"
// issues. This component is now implemented and fully wired to the real
// Super Admin backend (SuperAdminController / SuperAdminService), not mock
// data.
import React, { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  FiMapPin, FiUsers, FiTarget, FiUploadCloud, FiPlus, FiEdit2, FiTrash2,
  FiUpload, FiRefreshCw, FiCheckCircle, FiXCircle,
} from 'react-icons/fi';
import StatsCard from '../../components/Cards/StatsCard';
import { superAdminService } from '../../services/superAdminService';
import './SuperAdmin.css';

const emptyEmployee = {
  username: '', password: '', fullName: '', email: '', phone: '', role: 'EMPLOYEE',
};

const Modal = ({ show, title, onClose, children }) => {
  if (!show) return null;
  return (
    <div className="modal-overlay" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="modal-box" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="modal-header">
          <span className="modal-title">{title}</span>
          <button className="modal-close" onClick={onClose}>&times;</button>
        </div>
        <div className="modal-body">{children}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

const EmployeeForm = ({ initial, onSave, onClose, saving }) => {
  const [form, setForm] = useState(initial || emptyEmployee);
  const set = (k, v) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [k]: v }));
  const isEdit = Boolean(initial && initial.id);

  return (
    <div className="lead-form">
      <div className="form-grid-2">
        <div className="form-group">
          <label>Username *</label>
          <input value={form.username} disabled={isEdit}
            onChange={(e) => set('username', e.target.value)} placeholder="login username" />
        </div>
        <div className="form-group">
          <label>{isEdit ? 'New Password (leave blank to keep current)' : 'Password *'}</label>
          <input type="password" value={form.password || ''}
            onChange={(e) => set('password', e.target.value)} placeholder="password" />
        </div>
        <div className="form-group">
          <label>Full Name *</label>
          <input value={form.fullName} onChange={(e) => set('fullName', e.target.value)} placeholder="Full name" />
        </div>
        <div className="form-group">
          <label>Email *</label>
          <input value={form.email} onChange={(e) => set('email', e.target.value)} placeholder="Email" />
        </div>
        <div className="form-group">
          <label>Phone</label>
          <input value={form.phone || ''} onChange={(e) => set('phone', e.target.value)} placeholder="Phone" />
        </div>
        <div className="form-group">
          <label>Role *</label>
          <select value={form.role} onChange={(e) => set('role', e.target.value)}>
            <option value="EMPLOYEE">Employee (Telecaller)</option>
            <option value="SUPER_ADMIN">Super Admin (Manager)</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="form-actions">
        <button className="btn-outline" onClick={onClose} disabled={saving}>Cancel</button>
        <button className="btn-primary" disabled={saving} onClick={() => onSave(form)}>
          {saving ? 'Saving…' : isEdit ? 'Save Changes' : 'Create Employee'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

const SuperAdmin = () => {
  const [stats, setStats] = useState({ totalDistricts: 0, totalEmployees: 0, totalLeads: 0, totalUploads: 0 });
  const [employees, setEmployees] = useState([]);
  const [uploadHistory, setUploadHistory] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);

  const [showEmployeeModal, setShowEmployeeModal] = useState(false);
  const [editingEmployee, setEditingEmployee] = useState(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const [importing, setImporting] = useState(false);
  const [importMsg, setImportMsg] = useState(null);

  const loadAll = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const [statsRes, employeesRes, historyRes] = await Promise.all([
        superAdminService.getDashboardStats(),
        superAdminService.getEmployees(),
        superAdminService.getUploadHistory(),
      ]);
      setStats(statsRes || {});
      setEmployees(Array.isArray(employeesRes) ? employeesRes : []);
      setUploadHistory(Array.isArray(historyRes) ? historyRes : []);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || 'Failed to load Super Admin data');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => { loadAll(); }, [loadAll]);

  const overviewStats = [
    { label: 'Total Districts', value: stats.totalDistricts ?? 0, icon: <FiMapPin size={22} />, color: '#7b1113' },
    { label: 'Total Employees', value: stats.totalEmployees ?? 0, icon: <FiUsers size={22} />, color: '#2563eb' },
    { label: 'Total Leads', value: stats.totalLeads ?? 0, icon: <FiTarget size={22} />, color: '#16a34a' },
    { label: 'Total Uploads', value: stats.totalUploads ?? 0, icon: <FiUploadCloud size={22} />, color: '#7c3aed' },
  ];

  const openCreate = () => { setEditingEmployee(null); setShowEmployeeModal(true); };
  const openEdit = (emp) => {
    setEditingEmployee({
      id: emp.id, username: emp.username, password: '', fullName: emp.fullName,
      email: emp.email, phone: emp.phone, role: emp.role,
    });
    setShowEmployeeModal(true);
  };

  const handleSaveEmployee = async (form) => {
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      if (form.id) {
        const payload = { ...form };
        if (!payload.password) delete payload.password;
        await superAdminService.updateEmployee(form.id, payload);
      } else {
        if (!form.username || !form.password || !form.fullName || !form.email) {
          throw new Error('Username, password, full name and email are required');
        }
        await superAdminService.createEmployee(form);
      }
      setShowEmployeeModal(false);
      await loadAll();
    } catch (err) {
      alert(err.message || 'Failed to save employee');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteEmployee = async (emp) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Delete employee "${emp.fullName}" (${emp.username})? This cannot be undone.`)) return;
    try {
      await superAdminService.deleteEmployee(emp.id);
      await loadAll();
    } catch (err) {
      alert(err.message || 'Failed to delete employee');
    }
  };

  const handleImportFile = async (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setImporting(true);
    setImportMsg(null);
    try {
      const uploadedBy = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('user') || '{}').username || 'super-admin';
      const result = await superAdminService.importLeads(file, uploadedBy);
      setImportMsg({
        type: result.status === 'SUCCESS' ? 'success' : 'warning',
        text: `Imported ${result.importedRecords}/${result.totalRecords} leads${
          result.errors && result.errors.length ? ` (${result.errors.length} row errors)` : ''
        }.`,
      });
      await loadAll();
    } catch (err) {
      setImportMsg({ type: 'error', text: err.message || 'Import failed. CSV files only for now.' });
    } finally {
      setImporting(false);
      e.target.value = '';
    }
  };

  if (loading) return <div className="sa-loading">Loading Super Admin dashboard…</div>;

  return (
    <div className="ad-dashboard">
      {error && (
        <div className="sa-alert sa-alert-error">
          {error}
          <button className="btn-outline sa-retry-btn" onClick={loadAll}><FiRefreshCw /> Retry</button>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="ad-stats-grid">
        {overviewStats.map((s) => (
          <StatsCard key={s.label} icon={s.icon} label={s.label} value={s.value} color={s.color} />
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="ad-charts-row">
        {/* Employees */}
        <div className="ad-card sa-span-2">
          <div className="card-header">
            <span className="card-title">Employees</span>
            <button className="btn-primary sa-small-btn" onClick={openCreate}>
              <FiPlus /> New Employee
            </button>
          </div>
          <table className="ad-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Name</th><th>Username</th><th>Role</th><th>District</th><th>Status</th><th></th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {employees.length === 0 && (
                <tr><td colSpan={6} className="sa-empty-row">No employees yet. Click "New Employee" to add one.</td></tr>
              )}
              {employees.map((emp) => (
                <tr key={emp.id}>
                  <td>{emp.fullName}</td>
                  <td>{emp.username}</td>
                  <td>
                    <span className={`sa-badge ${emp.role === 'SUPER_ADMIN' ? 'sa-badge-purple' : 'sa-badge-blue'}`}>
                      {emp.role}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td>{emp.assignedDistrict || '—'}</td>
                  <td>
                    {emp.enabled
                      ? <span className="sa-badge sa-badge-green"><FiCheckCircle size={12} /> Active</span>
                      : <span className="sa-badge sa-badge-red"><FiXCircle size={12} /> Disabled</span>}
                  </td>
                  <td className="sa-row-actions">
                    <button className="sa-icon-btn" onClick={() => openEdit(emp)}><FiEdit2 /></button>
                    <button className="sa-icon-btn sa-icon-btn-danger" onClick={() => handleDeleteEmployee(emp)}><FiTrash2 /></button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        {/* Import leads */}
        <div className="ad-card">
          <div className="card-header"><span className="card-title">Import Leads (CSV)</span></div>
          <p className="muted-text">
            Upload a CSV with columns: name, mobile, district, email, area.
            The backend currently accepts CSV only.
          </p>
          <label className="sa-upload-dropzone">
            <FiUpload size={22} />
            <span>{importing ? 'Importing…' : 'Choose CSV file'}</span>
            <input type="file" accept=".csv" hidden disabled={importing} onChange={handleImportFile} />
          </label>
          {importMsg && (
            <div className={`sa-alert sa-alert-${importMsg.type}`} style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
              {importMsg.text}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="ad-tables-row">
        <div className="ad-card">
          <div className="card-header"><span className="card-title">Upload History</span></div>
          <table className="ad-table">
            <thead>
              <tr><th>File</th><th>Uploaded By</th><th>Total</th><th>Imported</th><th>Status</th><th>Date</th></tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {uploadHistory.length === 0 && (
                <tr><td colSpan={6} className="sa-empty-row">No uploads yet.</td></tr>
              )}
              {uploadHistory.map((h) => (
                <tr key={h.id}>
                  <td>{h.fileName}</td>
                  <td>{h.uploadedBy}</td>
                  <td>{h.totalRecords}</td>
                  <td>{h.importedRecords}</td>
                  <td>
                    <span className={`sa-badge ${h.status === 'SUCCESS' ? 'sa-badge-green' : 'sa-badge-amber'}`}>
                      {h.status}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td>{h.uploadedAt ? new Date(h.uploadedAt).toLocaleString() : '—'}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Modal show={showEmployeeModal} title={editingEmployee ? 'Edit Employee' : 'New Employee'} onClose={() => setShowEmployeeModal(false)}>
        <EmployeeForm initial={editingEmployee} onSave={handleSaveEmployee} onClose={() => setShowEmployeeModal(false)} saving={saving} />
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
};

export default SuperAdmin;
