  'Chennai', 'Chengalpattu', 'Kanchipuram', 'Coimbatore', 'Madurai', 
  'Trichy', 'Salem', 'Cuddalore', 'Pondicherry', 'Villupuram', 'Thindivanam'
];

// Permissions list
const TELECALLER_PERMISSIONS = [
  { id: 'dashboard', label: 'Dashboard', icon: FiHome },
  { id: 'leads', label: 'Leads', icon: FiTarget },
  { id: 'callpanel', label: 'Call Panel', icon: FiPhone },
  { id: 'clients', label: 'Clients', icon: FiUsers },
  { id: 'followups', label: 'Follow-ups', icon: FiClock },
  { id: 'demo', label: 'Demo', icon: FiVideo },
  { id: 'quotations', label: 'Quotations', icon: FiFileText },
  { id: 'agreements', label: 'Agreements', icon: FiFileText },
  { id: 'payment', label: 'Payment', icon: FiDollarSign },
  { id: 'reports', label: 'Reports', icon: FiBarChart2 },
  { id: 'calendar', label: 'Calendar', icon: FiCalendarIcon },
  { id: 'settings', label: 'Settings', icon: FiSettings },
  { id: 'help', label: 'Help and Support', icon: FiHelpCircle },
  { id: 'leave_permissions', label: 'Leave & Permissions', icon: FiBriefcase },
  { id: 'communications', label: 'Communications', icon: FiGlobe },
];

const PERMISSION_ACTIONS = ['read', 'create', 'edit', 'delete'];

const Telecallers = () => {
  // ===== State =====
  const [telecallers, setTelecallers] = useState([]);
  const [districts, setDistricts] = useState(FALLBACK_DISTRICTS);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [loadingDistricts, setLoadingDistricts] = useState(true);
  const [workTimings, setWorkTimings] = useState(DEFAULT_WORK_TIMINGS);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [showAddModal, setShowAddModal] = useState(false);
  const [editingTelecaller, setEditingTelecaller] = useState(null);
  const [portalTelecaller, setPortalTelecaller] = useState(null);
  const [followUpTelecaller, setFollowUpTelecaller] = useState(null);
  const [visiblePasswords, setVisiblePasswords] = useState({});
  const [selectedDistrict, setSelectedDistrict] = useState('All');
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState('All');
  const [showPerformance, setShowPerformance] = useState(false);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('telecallers');

  // ===== Form state =====
  const getDefaultPermissions = () => {
    return { ...DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS };
  };

  const getDefaultPerformance = () => ({
    callsToday: 0,
    followups: 0,
    conversions: 0,
    revenue: 0,
    attendance: 100,
    schoolsCreated: 0,
    connectedCalls: 0,
    invalidNumbers: 0,
    callsNotConnected: 0,
    alreadyUsingSoftware: 0,
    profileShared: 0,
    schoolClosed: 0,
    noRequirement: 0,
    followupsCompleted: 0,
    interestedLeads: 0,
    demoScheduled: 0,
    demoConducted: 0,
    totalLeads: 0,
  });

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    employeeId: '',
    fullName: '',
    mobile: '',
    email: '',
    username: '',
    password: '',
    role: 'Telecaller',
    districts: [],
    categories: [],
    joiningDate: '',
    status: 'Active',
    target: 100,
    permissions: getDefaultPermissions(),
    performance: getDefaultPerformance(),
    workTiming: { ...DEFAULT_WORK_TIMINGS },
  });

  // ===== Reset form =====
  const resetForm = () => {
    setForm({
      employeeId: '',
      fullName: '',
      mobile: '',
      email: '',
      username: '',
      password: '',
      role: 'Telecaller',
      districts: [],
      categories: [],
      joiningDate: '',
      status: 'Active',
      target: 100,
      permissions: getDefaultPermissions(),
      performance: getDefaultPerformance(),
      workTiming: { ...DEFAULT_WORK_TIMINGS },
    });
    setEditingTelecaller(null);
  };

  // ===== API calls =====
  const fetchTelecallers = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const data = await telecallerService.getAll();
      setTelecallers(data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to fetch telecallers:', error);
      alert('Error loading telecallers: ' + error.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const fetchDistricts = async () => {
    try {
      setLoadingDistricts(true);
      const data = await superAdminService.getDistricts();
      
      if (data && Array.isArray(data) && data.length > 0) {
        const districtNames = data.map(d => d.name || d);
        setDistricts(districtNames);
      } else {
        setDistricts(FALLBACK_DISTRICTS);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to fetch districts:', error);
      setDistricts(FALLBACK_DISTRICTS);
    } finally {
      setLoadingDistricts(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchTelecallers();
    fetchDistricts();
  }, []);

  // ===== CRUD handlers =====
  const handleSave = async (formData) => {
    if (!formData.fullName || !formData.mobile || !formData.username) {
      alert('Full Name, Mobile and Username are required.');
      return;
    }

    try {
      const saveData = {
        employeeId: formData.id || formData.employeeId || '',
        fullName: formData.fullName || formData.name,
        mobile: formData.mobile,
        email: formData.email || '',
        username: formData.username,
        password: formData.password || '',
        role: formData.role || 'Telecaller',
        districts: [],
        categories: [],
        joiningDate: formData.joiningDate || '',
        status: formData.status || 'Active',
        target: formData.target || 100,
        permissions: formData.permissions || getDefaultPermissions(),
        performance: formData.performance || getDefaultPerformance(),
        workTiming: formData.workTiming || { ...DEFAULT_WORK_TIMINGS },
      };

      if (editingTelecaller) {
        await telecallerService.update(editingTelecaller.id, saveData);
        alert('Telecaller updated successfully.');
      } else {
        await telecallerService.create(saveData);
        alert('Telecaller created successfully.');
      }
      await fetchTelecallers();
      setShowAddModal(false);
      resetForm();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Save error:', error);
      alert('Error saving telecaller: ' + error.message);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if (!window.confirm('Delete this telecaller? This cannot be undone.')) return;
    try {
      await telecallerService.delete(id);
      await fetchTelecallers();
    } catch (error) {
      alert('Error deleting telecaller: ' + error.message);
    }
  };

  const handleEditClick = (telecaller) => {
    setEditingTelecaller(telecaller);
    const { password, ...rest } = telecaller;
    setForm({
      ...rest,
      fullName: telecaller.fullName || telecaller.name || '',
      password: '',
      permissions: telecaller.permissions || getDefaultPermissions(),
      performance: telecaller.performance || getDefaultPerformance(),
      workTiming: telecaller.workTiming || { ...DEFAULT_WORK_TIMINGS },
    });
    setShowAddModal(true);
  };

  const handleAddClick = () => {
    resetForm();
    setShowAddModal(true);
  };

  const resetPassword = async (id) => {
    const newPass = prompt('Enter new temporary password (leave blank to generate):');
    if (newPass === null) return;
    const password = newPass.trim() || Math.random().toString(36).slice(-8);
    try {
      await telecallerService.resetPassword(id, password);
      alert(`Password reset successfully!\nNew password: ${password}`);
      await fetchTelecallers();
    } catch (error) {
      alert('Error resetting password: ' + error.message);
    }
  };

  const toggleStatus = async (id) => {
    try {
      await telecallerService.toggleStatus(id);
      await fetchTelecallers();
    } catch (error) {
      alert('Error toggling status: ' + error.message);
    }
  };

  // ===== Utility functions =====
  const togglePasswordVisibility = (id) => {
    setVisiblePasswords((v) => ({ ...v, [id]: !v[id] }));
  };

  const getPermissionSummary = (permissions) => {
    if (!permissions) return 'No permissions set';
    let total = 0, granted = 0;
    TELECALLER_PERMISSIONS.forEach((p) => {
      if (permissions[p.id]) {
        PERMISSION_ACTIONS.forEach((action) => {
          total++;
          if (permissions[p.id][action]) granted++;
        });
      }
    });
    return `${granted}/${total}`;
  };

  const handleWorkTimingsSave = (newTimings) => {
    setWorkTimings(newTimings);
  };

  // ===== Filtering =====
  const filteredTelecallers = telecallers.filter((t) => {
    const q = search.toLowerCase();
    const matchesSearch =
      (t.fullName || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      (t.mobile || '').includes(search) ||
      (t.email || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      (t.employeeId || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      (t.username || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
    const matchesDistrict =
      selectedDistrict === 'All' || (t.districts || []).includes(selectedDistrict);
    const matchesCategory =
      selectedCategory === 'All' || (t.categories || []).includes(selectedCategory);
    return matchesSearch && matchesDistrict && matchesCategory;
  });

  // ===== Stats =====
  const totalTelecallers = telecallers.length;
  const activeTelecallers = telecallers.filter((t) => t.status === 'Active').length;
  const inactiveTelecallers = telecallers.filter((t) => t.status === 'Inactive').length;
  const totalConversions = telecallers.reduce(
    (sum, t) => sum + (t.performance?.conversions || 0),
    0
  );
  const totalRevenue = telecallers.reduce(
    (sum, t) => sum + (t.performance?.revenue || 0),
    0
  );

  const currency = (n) =>
    '₹' + (n >= 100000 ? (n / 100000).toFixed(1) + 'L' : (n || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN'));

  // ===== Render =====
  return (
    <div className="tc-page">
      <div className="tc-header">
        <div>
          <h1 className="page-title">📞 Telecaller Management</h1>
          <p className="page-subtitle">
            Manage your telecalling team, accounts, assignments and performance
          </p>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          <button
            className="primary-btn"
            onClick={() => setShowPerformance(!showPerformance)}
            style={{
              background: showPerformance ? '#6b7280' : '#7b1113',
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              gap: '8px',
            }}
          >
            <FiBarChart2 /> {showPerformance ? 'Hide Performance' : 'View Performance'}
          </button>
          <button className="primary-btn" onClick={handleAddClick}>
            <FiUserPlus /> Add Telecaller
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Stats Cards */}
      <div className="tc-stats-grid">
        <div className="tc-stat-card">
          <div className="tc-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#e0e7ff', color: '#4338ca' }}>
            <FiUsers size={24} />
          </div>
          <div className="tc-stat-content">
            <div className="tc-stat-value">{totalTelecallers}</div>
            <div className="tc-stat-label">Total Telecallers</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="tc-stat-card">
          <div className="tc-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#dcfce7', color: '#16a34a' }}>
            <FiCheckCircle size={24} />
          </div>
          <div className="tc-stat-content">
            <div className="tc-stat-value">{activeTelecallers}</div>
            <div className="tc-stat-label">Active</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="tc-stat-card">
          <div className="tc-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#fee2e2', color: '#dc2626' }}>
            <FiXCircle size={24} />
          </div>
          <div className="tc-stat-content">
            <div className="tc-stat-value">{inactiveTelecallers}</div>
            <div className="tc-stat-label">Inactive</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="tc-stat-card">
          <div className="tc-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#fef3c7', color: '#b45309' }}>
            <FiTarget size={24} />
          </div>
          <div className="tc-stat-content">
            <div className="tc-stat-value">{totalConversions}</div>
            <div className="tc-stat-label">Total Conversions</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="tc-stat-card">
          <div className="tc-stat-icon" style={{ background: '#e0f2fe', color: '#0369a1' }}>
            <FiBarChart2 size={24} />
          </div>
          <div className="tc-stat-content">
            <div className="tc-stat-value">{currency(totalRevenue)}</div>
            <div className="tc-stat-label">Total Revenue</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Tabs */}
      <div className="tc-tabs">
        <button
          className={`tc-tab ${activeTab === 'telecallers' ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('telecallers')}
        >
          <FiUsers /> Telecallers
        </button>
        <button
          className={`tc-tab ${activeTab === 'worktimings' ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('worktimings')}
        >
          <FiClockIcon /> Work Timings
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Tab Content */}
      {activeTab === 'worktimings' ? (
        <WorkTimings workTimings={workTimings} onSave={handleWorkTimingsSave} />
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* Performance Details Section */}
          {showPerformance && (
            <TelecallerPerformanceDetails telecallers={telecallers} />
          )}

          {/* Search and Filters */}
          <div className="tc-toolbar">
            <div className="search-box">
              <FiSearch />
              <input
                placeholder="Search by name, mobile, email or ID..."
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              />
            </div>
            <select
              className="filter-select"
              value={selectedDistrict}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedDistrict(e.target.value)}
            >
              <option value="All">All Districts</option>
              {districts.map((d) => (
                <option key={d} value={d}>{d}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <select
              className="filter-select"
              value={selectedCategory}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedCategory(e.target.value)}
            >
              <option value="All">All Categories</option>
              {CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
                <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Telecallers Grid */}
          {loading ? (
            <div className="empty-state" style={{ padding: '40px', textAlign: 'center' }}>
              <p>Loading telecallers...</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="tc-grid">
              {filteredTelecallers.map((t) => (
                <div key={t.id} className="tc-card">
                  <div className="tc-card-header">
                    <div className="tc-card-id">{t.employeeId}</div>
                    <div className={`tc-card-status ${t.status === 'Active' ? 'status-active' : 'status-inactive'}`}>
                      {t.status === 'Active' ? <FiCheckCircle size={14} /> : <FiXCircle size={14} />}
                      {t.status}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="tc-card-body">
                    <div className="tc-card-name">{t.fullName}</div>
                    <div className="tc-card-role-badge">{t.role}</div>

                    <div className="tc-card-details">
                      <div className="tc-card-detail">
                        <span className="tc-card-detail-label"><FiPhone size={14} /> Mobile</span>
                        <span>{t.mobile}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="tc-card-detail">
                        <span className="tc-card-detail-label"><FiMail size={14} /> Email</span>
                        <span>{t.email}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="tc-card-detail">
                        <span className="tc-card-detail-label"><FiUser size={14} /> Username</span>
                        <span>{t.username}</span>
                      </div>
                      
                      <div className="tc-card-detail">
                        <span className="tc-card-detail-label"><FiKey size={14} /> Password</span>
                        <div className="password-cell">
                          <span>••••••••</span>
                          <button className="icon-btn-sm" onClick={() => togglePasswordVisibility(t.id)}>
                            {visiblePasswords[t.id] ? <FiEyeOff size={14} /> : <FiEye size={14} />}
                          </button>
                          <button
                            className="icon-btn-sm"
                            onClick={() => resetPassword(t.id)}
                            style={{ marginLeft: '4px', color: '#2563eb' }}
                            title="Reset Password"
                          >
                            <FiKey size={14} /> Reset
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      
                      <div className="tc-card-detail">
                        <span className="tc-card-detail-label"><FiShield size={14} /> Permissions</span>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#7b1113', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                          {getPermissionSummary(t.permissions)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="tc-card-detail">
                        <span className="tc-card-detail-label"><FiAward size={14} /> Target</span>
                        <span>{t.target} leads</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="tc-card-detail">
                        <span className="tc-card-detail-label"><FiCalendar size={14} /> Joining</span>
                        <span>{t.joiningDate}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="tc-card-detail">
                        <span className="tc-card-detail-label"><FiMapPin size={14} /> Districts</span>
                        <div className="chip-list">
                          {(t.districts || []).map((d) => (
                            <span key={d} className="chip chip-district">{d}</span>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="tc-card-detail">
                        <span className="tc-card-detail-label">Categories</span>
                        <div className="chip-list">
                          {(t.categories || []).map((c) => (
                            <span key={c} className="chip chip-category">{c}</span>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="tc-card-actions">
                    <button className="tc-action-btn view-btn" onClick={() => setPortalTelecaller(t)}>
                      <FiEye /> View
                    </button>
                    <button className="tc-action-btn view-btn" onClick={() => setFollowUpTelecaller(t)}>
                      <FiClock /> Follow-ups
                    </button>
                    <button className="tc-action-btn edit-btn" onClick={() => handleEditClick(t)}>
                      <FiEdit2 /> Edit
                    </button>
                    <button className="tc-action-btn password-btn" onClick={() => resetPassword(t.id)}>
                      <FiKey /> Reset Password
                    </button>
                    <button className="tc-action-btn status-btn" onClick={() => toggleStatus(t.id)}>
                      {t.status === 'Active' ? <FiLock /> : <FiUnlock />}
                      {t.status === 'Active' ? 'Deactivate' : 'Activate'}
                    </button>
                    <button className="tc-action-btn delete-btn" onClick={() => handleDelete(t.id)}>
                      <FiTrash2 /> Delete
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && filteredTelecallers.length === 0 && (
            <div className="empty-state">
              <p>No telecallers found matching your filters.</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {/* Add/Edit Modal */}
      {showAddModal && (
        <div 
          className="modal-overlay" 
          onClick={() => {
            setShowAddModal(false);
            resetForm();
          }}
          style={{
            position: 'fixed',
            top: 0,
            left: 0,
            right: 0,
            bottom: 0,
            backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6)',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            zIndex: 9999,
            padding: '20px',
          }}
        >
          <div 
            className="modal-content"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            style={{
              backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
              borderRadius: '16px',
              width: '100%',
              maxWidth: '900px',
              maxHeight: '90vh',
              overflow: 'auto',
              boxShadow: '0 20px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3)',
            }}
          >
            <AddTelecaller
              telecallers={telecallers}
              editing={editingTelecaller}
              onSave={handleSave}
              onCancel={() => {
                setShowAddModal(false);
                resetForm();
              }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* View Portal Modal */}
      {portalTelecaller && (
        <TelecallerPortal
          telecaller={portalTelecaller}
          onClose={() => setPortalTelecaller(null)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Telecaller Follow-ups Modal */}
      {followUpTelecaller && (
        <TelecallerFollowUps
          telecaller={followUpTelecaller}
          onClose={() => setFollowUpTelecaller(null)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default Telecallers;

export const nextEmpId = (list) => {
  const max = list.reduce((m, t) => Math.max(m, parseInt(t.id.replace('EMP-', ''), 10) || 0), 100);
  return `EMP-${max + 1}`;
};

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\pages\Telecallers\UserAccounts.jsx =====
// src/pages/Telecallers/UserAccounts.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FiKey, FiEye, FiEyeOff, FiLock, FiUnlock } from 'react-icons/fi';
import { ROLES } from './telecallersData';

const UserAccounts = ({ telecallers, onUpdate, onResetPassword }) => {
  const [visiblePasswords, setVisiblePasswords] = useState({});

  const togglePasswordVisibility = (id) => {
    setVisiblePasswords((v) => ({ ...v, [id]: !v[id] }));
  };

  return (
    <div className="tc-section">
      <p className="tc-section-hint">Manage login credentials, roles and account status for every telecaller.</p>
      <div className="tc-table-card">
        <table className="tc-table">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Employee Name</th>
              <th>Username</th>
              <th>Password</th>
              <th>Role</th>
              <th>Status</th>
              <th>Actions</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {telecallers.map((t) => (
              <tr key={t.id}>
                <td className="cell-name">{t.name}</td>
                <td>{t.username}</td>
                <td>
                  <div className="password-cell">
                    <span>{visiblePasswords[t.id] ? t.password : '•'.repeat(Math.max(t.password.length, 8))}</span>
                    <button className="icon-btn-sm" onClick={() => togglePasswordVisibility(t.id)}>
                      {visiblePasswords[t.id] ? <FiEyeOff /> : <FiEye />}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </td>
                <td>
                  <select value={t.role} onChange={(e) => onUpdate(t.id, { role: e.target.value })}>
                    {ROLES.map((r) => <option key={r}>{r}</option>)}
                  </select>
                </td>
                <td><span className={`status-pill ${t.status === 'Active' ? 'status-active' : 'status-inactive'}`}>{t.status}</span></td>
                <td>
                  <div className="row-actions">
                    <button title="Reset Password" onClick={() => onResetPassword(t.id)}><FiKey /></button>
                    <button
                      title={t.status === 'Active' ? 'Disable Login' : 'Enable Login'}
                      className={t.status === 'Active' ? 'btn-deactivate' : 'btn-activate'}
                      onClick={() => onUpdate(t.id, { status: t.status === 'Active' ? 'Inactive' : 'Active' })}
                    >
                      {t.status === 'Active' ? <FiLock /> : <FiUnlock />}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default UserAccounts;

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\pages\Telecallers\WorkTimings.jsx =====
// src/pages/Telecallers/WorkTimings.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FiClock, FiCoffee, FiSun, FiLock, FiUnlock, FiAlertCircle } from 'react-icons/fi';

const WorkTimings = ({ workTimings, onSave }) => {
  const [form, setForm] = useState(workTimings || {
    shiftName: 'Morning Shift',
    shiftStart: '09:00',
    shiftEnd: '18:00',
    graceTime: 15,
    workingHours: 8,
    enableLunch: true,
    lunchStart: '13:00',
    lunchEnd: '13:45',
    enableTeaBreak: true,
    morningTeaStart: '10:30',
    morningTeaEnd: '10:45',
    eveningTeaStart: '16:30',
    eveningTeaEnd: '16:45',
    lockDuringBreak: true,
    showBreakTimer: true,
    autoResume: true,
  });

  const handleSave = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    onSave(form);
    alert('Work timings updated for all telecallers.');
  };

  // Helper function to check if a break is currently active
  const isBreakActive = (startTime, endTime) => {
    const now = new Date();
    const currentMinutes = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
    
    const [startHours, startMinutes] = startTime.split(':').map(Number);
    const [endHours, endMinutes] = endTime.split(':').map(Number);
    
    const startTotal = startHours * 60 + startMinutes;
    const endTotal = endHours * 60 + endMinutes;
    
    return currentMinutes >= startTotal && currentMinutes <= endTotal;
  };

  return (
    <div className="tc-section">
      <div className="tc-section-header">
        <h3 className="tc-section-title">⏰ Work Timings & Break Management</h3>
        <p className="tc-section-hint">Configure shift hours, lunch breaks, tea breaks, and dashboard lock policies for all telecallers.</p>
      </div>

      <form className="tc-work-timing-form" onSubmit={handleSave}>
        {/* Shift Details */}
        <div className="timing-section">
          <div className="timing-section-header">
            <FiClock className="section-icon" />
            <h4>Shift Details</h4>
          </div>
          <div className="form-grid">
            <div className="form-field">
              <label>Shift Name</label>
              <select 
                value={form.shiftName} 
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, shiftName: e.target.value })}
              >
                <option>Morning Shift</option>
                <option>Afternoon Shift</option>
                <option>Evening Shift</option>
                <option>Night Shift</option>
                <option>Flexible Shift</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div className="form-field">
              <label>Shift Start</label>
              <input 
                type="time" 
                value={form.shiftStart} 
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, shiftStart: e.target.value })}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="form-field">
              <label>Shift End</label>
              <input 
                type="time" 
                value={form.shiftEnd} 
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, shiftEnd: e.target.value })}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="form-field">
              <label>Grace Time (minutes)</label>
              <input 
                type="number" 
                value={form.graceTime} 
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, graceTime: Number(e.target.value) })}
                min="0"
                max="60"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="form-field">
              <label>Working Hours</label>
              <input 
                type="number" 
                value={form.workingHours} 
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, workingHours: Number(e.target.value) })}
                min="4"
                max="12"
                step="0.5"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Lunch Break */}
        <div className="timing-section">
          <div className="timing-section-header">
            <FiSun className="section-icon" />
            <h4>Lunch Break</h4>
          </div>
          <div className="form-grid">
            <div className="form-field">
              <label>Enable Lunch Break</label>
              <select 
                value={form.enableLunch ? 'Yes' : 'No'} 
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, enableLunch: e.target.value === 'Yes' })}
              >
                <option>Yes</option>
                <option>No</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div className="form-field">
              <label>Lunch Start</label>
              <input 
                type="time" 
                value={form.lunchStart} 
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, lunchStart: e.target.value })}
                disabled={!form.enableLunch}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="form-field">
              <label>Lunch End</label>
              <input 
                type="time" 
                value={form.lunchEnd} 
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, lunchEnd: e.target.value })}
                disabled={!form.enableLunch}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="form-field">
              <label>Duration</label>
              <input 
                type="text" 
                value={(() => {
                  if (!form.enableLunch) return 'Disabled';
                  const start = new Date(`2000-01-01T${form.lunchStart}:00`);
                  const end = new Date(`2000-01-01T${form.lunchEnd}:00`);
                  const diff = (end - start) / 60000;
                  const hours = Math.floor(diff / 60);
                  const minutes = diff % 60;
                  return hours > 0 ? `${hours}h ${minutes}m` : `${minutes}m`;
                })()}
                disabled
                style={{ background: '#f5f6fa' }}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Tea Breaks */}
        <div className="timing-section">
          <div className="timing-section-header">
            <FiCoffee className="section-icon" />
            <h4>Tea Breaks</h4>
          </div>
          <div className="form-grid">
            <div className="form-field">
              <label>Enable Tea Breaks</label>
              <select 
                value={form.enableTeaBreak ? 'Yes' : 'No'} 
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, enableTeaBreak: e.target.value === 'Yes' })}
              >
                <option>Yes</option>
                <option>No</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div className="form-field">
              <label>Morning Tea Start</label>
              <input 
                type="time" 
                value={form.morningTeaStart} 
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, morningTeaStart: e.target.value })}
                disabled={!form.enableTeaBreak}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="form-field">
              <label>Morning Tea End</label>
              <input 
                type="time" 
                value={form.morningTeaEnd} 
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, morningTeaEnd: e.target.value })}
                disabled={!form.enableTeaBreak}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="form-field">
              <label>Evening Tea Start</label>
              <input

              <input

                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, eveningTeaEnd: e.target.value })}
                disabled={!form.enableTeaBreak}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Dashboard Lock */}
        <div className="timing-section">
          <div className="timing-section-header">
            <FiLock className="section-icon" />
            <h4>Dashboard Lock Policies</h4>
          </div>
          <div className="form-grid">

              >
                <option>Yes</option>
                <option>No</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div className="form-field">
              <label>Show Break Timer on Dashboard</label>
              <select 
                value={form.showBreakTimer ? 'Yes' : 'No'}

              >
                <option>Yes</option>

              </select>

            <div className="form-field">

                <option>Yes</option>

          {form.lockDuringBreak && (

                </ul>

          )}
        </div>

        {/* Break Preview */}
        <div className="timing-section break-preview">
          <div className="timing-section-header">

            <h4>Today's Break Schedule</h4>
          </div>
          <div className="break-schedule">
            {form.enableLunch && (

        </div>

        <div className="modal-footer">
          <button type="submit" className="primary-btn">
            Save Work Timings
          </button>

      </form>

      <style>{`

// src/services/agreementAdminService.js
// Super Admin-only Agreement APIs.
//
// FIX: the backend has NO "/agreements/admin" routes. "/agreements/admin" was
// being matched by GET /agreements/{id} with id = "admin", which is why the
// page showed "Agreement not found" (404). AgreementService.list() already
// returns ALL agreements for ROLE_SUPER_ADMIN / ROLE_ADMIN, so the plain
// "/agreements" endpoints are used here.

const API_BASE =
  import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE ||
  'https://crmbackend-5d2o.onrender.com';

const getToken = () => localStorage.getItem('token');

const authHeaders = (extra = {}) => {
  const token = getToken();
  return {
    Accept: 'application/json',
    'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
    ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
    ...extra,
  };
};

const handle = async (response) => {
  if (response.status === 401) {
    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('user');
    window.location.href = '/login';
    throw new Error('Session expired. Please login again.');
  }

  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(
      error.message ||
      error.error ||
      `API error: ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`
    );
  }

  return response;
};

export const agreementAdminService = {
  // GET /api/agreements  (optional ?status=...)
  getAll: async (status) => {
    const query =
      status && status !== 'All'
        ? `?status=${encodeURIComponent(status)}`
        : '';
    const response = await fetch(
      `${API_BASE}/agreements${query}`,
      { headers: authHeaders() }
    );
    return (await handle(response)).json();
  },

  // GET /api/agreements/{id}
  get: async (id) => {
    const response = await fetch(
      `${API_BASE}/agreements/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`,
      { headers: authHeaders() }
    );
    return (await handle(response)).json();
  },

  approve: async (id, remark = '') => {
    const response = await fetch(
      `${API_BASE}/agreements/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/approve`,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: authHeaders({ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }),
        body: JSON.stringify({ remark }),
      }
    );
    return (await handle(response)).json();
  },

  reject: async (id, reason) => {
    const response = await fetch(
      `${API_BASE}/agreements/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/reject`,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: authHeaders({ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }),
        body: JSON.stringify({ reason }),
      }
    );
    return (await handle(response)).json();
  },

  // GET /api/agreements/{id}/pdf
  downloadPdfBlob: async (id) => {
    const response = await fetch(
      `${API_BASE}/agreements/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/pdf`,
      { headers: authHeaders() }
    );
    await handle(response);
    return response.blob();
  },
};

export default agreementAdminService;

// src/services/agreementCategoryService.js
// Super Admin: list / read / save / reset the editable agreement for each category.

const API_BASE =
  import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE ||
  'https://crmbackend-5d2o.onrender.com';

const authHeaders = (extra = {}) => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
  return {
    Accept: 'application/json',
    'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
    ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
    ...extra,
  };
};

const handle = async (response) => {
  if (response.status === 401) {
    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('user');
    window.location.href = '/login';
    throw new Error('Session expired. Please login again.');
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(error.message || error.error || `API error: ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`);
  }
  return response.json();
};

export const agreementCategoryService = {
  list: async () =>
    handle(await fetch(`${API_BASE}/agreement-categories`, { headers: authHeaders() })),

  get: async (key) =>
    handle(await fetch(`${API_BASE}/agreement-categories/${encodeURIComponent(key)}`, { headers: authHeaders() })),

  save: async (key, contentHtml) =>
    handle(
      await fetch(`${API_BASE}/agreement-categories/${encodeURIComponent(key)}`, {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: authHeaders({ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }),
        body: JSON.stringify({ contentHtml }),
      })
    ),

  reset: async (key) =>
    handle(
      await fetch(`${API_BASE}/agreement-categories/${encodeURIComponent(key)}/reset`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: authHeaders(),
      })
    ),
};

export default agreementCategoryService;

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\services\agreementService.js =====
// src/services/agreementService.js

const API_BASE =
  import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE ||
  'https://crmbackend-5d2o.onrender.com';

const REQUEST_TIMEOUT = 30000;

/**
 * Build authentication headers.
 */
const auth = (json = true) => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('token');

  return {
    ...(token
      ? {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        }
      : {}),
    ...(json
      ? {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        }
      : {}),
    Accept: 'application/json',
    'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
  };
};

/**
 * Common API request helper.
 */
async function request(path, options = {}) {
  const controller = new AbortController();

  const timer = setTimeout(() => {
    controller.abort();
  }, REQUEST_TIMEOUT);

  const isForm = options.body instanceof FormData;

  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
      ...options,
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: {
        ...auth(!isForm),
        ...(options.headers || {}),
      },
    });

    const text = await response.text().catch(() => '');

    let data = null;

    try {
      data = text ? JSON.parse(text) : null;
    } catch {
      data = text
        ? {
            message: text,
          }
        : null;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // SESSION EXPIRED
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    if (response.status === 401) {
      localStorage.removeItem('token');
      localStorage.removeItem('user');

      window.location.href = '/login';

      throw new Error('Session expired. Please login again.');
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // FORBIDDEN
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    if (response.status === 403) {
      throw new Error(
        data?.message ||
          data?.error ||
          'You do not have permission to perform this action.'
      );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // OTHER API ERRORS
    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(
        data?.message ||
          data?.error ||
          `API error: ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`
      );
    }

    return data;
  } catch (error) {
    if (error?.name === 'AbortError') {
      throw new Error('Request timed out. Please try again.');
    }

    throw error;
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

/**
 * Build query string.
 */
const query = (params = {}) => {
  const value = new URLSearchParams(
    Object.entries(params)
      .filter(
        ([, value]) =>
          value !== undefined &&
          value !== null &&
          value !== ''
      )
      .map(([key, value]) => [
        key,
        String(value),
      ])
  ).toString();

  return value ? `?${value}` : '';
};

/**
 * Convert Spring Page response to content array.
 *
 * Spring Boot:
 * {
 *   content: [],
 *   totalElements: 10,
 *   totalPages: 1,
 *   ...
 * }
 *
 * Also supports APIs that directly return an array.
 */
const pageContent = (value) =>
  Array.isArray(value)
    ? value
    : value?.content || [];


/**
 * ============================================================
 * AGREEMENT SERVICE
 * ============================================================
 */
export const agreementService = {
  // ============================================================
  // AGREEMENTS
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Get agreements.
   *
   * Example:
   * agreementService.list()
   * agreementService.list({ status: 'DRAFT' })
   */
  list: (params = {}) =>
    request(
      `/agreements${query(params)}`
    ),

  /**
   * Get a single agreement.
   */
  get: (id) =>
    request(
      `/agreements/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`
    ),

  /**
   * Create agreement.
   */
  create: (data) =>
    request(
      '/agreements',
      {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify(data),
      }
    ),

  /**
   * Update agreement.
   */
  update: (id, data) =>
    request(
      `/agreements/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`,
      {
        method: 'PUT',
        body: JSON.stringify(data),
      }
    ),

  /**
   * Preview agreement.
   */
  previewAgreement: (data) =>
    request(
      '/agreements/preview',
      {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify(data),
      }
    ),

  /**
   * Submit agreement.
   */
  submit: (id) =>
    request(
      `/agreements/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/submit`,
      {
        method: 'POST',
      }
    ),

  /**
   * Approve agreement.
   */
  approve: (id, remark = '') =>
    request(
      `/agreements/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/approve`,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({
          remark,
        }),
      }
    ),

  /**
   * Reject agreement.
   */
  reject: (id, reason = '') =>
    request(
      `/agreements/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/reject`,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({
          reason,
        }),
      }
    ),

  /**
   * Send agreement.
   */
  send: (id, channel = 'WhatsApp') =>
    request(
      `/agreements/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/send`,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({
          channel,
        }),
      }
    ),

  /**
   * Delete agreement.
   */
  remove: (id) =>
    request(
      `/agreements/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`,
      {
        method: 'DELETE',
      }
    ),


  // ============================================================
  // AGREEMENT TEMPLATES
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Get all agreement templates.
   */
  templates: () =>
    request('/agreement-templates'),

  /**
   * Get templates by type.
   */
  templatesByType: (type) =>
    request(
      `/agreement-templates${query({ type })}`
    ),


  // ============================================================
  // AGREEMENT PLANS
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Get agreement plans.
   *
   * Example:
   * agreementService.plans('ERP')
   */
  plans: (type) =>
    request(
      `/agreement-plans${query({ type })}`
    ),


  // ============================================================
  // TELECALLER LEADS
  // ============================================================
  //
  // IMPORTANT:
  //
  // DO NOT use:
  //
  //     /leads
  //
  // because that becomes:
  //
  //     /api/leads
  //
  // For the Telecaller Agreement page we must use:
  //
  //     /telecaller/leads
  //
  // which becomes:
  //
  //     /api/telecaller/leads
  //
  // The backend Telecaller controller is mapped to:
  //
  //     /api/telecaller
  //
  // and its leads endpoint is:
  //
  //     GET /api/telecaller/leads
  //
  // ============================================================

  leads: () =>
    request(
      '/telecaller/leads?size=1000&sort=createdAt,desc'
    ).then(pageContent),


  // ============================================================
  // AGREEMENT PDF
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Get agreement PDF as Blob URL.
   *
   * Usage:
   *
   * const url =
   *   await agreementService.pdfBlobUrl(id);
   *
   * window.open(url);
   */
  pdfBlobUrl: async (id) => {
    const response = await fetch(
      `${API_BASE}/agreements/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/pdf`,
      {
        headers: auth(false),
      }
    );

    // ----------------------------------------------------------
    // SESSION EXPIRED
    // ----------------------------------------------------------
    if (response.status === 401) {
      localStorage.removeItem('token');
      localStorage.removeItem('user');

      window.location.href = '/login';

      throw new Error(
        'Session expired. Please login again.'
      );
    }

    // ----------------------------------------------------------
    // FORBIDDEN
    // ----------------------------------------------------------
    if (response.status === 403) {
      const text = await response
        .text()
        .catch(() => '');

      throw new Error(
        text ||
          'You do not have permission to view this PDF.'
      );
    }

    // ----------------------------------------------------------
    // OTHER ERRORS
    // ----------------------------------------------------------
    if (!response.ok) {
      const text = await response
        .text()
        .catch(() => '');

      throw new Error(
        text ||
          `Unable to load PDF: ${response.status}`
      );
    }

    // ----------------------------------------------------------
    // CREATE BLOB URL
    // ----------------------------------------------------------
    const blob = await response.blob();

    return URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  },
};


/**
 * Default export.
 */
export default agreementService;

const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE || 'https://crmbackend-5d2o.onrender.com';
const request = async (endpoint, options = {}) => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
  const headers = {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
    ...options.headers,
  };
  if (token) headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;
  // ✅ credentials: 'include' removed
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${endpoint}`, {
    headers,
    ...options,
  });
  if (response.status === 401) {
    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('user');
    window.location.href = '/login';
    throw new Error('Session expired. Please login again.');
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    const errorData = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(errorData.message || `API error: ${response.status}`);
  }
  return response.json();
};

const API_BASE =
  import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE ||
  'https://crmbackend-5d2o.onrender.com';

const BASE =
  `${API_BASE}/communications`;

const getToken = () =>
  localStorage.getItem('token');

const request = async (
  endpoint,
  options = {}
) => {

  const token = getToken();

  const headers = {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
    ...(options.headers || {}),
  };

  if (token) {
    headers.Authorization =
      `Bearer ${token}`;
  }

  const response =
    await fetch(
      `${BASE}${endpoint}`,
      {
        ...options,
        headers,
      }
    );

  if (response.status === 401) {

    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('user');

    window.location.href =
      '/login';

    throw new Error(
      'Session expired. Please login again.'
    );
  }

  if (response.status === 403) {

    throw new Error(
      'You do not have permission to use Communications.'
    );
  }

  if (!response.ok) {

    const error =
      await response
        .json()
        .catch(() => ({}));

    throw new Error(
      error.message ||
      error.error ||
      `API error: ${response.status}`
    );
  }

  return response.json();
};

const communicationService = {

  getParticipants: () =>
    request('/participants'),

  getMessages: (
    chatId = 'GROUP-ALL'
  ) =>
    request(
      `/messages?chatId=${encodeURIComponent(
        chatId
      )}`
    ),

  sendMessage: (
    data
  ) =>
    request(
      '/messages',
      {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify(data),
      }
    ),

  sendAnnouncement: (
    text
  ) =>
    request(
      '/announcements',
      {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({
          text,
        }),
      }
    ),

  markRead: (
    id
  ) =>
    request(
      `/messages/${encodeURIComponent(
        id
      )}/read`,
      {
        method: 'PATCH',
      }
    ),

  uploadAttachment: async (
    file
  ) => {

    const token =
      getToken();

    const formData =
      new FormData();

    formData.append(
      'file',
      file
    );

    const response =
      await fetch(
        `${BASE}/attachments`,
        {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {
            'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
            ...(token
              ? {
                  Authorization:
                    `Bearer ${token}`,
                }
              : {}),
          },
          body: formData,
        }
      );

    if (!response.ok) {

      const error =
        await response
          .json()
          .catch(() => ({}));

      throw new Error(
        error.message ||
        'Attachment upload failed'
      );
    }

    return response.json();
  },

  getMonitorConversations: () =>
    request('/monitor/conversations'),

  getMonitorMessages: (firstUsername, secondUsername) =>
    request(
      `/monitor/messages?firstUsername=${encodeURIComponent(firstUsername)}&secondUsername=${encodeURIComponent(secondUsername)}`
    ),

  attachmentUrl: (
    attachmentId
  ) =>
    `${BASE}/attachments/${encodeURIComponent(
      attachmentId
    )}`,

  // Fetch protected attachment/audio with JWT and return a Blob URL.
  getAttachmentBlobUrl: async (attachmentId, expectedSizeBytes = 0) => {
    if (!attachmentId) {
      throw new Error('Attachment ID is missing');
    }

    const token = getToken();
    let lastError = null;

    // A voice/file download must be complete before it is handed to the
    // audio element. Retry once if the browser receives a short response.
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt += 1) {
      try {
        const response = await fetch(
          `${BASE}/attachments/${encodeURIComponent(attachmentId)}`,
          {
            method: 'GET',
            cache: 'no-store',
            headers: {
              ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
              'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
              Accept: '*/*',
            },
          }
        );

        if (response.status === 401) {
          localStorage.removeItem('token');
          localStorage.removeItem('user');
          window.location.href = '/login';
          throw new Error('Session expired. Please login again.');
        }

        if (!response.ok) {
          const text = await response.text();
          throw new Error(
            text || `Attachment request failed: ${response.status}`
          );
        }

        const rawBlob = await response.blob();
        const serverType = response.headers.get('Content-Type')?.split(';')[0]?.trim();
        const blob = serverType && rawBlob.type !== serverType
          ? new Blob([rawBlob], { type: serverType })
          : rawBlob;
        const actualSize = blob.size;
        const expected = Number(expectedSizeBytes) || 0;

        if (expected > 0 && actualSize !== expected) {
          throw new Error(
            `Incomplete attachment download (${actualSize} of ${expected} bytes).`
          );
        }

        if (!actualSize) {
          throw new Error('Attachment download returned an empty file.');
        }

        return URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      } catch (err) {
        lastError = err;
        if (attempt === 0) {
          await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 250));
        }
      }
    }

    throw lastError || new Error('Unable to load attachment.');
  },

  openAttachment: async (attachmentId) => {
    const url = await communicationService.getAttachmentBlobUrl(attachmentId);
    window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 60000);
  },
};

export default communicationService;

const API_BASE =
  import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE ||
  "https://crmbackend-5d2o.onrender.com";

async function request(path) {
  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
    headers: {
      Accept: "application/json",
      ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
      "ngrok-skip-browser-warning": "true",
    },
  });

  if (response.status === 401) {
    localStorage.removeItem("token");
    localStorage.removeItem("user");
    throw new Error("Your session has expired. Please log in again.");
  }

  const body = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(body.message || body.error || `API error: ${response.status}`);
  }

  return body;
}

  getDashboard: () => request("/dashboard"),
  // Admin / Super Admin page: summary + monthly sales + daily calls + district / category performance
  getAdminDashboard: () => request("/dashboard/admin"),
};

const API_BASE =
  import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE ||
  'https://crmbackend-5d2o.onrender.com';

const headers = () => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('token');

  return {
    'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
    ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
  };
};

const handle = async (response) => {
  if (!response.ok) {
    const body = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(body.message || 'Request failed');
  }

  return response;
};

const upload = async (path, file) => {
  const formData = new FormData();
  formData.append('file', file);

  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: headers(),
    body: formData,
  });

  return (await handle(response)).json();
};

const get = async (path) => {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
    headers: headers(),
  });

  return (await handle(response)).json();
};

// E:\crm-app(telecallers)\crm-app(telecallers)\crm-app\src\services\leadService.js
const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE || 'https://crmbackend-5d2o.onrender.com';
const getToken = () => localStorage.getItem('token');
const apiCall = async (endpoint, options = {}) => {
  const token = getToken();
  // /auth/login and /auth/register must never be treated as "session expired":
  // a wrong password also answers 401, and the old code then wiped storage and
  // reloaded /login without ever showing the real message ("login aagala").
  const isAuthEndpoint = endpoint.startsWith('/auth/login') || endpoint.startsWith('/auth/register');
  const headers = {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
    ...options.headers,
  };
  if (token && !isAuthEndpoint) headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${endpoint}`, {
    ...options,
    headers,
  });
  // 401 = not logged in / token expired -> back to login.
  // 403 = logged in but not allowed -> show the error, do NOT log the user out.
  if (response.status === 401 && !isAuthEndpoint) {
    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('user');
    window.location.href = '/login';
    throw new Error('Session expired. Please login again.');
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(
      error.message || error.error ||
      (response.status === 403 ? 'You do not have permission to do this.' : `API error: ${response.status}`)
    );
  }
  return response.json();
};
export const leadService = {
  getLeads: (page = 0, size = 10, search = '', category = '', status = '', assignedTo = '') => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams({
      page,
      size,
    });
    if (search) params.append('search', search);
    if (category && category !== 'All Categories') params.append('category', category);
    if (status && status !== 'All Status') params.append('status', status);
    if (assignedTo && assignedTo !== 'All Agents' && assignedTo !== '') params.append('assignedTo', assignedTo);
    return apiCall(`/leads?${params.toString()}`);
  },
  getStats: (assignedTo = '') => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    if (assignedTo) params.append('assignedTo', assignedTo);
    const qs = params.toString();
    return apiCall(`/leads/stats${qs ? `?${qs}` : ''}`);
  },
  getCampaignStats: () => apiCall('/leads/campaigns/stats'),
  getCategoryStats: () => apiCall('/leads/categories/stats'),
  getLeadsByDistrict: (district) => apiCall(`/leads/district/${encodeURIComponent(district)}`),
  getLead: (id) => apiCall(`/leads/${id}`),
  createLead: (leadData) => apiCall('/leads', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: JSON.stringify(leadData),
  }),
  updateLead: (id, leadData) => apiCall(`/leads/${id}`, {
    method: 'PUT',
    body: JSON.stringify(leadData),
  }),
  deleteLead: (id) => apiCall(`/leads/${id}`, {
    method: 'DELETE',
  }),
  deleteMultipleLeads: (ids) => apiCall('/leads/batch', {
    method: 'DELETE',
    body: JSON.stringify(ids),
  }),
  // Super admin: every follow-up (lead with a next follow-up date) of one telecaller.
  getTelecallerFollowUps: (username) =>
    apiCall(`/leads/followups/telecaller/${encodeURIComponent(username)}`),
};
export const authService = {
  login: (username, password) => apiCall('/auth/login', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: JSON.stringify({ username, password }),
  }),
  register: (userData) => apiCall('/auth/register', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: JSON.stringify(userData),
  }),
  logout: () => {
    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('user');
    window.location.href = '/login';
  },
  validateToken: () => {
    const token = getToken();
    if (!token) return Promise.reject('No token');
    return apiCall('/auth/validate', {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    });
  },
};
export default leadService;

const API_BASE =
  import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE ||
  'https://crmbackend-5d2o.onrender.com';
const request = async (endpoint, options = {}) => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
  const headers = {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
    ...(options.headers || {}),
  };
  if (token) {
    headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
  }
  console.log('Leave Permission API Request:', {
    url: `${API_BASE}${endpoint}`,
    method: options.method || 'GET',
    hasToken: !!token,
    tokenPreview: token
      ? `${token.substring(0, 20)}...`
      : 'NO TOKEN',
  });
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${endpoint}`, {
    ...options,
    headers,
  });
  console.log('Leave Permission API Response:', {
    status: response.status,
    statusText: response.statusText,
    url: `${API_BASE}${endpoint}`,
  });
  if (response.status === 401) {
    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('user');
    window.location.href = '/login';
    throw new Error(
      'Session expired. Please login again.'
    );
  }
  if (response.status === 403) {
    throw new Error(
      'Access denied. Your account does not have permission to perform this action.'
    );
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    const data = await response
      .json()
      .catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(
      data.message ||
      data.error ||
      `API error: ${response.status}`
    );
  }
  if (response.status === 204) {
    return null;
  }
  return response.json();
};

    ),

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\services\notificationService.js =====
const API_BASE_URL =
  import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE ||
  import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL ||
  'http://localhost:8080/api';

const getToken = () =>
  localStorage.getItem('token') ||
  localStorage.getItem('jwtToken') ||
  localStorage.getItem('accessToken');

const request = async (url, options = {}) => {
  const token = getToken();
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${url}`, {
    ...options,
    headers: {
      Accept: 'application/json',
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
      ...(options.headers || {})
    }
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    const text = await response.text().catch(() => '');
    throw new Error(text || `HTTP ${response.status}`);
  }

  return response.status === 204 ? null : response.json();
};

export default {
  getNotifications: () => request('/notifications'),
  getUnreadCount: () => request('/notifications/unread-count'),
  markAsRead: (id) => request(`/notifications/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/read`, { method: 'PUT' }),
  markAllAsRead: () => request('/notifications/read-all', { method: 'PUT' })
};

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\services\paymentAdminService.js =====
// src/services/paymentAdminService.js
const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE || 'https://crmbackend-5d2o.onrender.com';
const getToken = () => localStorage.getItem('token');
const authHeaders = (extra = {}) => {
  const token = getToken();
  return {
    'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
    ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
    ...extra,
  };
};
const handle = async (response) => {
  if (response.status === 401) {
    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('user');
    window.location.href = '/login';
    throw new Error('Session expired. Please login again.');
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    const err = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(err.message || err.error || `API error: ${response.status}`);
  }
  return response;
};
const request = async (path, method = 'GET', body) => {
  const headers = authHeaders(body === undefined ? {} : { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' });
  const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
    method,
    headers,
    body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  return (await handle(res)).json();
};

export const paymentAdminService = {
  getAll: () => request('/payments/admin'),
  get: (id) => request(`/payments/admin/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`),
  update: (id, payload) => request(`/payments/admin/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, 'PUT', payload),
  addPaymentRecord: (id, payload) => request(`/payments/admin/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/payment-records`, 'POST', payload),
  updatePaymentRecord: (id, recordId, payload) => request(`/payments/admin/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/payment-records/${encodeURIComponent(recordId)}`, 'PUT', payload),
  uploadQr: async (file) => { const fd = new FormData(); fd.append('file', file); const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/payments/admin/qr`, { method: 'POST', headers: authHeaders(), body: fd }); return (await handle(res)).json(); },
  getInvoice: (id) => request(`/payments/admin/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/invoice`),
  updateInvoice: (id, payload) => request(`/payments/admin/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/invoice`, 'PUT', payload),
  getSharedQrUrl: () => `${API_BASE}/payments/qr`,
  getSharedQr: () => request('/payments/qr/data'),
  verify: (id) => request(`/payments/admin/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/verify`, 'POST', {}),
  generateInvoice: (id, recordId) => request(`/payments/admin/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/generate-invoice${recordId ? `?recordId=${encodeURIComponent(recordId)}` : ''}`, 'POST', {}),
  sendToTelecaller: (id) => request(`/payments/admin/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/send-to-telecaller`, 'POST', {}),

  downloadInvoiceRecordBlob: async (id, recordId) => { const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/payments/admin/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/payment-records/${encodeURIComponent(recordId)}/invoice/pdf`, { headers: authHeaders() }); await handle(res); return res.blob(); },

  downloadInvoiceBlob: async (id) => {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/payments/admin/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/invoice/pdf`, { headers: authHeaders() });
    await handle(res);
    return res.blob();
  },
  downloadInvoiceExcelBlob: async (id) => {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/payments/admin/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/invoice/excel`, { headers: authHeaders() });
    await handle(res);
    return res.blob();
  },
};
export default paymentAdminService;

const API_BASE=import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE||'/api';
const auth=()=>{const t=localStorage.getItem('token');return t?{Authorization:`Bearer ${t}`}:{}};
const err=async r=>{try{const d=await r.json();return d?.message||d?.error||`Request failed (${r.status})`}catch{return `Request failed (${r.status})`}};
const request=async(path,options={})=>{const r=await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`,{...options,headers:{...auth(),...(options.headers||{})}});if(!r.ok)throw new Error(await err(r));return r.status===204?null:r.json()};
const upload=(path,file)=>{if(!file)return Promise.reject(new Error('Please select an image first.'));const f=new FormData();f.append('file',file);return request(path,{method:'POST',body:f})};
export const paymentService={uploadQr:file=>upload('/payments/admin/qr',file),uploadInvoiceLogo:file=>upload('/payments/admin/invoice-assets/logo',file),uploadInvoiceSignature:file=>upload('/payments/admin/invoice-assets/signature',file),uploadThankYouImage:file=>upload('/payments/admin/invoice-assets/thank-you',file),getInvoiceBrandAssets:()=>request('/payments/invoice-assets/data'),getQrData:()=>request('/payments/qr/data')};

// src/services/quotationAdminService.js
// Super Admin visibility + approve/reject actions over every telecaller's
// quotations. Mirrors the request/error-handling conventions already used
// in leadService.js / superAdminService.js.
const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE || 'https://crmbackend-5d2o.onrender.com';
const getToken = () => localStorage.getItem('token');
const authHeaders = (extra = {}) => {
  const token = getToken();
  return {
    'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
    ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
    ...extra,
  };
};
const handle = async (response) => {
  if (response.status === 401) {
    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('user');
    window.location.href = '/login';
    throw new Error('Session expired. Please login again.');
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    const err = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(err.message || err.error || `API error: ${response.status}`);
  }
  return response;
};

export const quotationAdminService = {
  // status: omit or 'All' for everything, otherwise one of
  // Draft | Pending Approval | Approved | Rejected | Sent
  getAll: async (status) => {
    const query = status && status !== 'All' ? `?status=${encodeURIComponent(status)}` : '';
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/quotations/admin${query}`, { headers: authHeaders() });
    return (await handle(res)).json();
  },
  getSummary: async () => {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/quotations/admin/summary`, { headers: authHeaders() });
    return (await handle(res)).json();
  },
  get: async (id) => {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/quotations/admin/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, { headers: authHeaders() });
    return (await handle(res)).json();
  },
  // status must be 'Approved' or 'Rejected'; rejectionReason is required when rejecting.
  review: async (id, status, rejectionReason) => {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/quotations/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/review`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: authHeaders({ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }),
      body: JSON.stringify({ status, rejectionReason }),
    });
    return (await handle(res)).json();
  },
  // ── Letterhead / logo / footer used on every generated quotation PDF ──
  getBranding: async () => {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/quotation-branding`, { headers: authHeaders() });
    return (await handle(res)).json();
  },
  uploadBranding: async (type, file) => {            // type: 'logo' | 'letterpad'
    const fd = new FormData();
    fd.append('file', file);
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/quotation-branding/${type}`, { method: 'POST', headers: authHeaders(), body: fd });
    return (await handle(res)).json();
  },
  deleteBranding: async (type) => {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/quotation-branding/${type}`, { method: 'DELETE', headers: authHeaders() });
    return (await handle(res)).json();
  },
  saveBrandingSettings: async (settings) => {         // { showBrandText, phone, website, address }
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/quotation-branding/settings`, {
      method: 'PUT', headers: authHeaders({ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }), body: JSON.stringify(settings),
    });
    return (await handle(res)).json();
  },
  downloadPdfBlob: async (id) => {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/quotations/admin/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/pdf`, { headers: authHeaders() });
    await handle(res);
    return res.blob();
  },
};
export default quotationAdminService;

const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE || 'https://crmbackend-5d2o.onrender.com';
const getToken = () => localStorage.getItem('token');
const authHeaders = (extra = {}) => {
  const token = getToken();
  return {
    'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true', // FIX: was missing — needed so ngrok returns JSON, not its HTML warning page
    ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
    ...extra,
  };
};
const handle = async (response) => {
  if (response.status === 401) {
    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('user');
    window.location.href = '/login';
    throw new Error('Session expired. Please login again.');
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    const err = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(err.message || err.error || `API error: ${response.status}`);
  }
  return response;
};
const request = async (path, method = 'GET', body) => {
  const headers = authHeaders(body === undefined ? {} : { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' });
  const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
    method,
    headers,
    body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  return (await handle(res)).json();
};

export const quotationService = {

  create: (payload) => request('/quotations', 'POST', payload),

  submit: (id) => request(`/quotations/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/submit`, 'POST', {}),

  requestRevision: (id, reason) => request(`/quotations/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/revision`, 'POST', { reason }),
  markSent: (id, channel) => request(`/quotations/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/mark-sent`, 'POST', { channel }),
  remove: (id) => request(`/quotations/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, 'DELETE'),
  downloadPdfBlob: async (id) => {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/quotations/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/pdf`, { headers: authHeaders() });
    await handle(res);
    return res.blob();
  },
};

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\services\reportService.js =====
const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || "/api";

const getToken = () =>
  localStorage.getItem("token") ||
  localStorage.getItem("accessToken");

const request = async (endpoint) => {
  const token = getToken();

  const response = await fetch(
    `${API_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`,
    {
      method: "GET",
      headers: {
        Accept: "application/json",
        ...(token
          ? {
              Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
            }
          : {}),
      },
    }
  );

  const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(
      data.message ||
        data.error ||
        `Request failed with status ${response.status}`
    );
  }

  return data;
};

export const reportService = {
  getMyReport: (period = "MONTH") =>
    request(
      `/reports/mine?period=${encodeURIComponent(period)}`
    ),

  getOverallReport: (period = "MONTH") =>
    request(
      `/reports/overall?period=${encodeURIComponent(period)}`
    ),

  getTelecallerReports: (period = "MONTH") =>
    request(
      `/reports/telecallers?period=${encodeURIComponent(period)}`
    ),

  getTelecallerReport: (username, period = "MONTH") =>
    request(
      `/reports/telecallers/${encodeURIComponent(
        username
      )}?period=${encodeURIComponent(period)}`
    ),
};

===== D:\CRM Main\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app (superadmin)\crm-app\src\services\superAdminService.js =====
// src/services/superAdminService.js
const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE || 'https://crmbackend-5d2o.onrender.com';
const SUPER_ADMIN_API = `${API_BASE}/super-admin`;
const getToken = () => localStorage.getItem('token');
const request = async (endpoint, options = {}) => {
  const token = getToken();
  const headers = {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
    ...options.headers,
  };
  if (token) headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;
  // ✅ credentials: 'include' removed
  const response = await fetch(`${SUPER_ADMIN_API}${endpoint}`, {
    ...options,
    headers,
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(error.message || error.error || `API error: ${response.status}`);
  }
  return response.json();
};
export const superAdminService = {
  getDistricts: () => request('/districts'),
  getEmployees: () => request('/employees'),
  getDistrict: (name) => request(`/districts/${encodeURIComponent(name)}`),
  getTelecallerDistrictAssignments: () => request('/telecaller-district-assignments'),
  setTelecallerDistricts: (username, districts) => request(`/telecallers/${encodeURIComponent(username)}/districts`, { method: 'PUT', body: JSON.stringify({ districts }) }),
  assignDistrict: (districtName, employeeUsername) =>
    request('/districts/assign', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ districtName, employeeUsername }),
    }),
  unassignDistrict: (name) =>
    request(`/districts/unassign/${encodeURIComponent(name)}`, { method: 'POST' }),
  createDistrict: (name) =>
    request('/districts', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ name }),
    }),
  // Employees
  getEmployees: () => request('/employees'),
  getEmployee: (username) => request(`/employees/${encodeURIComponent(username)}`),
  createEmployee: (data) =>
    request('/employees', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify(data),
    }),
  updateEmployee: (id, data) =>
    request(`/employees/${id}`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      body: JSON.stringify(data),
    }),
  deleteEmployee: (id) => request(`/employees/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),
  // Import Leads (multipart)
  importLeads: async (file, uploadedBy, district) => {
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('file', file);
    formData.append('uploadedBy', uploadedBy);
    if (district) formData.append('district', district);
    const token = getToken();
    const response = await fetch(`${SUPER_ADMIN_API}/import-leads`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        Authorization: token ? `Bearer ${token}` : '',
        'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
      },
      body: formData,
    });
    if (!response.ok) {
      const error = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
      throw new Error(error.error || error.message || 'Import failed');
    }
    return response.json();
  },
  // Upload History
  getUploadHistory: () => request('/upload-history'),
  getUploadHistoryById: (id) => request(`/upload-history/${id}`),
  // Stats
  getDistrictStats: () => request('/district-stats'),
  getDashboardStats: () => request('/dashboard-stats'),
};

// src/services/telecallerService.js
const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE || 'https://crmbackend-5d2o.onrender.com';
const getToken = () => localStorage.getItem('token');
const request = async (endpoint, options = {}) => {
  const token = getToken();
  const headers = {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': 'true',
    ...options.headers,
  };
  if (token) headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${endpoint}`, {
    ...options,
    headers,
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(error.message || error.error || `API error: ${response.status}`);
  }
  return response.json();
};
export const telecallerService = {
  getAll: () => request('/telecallers'),
  getById: (id) => request(`/telecallers/${id}`),
  getByEmployeeId: (empId) => request(`/telecallers/employee/${empId}`),
  
  create: (data) => {
    const { id, employeeId, ...cleanData } = data;
    return request('/telecallers', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify(cleanData),
    });
  },
  
  update: (id, data) => {
    const { id: _, employeeId, ...cleanData } = data;
    return request(`/telecallers/${id}`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      body: JSON.stringify(cleanData),
    });
  },
  
  delete: (id) => request(`/telecallers/${id}`, {
    method: 'DELETE',
  }),
  
  resetPassword: (id, newPassword) => request(`/telecallers/${id}/reset-password`, {
    method: 'POST',
    body: JSON.stringify({ newPassword }),
  }),
  
  toggleStatus: (id) => request(`/telecallers/${id}/toggle-status`, {
    method: 'PATCH',
  }),
};
